Using composite Lyapunov analysis, we prove forward invariance of the safe set and the uniform ultimately boundedness of the impedance-tracking error. Simulations on a 7-DOF manipulator with severe parametric uncertainty and external interaction wrenches demonstrate safe constraint satisfaction and robust impedance tracking.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Subcortical Shape Variations and Their Associations with Cognition Across the 8th Decade of Life. A Study in the Lothian Birth Cohort 1936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29703)
*Maria del C. Valdes-Hernandez,Wonjung Park,Joanna Moodie,Susana Muñoz Maniega,Janie Corley,Fraser N. Sneden,Mark E. Bastin,Joanna M. Wardlaw,Simon R. Cox,Jinah Park*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究利用Lothian Birth Cohort 1936纵向数据，探索社区居住老年人在80岁左右十年间皮层下脑结构的形态变化及其与认知老化的关联。研究发现皮层下结构形状变化具有异质性，海马和腹侧间脑显示不对称变形，而丘脑和苍白球呈现较均匀的对称性体积收缩。一般认知能力的变化主要与时间点之间的顶点向内和向外位移相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正常个体脑形态变化可能捕捉到粗体积测量未能完全反映的功能相关脑老化特征。尽管皮层下脑结构在认知中起重要作用，但其形态轨迹与认知老化的关联尚未被充分记录。

Method: 使用Lothian Birth Cohort 1936的大样本纵向研究数据（神经影像、人口统计学和认知数据），采用ANCOVA和混合线性模型分析，探索社区居住个体在80岁左右十年间皮层下脑结构的形状变化及其与认知老化的关联。

Result: 皮层下形状变化具有异质性：海马和腹侧间脑经历左右半球不同的形态变形，而丘脑和苍白球等结构呈现更均匀、几乎对称的体积收缩。一般认知变化主要与时间点之间的顶点向内和向外位移相关。

Conclusion: 皮层下脑结构形态轨迹与认知老化存在关联，且不同脑区的形态变化模式各异，提示这些形态学指标可能作为认知老化的敏感生物标记物。

Abstract: The study of brain morphology changes in normal individuals may capture aspects of functionally-relevant brain aging not fully indicated by gross volumetry. Despite the important role of subcortical brain structures in cognition, the associations between their morphological trajectories and cognitive changes in aging have not been documented. We use neuroimaging, demographic, and cognitive data from a large longitudinal study of cognitive aging, the Lothian Birth Cohort 1936, to explore shape changes in subcortical brain structures of community-dwelling individuals across their 8th decade of life. We investigate the association of these changes with cognitive aging using ANCOVA and mixed linear model analyses. Subcortical shape changes were heterogeneous, with varied atrophy patterns across whole period. The hippocampus and the ventral DC experienced varied morphological deformations (from its baseline point) different in left and right hemispheres, while the thalami and globus pallidi shapes, for example, experienced a more uniform volume contraction, nearly symmetrical throughout different timelines. Changes in general cognition were mainly associated with inwards and outwards vertex displacements between the time-points.

</details>


### [222] [Inward rectifier potassium channels interact with calcium channels to promote robust and physiological bistability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14107)
*Anaëlle De Worm,Guillaume Drion,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过最小化电导模型揭示了CaL+Kir通道组合通过Kir电流的负微分电导特性扩大双稳态窗口，从而产生稳健的生理性双稳态，并提出了中枢敏化的内在机制候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究显示CaL通道与电压门控钾通道配对可产生双稳态，但大多数钾通道会缩小双稳态窗口，因此稳健且生理性的双稳态如何产生尚不清楚。

Method: 采用最小化电导模型、分岔分析以及双房室模型，比较CaL+Kir与CaL+KM两种通道组合对双稳态窗口大小、噪声鲁棒性和兴奋性类型的影响。

Result: CaL+Kir组合产生显著更大的双稳态窗口，对噪声和内在变异性更鲁棒；该效应源于Kir稳态电流的负微分电导特征；两组合分别支持平台电位双稳态（CaL+Kir）和共振样动力学（CaL+KM）。

Conclusion: CaL+Kir通道组合是中枢敏化的候选内在机制。

Abstract: Projection neurons in the dorsal horn relay nociceptive input to supraspinal centers. During central sensitization, a subset of them switches from tonic firing to plateau potentials with sustained afterdischarges, a change that requires intrinsic bistability between a resting and a spiking state. Voltage-gated L-type calcium (CaL) channels can produce bistability, but reach physiological resting states only when paired with voltage-gated potassium channels, most of which simultaneously shrink the bistability window. How robust, physiological bistability arises has therefore remained unclear. Using a minimal conductance-based model, we show that inward rectifier potassium (Kir) channels enlarge the bistability window when combined with CaL channels, while M-type potassium (KM) channels slightly reduce it. Within the parameter region where bistability is both robust and physiological, both channel types can sustain bistability, but the CaL+Kir combination produces a substantially larger window and is more robust to noise and intrinsic variability. This window-enlarging effect traces to a shape feature of the outward Kir steady-state current: like the CaL current, it has a region of negative differential conductance around the spike threshold, a feature absent from KM and from most other voltage-gated potassium currents. Bifurcation analysis further shows that the two pairs support qualitatively distinct excitability: plateau-generating bistability for CaL+Kir and resonator-like dynamics for CaL+KM. These conclusions hold in a two-compartment model of deep projection neurons with realistic ion channel complements, and identify the CaL+Kir pair as a candidate intrinsic mechanism for central sensitization.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Does Distributed Training Undermine Compute Governance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29359)
*Robi Rahman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了分布式训练技术可能被用于规避计算治理监管的问题，并提出了相应的检测和预防措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算治理提案通常假设前沿AI训练需要大型、可检测的计算集群，但分布式训练算法的进步使得开发者可以在分散的硬件聚合上进行训练，从而可能规避注册和监控要求，因此需要研究这种规避行为的可行性并制定对策。

Method: 评估分布式训练规避计算治理的可行性，并提出包括举报机制、芯片追踪、法务会计以及集群内存和计算阈值在内的反制措施。

Result: 分布式训练技术确实可能被用于逃避计算治理监管，现有的治理框架存在被利用的风险。

Conclusion: 计算治理必须设计能够检测和预防非法分布式训练操作的监管机制，具体措施包括举报机制、芯片追踪、法务会计以及为集群设定内存和计算阈值。

Abstract: Compute governance proposals often rely on the assumption that frontier AI training requires large, detectable computing clusters. However, recent advances in distributed training algorithms could allow developers to conduct frontier-scale training on distributed agglomerations of hardware, rather than needing large datacenter facilities. Developers who prefer not to be constrained by regulations may structure their hardware in a manner that evades the registration and monitoring requirements associated with compute governance. Therefore, regulations must be designed to detect and prevent illicit distributed training operations. This paper evaluates the feasibility of such evasion and outlines recommended countermeasures, including whistleblowing, chip tracking, forensic accounting, and memory and compute thresholds for clusters.

</details>


### [224] [The New Pro Se: Generative AI and the Surge in Federal Civil Self-Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29493)
*Or Cohen-Sasson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 利用约280万份联邦民事诉讼数据，研究发现生成式AI普及后，自诉原告比例从11.33%升至16.94%。通过文体计量学检测，13.9%的自诉状具有AI一致性特征。AI辅助撰写的诉状引用密度更高、更多来自首次诉讼者、地理分布不均，且并未提高胜诉率，反而更早被驳回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式AI工具普及是否导致联邦民事诉讼中自诉案件数量增加，并分析AI对诉状文本、诉讼结果及自诉人构成的具体影响。

Method: 收集FY2008-2025年约280万份联邦民事案件数据，采用时间趋势分析和协变量调整的稳健性检验；针对民权和法定案件，基于文体计量学AI检测指标构建可解释的AI一致性文本度量。

Result: 自诉率从11.33%升至16.94%；非模板诉状中AI标记比例为13.9%；AI标记诉状引用密度更高、首次诉讼者占比更大、地理分布不均，女性原告比例略有上升；AI标记案件驳回率更高、结案阶段更早。

Conclusion: 生成式AI并未提升自诉人的诉讼效果，反而可能导致法院筛选负担加重；研究区分了法律形式性与法律实效性，对司法可及性提出新的问题。

Abstract: Since public access to generative AI tools became widespread, federal civil litigation has seen a marked increase in pro se (self-represented) plaintiffs. This paper analyzes that shift using ~2.8 million filings, asking whether the post-GenAI period is associated not only with more pro se filings, but also with detectable changes in complaint text, litigation outcomes, and the composition of pro se litigants.
  Using civil filing data from FY2008-2025, we find that the federal civil pro se plaintiff rate rose from 11.33% pre-GenAI to 16.94% post-GenAI, a 5.61 percentage-point increase that persists after trend and covariate-adjusted robustness checks. We then focus on Civil Rights and Other Statutory cases, where the increase is especially pronounced, and link case metadata to pro se complaints. Drawing on stylometric AI detection indicators, we develop an interpretable measure of AI-consistent drafting. Against a threshold calibrated to the pre-GenAI baseline, the net AI-flagged share is 13.9% of post-GenAI non-form complaints.
  Analysis of the AI-flagged complaints shows that they are more citation-dense, disproportionately associated with first-time rather than repeat filers, and geographically unevenly distributed. This composition pattern suggests that AI-consistent drafting is not merely a repeat-filer phenomenon; it also includes a modest, suggestive increase in name-inferred female plaintiffs. We find no evidence of improved win rates; in fact, AI-flagged complaints are more likely to be dismissed and to terminate at earlier procedural phases. These findings raise new questions about access to justice and court screening burdens, and sharpen the distinction between legal formality and legal efficacy.

</details>


### [225] [Generalizing a Highly Configurable Analytics Pipeline to Replicate and Support Educational Research Across Multiple Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30303)
*Yallen Bai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出A4L数据架构，一种模块化数据分析管道，用于收集、集成和分析教育AI助手产生的异构学习交互数据，并通过跨三个在线学习场景的案例研究验证了其可复用性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习环境中AI助手产生大量异构交互数据，缺乏统一的架构来系统化收集、整合和分析这些数据以生成教学洞察，难以实现个性化学习和双向反馈循环。

Method: 设计了A4L数据分析管道的模块化架构，描述了其设计原则和开发过程；通过在佐治亚理工学院部署的三个教育AI助手对应的三个研究领域进行案例研究，评估管道的跨领域一致性和可扩展性。

Result: 一组通用的统计分析方法可以一致地应用于不同结构和教学背景的数据集，跨领域复现关键分析发现；最初为一个领域开发的分析能力可以扩展到支持其他领域的更丰富分析。

Conclusion: A4L分析管道可作为可复用基础设施，用于分析未来教育AI助手产生的数据；其系统性可扩展的分析能力可为教育AI系统的设计和评估提供洞察基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence assistants deployed in online learning environments create new opportunities to collect large volumes of learner interaction data and generate insights to improve student outcomes. Architecture for AI-Augmented Learning (A4L) is a modular data architecture that enables the collection, integration, and analysis of learner interaction data from educational AI systems, supporting the generation of instructional insights that facilitate personalized learning and reinforce the bidirectional feedback loop between instructors and learners. This study examines the modular design of the A4L Data Analytics Pipeline, an extensible data infrastructure that enables the ingestion, processing, and analysis of heterogeneous datasets generated by educational AI assistants. We describe the design principles and development process used to extend the pipeline's analytical capabilities while preserving flexibility across domains. We evaluate the pipeline through case studies spanning three research domains corresponding to three educational AI assistants deployed in online learning environments at Georgia Tech. Results show that a common set of statistical analysis methods can be consistently applied across datasets with differing structures and instructional contexts, enabling the pipeline to reproduce key analytical findings across domains. We demonstrate how analytical capabilities initially developed for one domain can be extended to support richer analyses in another, illustrating the pipeline's extensibility. These findings suggest that the A4L Analytics Pipeline can serve as reusable infrastructure for analyzing data generated by future educational AI assistants. By enabling analytics that can be systematically extended to new domains, the pipeline provides a foundation for deriving insights that inform the design and evaluation of educational AI systems.

</details>


### [226] [Agent4Edu: Generating Learner Response Data by Generative Agents for Intelligent Education Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10332)
*Weibo Gao,Qi Liu,Linan Yue,Fangzhou Yao,Rui Lv,Zheng Zhang,Hao Wang,Zhenya Huang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent4Edu，一种基于大语言模型驱动的生成式智能体模拟器，通过构建具备学习者档案、记忆和动作模块的智能体，弥合个性化学习系统中离线指标与在线性能之间的差距，并支持与计算机化自适应测试等算法交互以实现个性化教育评估与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化学习系统中离线指标与在线性能之间存在显著差异，阻碍了系统在实际教育环境中的进展，亟需一种能够更真实模拟学习者行为的工具来桥接这一差距。

Method: 利用大语言模型构建生成式智能体，包含学习者档案模块（基于真实响应数据初始化，捕获练习风格与认知因素）、记忆模块（记录练习事实与高层摘要，融入心理学反思机制）和动作模块（支持习题理解、分析与响应生成）。智能体可与个性化学习算法（如计算机化自适应测试）交互。

Result: 通过全面评估，探索了Agent4Edu的优势与不足，重点关注智能体与人类学习者之间响应的一致性与差异性。

Conclusion: Agent4Edu能够有效模拟学习者行为，为个性化学习算法的离线评估与优化提供更可靠的仿真平台，但智能体与真实人类学习者之间仍存在一定差异需进一步改进。

Abstract: Personalized learning represents a promising educational strategy within intelligent educational systems, aiming to enhance learners' practice efficiency. However, the discrepancy between offline metrics and online performance significantly impedes their progress. To address this challenge, we introduce Agent4Edu, a novel personalized learning simulator leveraging recent advancements in human intelligence through large language models (LLMs). Agent4Edu features LLM-powered generative agents equipped with learner profile, memory, and action modules tailored to personalized learning algorithms. The learner profiles are initialized using real-world response data, capturing practice styles and cognitive factors. Inspired by human psychology theory, the memory module records practice facts and high-level summaries, integrating reflection mechanisms. The action module supports various behaviors, including exercise understanding, analysis, and response generation. Each agent can interact with personalized learning algorithms, such as computerized adaptive testing, enabling a multifaceted evaluation and enhancement of customized services. Through a comprehensive assessment, we explore the strengths and weaknesses of Agent4Edu, emphasizing the consistency and discrepancies in responses between agents and human learners. The code, data, and appendix are publicly available at https://github.com/bigdata-ustc/Agent4Edu.

</details>


### [227] [Multi-Level Barriers to Generative AI Adoption Across Disciplines and Professional Roles in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27052)
*Jianhua Yang,Kerem Öge,Adrian von Mühlenen,Abdullah Bilal Akbulut,Tanya Suzanne Carey,Chidi Okorro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过对英国罗素集团大学272名学术和专业服务人员的多方法调查分析，发现GenAI采纳障碍并非主要源于个体感知因素，而是结构性产生的：非STEM学者主要报告伦理与文化障碍，STEM和专业服务人员则强调制度、治理与基础设施约束，表明大学需制定角色特定的治理与支持框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常将GenAI采纳障碍归因于个体层面的感知有用性和易用性，但跨学科和不同机构角色的采纳障碍差异尚未得到充分探索，本研究旨在调查这些障碍是否由结构性因素产生。

Method: 采用多方法调查分析，对英国罗素集团大学272名学术和专业服务人员进行调查，综合运用多项逻辑回归（MLR）、结构方程模型（SEM）和开放式回答的语义聚类分析。

Result: 发现存在清晰、系统性的差异：非STEM学者主要报告与学术诚信相关的伦理和文化障碍，而STEM和专业服务人员则不成比例地强调制度、治理和基础设施约束。

Conclusion: GenAI采纳障碍深深嵌入组织生态系统和认识论规范中，大学必须超越通用培训，制定针对特定角色的治理和支持框架。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is rapidly reshaping higher education, yet barriers to its adoption across different disciplines and institutional roles remain underexplored. Existing literature frequently attributes adoption barriers to individual-level factors such as perceived usefulness and ease of use. This study instead investigates whether such barriers are structurally produced. Drawing on a multi-method survey analysis of 272 academic and professional services (PSs) staff at a Russell Group university, we examine how disciplinary contexts and institutional roles shape perceived barriers. By integrating multinomial logistic regression (MLR), structural equation modelling (SEM), and semantic clustering of open-ended responses, we move beyond descriptive accounts to provide a multi-level explanation of GenAI adoption. Our findings reveal clear, systematic differences: non-STEM academics primarily report ethical and cultural barriers related to academic integrity, whereas STEM and PSs staff disproportionately emphasize institutional, governance, and infrastructure constraints. We conclude that GenAI adoption barriers are deeply embedded in organizational ecosystems and epistemic norms, suggesting that universities must move beyond generalized training to develop role-specific governance and support frameworks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现知识编辑方法（ROME/MEMIT）在修改不同事实时虽然权重变化不同，但都依赖一个共同的权重子集来维持编辑效果。通过训练二元掩码，证明该共同子集可在测试集上逆转70%以上的编辑。分析表明，该机制通过消除后层过度注意来实现编辑，且该机制对编辑成功至关重要。编辑实际上抑制而非覆盖知识，这解释了为何相关事实传播失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑方法（如ROME和MEMIT）主要通过修改MLP权重来更新Transformer模型中的事实关联，但已有评估主要关注输出行为，对其内部机制的理解尚不充分。作者旨在探究这些编辑方法是否依赖于共同的机制，无论修改的是哪个事实。

Method: 训练一个紧凑的二元掩码（binary mask）来隔离编辑后权重中维持编辑效果的关键子集。通过掩码反转编辑效果，并注入掩码观察编辑成功率变化。

Result: 该掩码在训练集上逆转80%的编辑，在测试集上逆转超过70%。注入掩码使编辑成功率从98%降至38%。分析发现掩码通过消除后层的过度注意力来逆转编辑。

Conclusion: 不同事实编辑共享共同的功能结构，该机制对编辑成功至关重要。编辑实质上是抑制而非覆盖已有知识，这解释了ROME和MEMIT无法将更改传播到相关事实的原因。发现的共同功能子空间可用于检测和防御不当编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [229] [Neural-Behavioral Representation of Natural Whole-body Movement in Monkeys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29355)
*Jieshi He,Puzhe Li,Yanan Sui,Mu-ming Poo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大规模皮层电信号与多视角动作捕捉的神经-行为建模框架，在自由移动的猕猴中实现了全身运动解码，证明了利用颅内神经活动解码自然全身行为的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动解码研究受限于约束任务和有限肢体运动，缺乏对自然全身行为的大规模神经表征，亟需一种能在自由移动状态下解码全身运动的方法。

Method: 采用定制的数据采集平台，同步记录猕猴感觉-运动相关脑区的大规模硬膜外皮层信号与多视角运动捕捉数据；通过自回归编码器-解码器模型学习紧凑的行为先验，并以神经信号为条件解码全身运动。

Result: 模型在无需显式物理约束的条件下，成功解码出准确且逼真的全身运动，为灵长类自然全身运动解码提供了概念验证。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了利用大规模颅内神经活动解码灵长类自然全身行为的可行性，为后续神经假肢和运动康复研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Understanding how cortical activity represents natural whole-body behaviors in primates remains challenging. Limited by the diversity of movements and inaccessibility of large-scale neural representation of whole-body kinematics, previous motor decoding studies focused on constrained tasks and limited limb movements. Here, we present a neural-behavioral recording and modeling framework for freely moving monkeys, combining large-scale epidural cortical signals from distributed sensory- and motor-related areas with synchronized multi-view motion capture through a custom-made data collection platform. We reconstructed whole-body monkey kinematics and learned a compact behavior prior using an autoregressive encoder-decoder model. Conditioned on neural signals, the model decoded accurate and realistic whole-body movement without explicit physical constraints. Our results provide a novel proof-of-concept approach for decoding natural whole-body movements in primates using large-scale intracranial neural activity.

</details>


### [230] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出差异电路脆弱性指标，从机制层面比较RL和SFT对LLM内部计算电路的保留程度，发现SFT适应快但破坏电路多、遗忘严重，而RL保留更多电路但适应慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明RL比SFT更能保留模型先验能力，但仅停留在行为层面。本文希望在机制层面探究RL优势是否体现在内部计算电路的更好保留上。

Method: 提出差异电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability）这一头级别的度量指标，用于衡量微调过程中电路退化程度。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上，针对科学问答任务，比较RL和SFT对内部电路的影响。

Result: SFT对目标任务适应更快，但导致更大的电路破坏和先验能力遗忘；RL保留了更多基础电路，但任务适应速度较慢。存在清晰的机制性权衡。

Conclusion: 电路保留有助于解释RL为何对灾难性遗忘更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [231] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种轨迹感知的大语言模型推理智能体，将分子先导优化中的工具选择建模为序列决策问题，通过前瞻性轨迹规划实现多步分子优化，在ADMET优化任务中优于传统单步优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子先导优化方法多为单步优化，无法考虑序列设计决策的长期影响，缺乏对优化轨迹的前瞻性规划能力。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，利用大语言模型作为推理智能体，将分子优化工具的选择建模为行动轨迹上的序列决策问题，实现前向视角的约束性分子优化。

Result: 在多个ADMET优化任务中，TRACE在优化成功率、性质改善幅度、分子有效性以及分子相似性保持方面均优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 将轨迹感知与LLM推理结合用于分子先导优化的序列决策，能有效提升多步分子优化的性能表现。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [232] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四人非完美信息纸牌游戏Big 2中，通过自监督强化学习框架比较了多种算法，发现PPO在多个对手设置下优于MC-Q、SARSA和Q-learning，并指出适度熵正则化和当前策略自对战能有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非完美信息多人博弈中智能体在隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手环境下的表现，Big 2作为四人不完美信息纸牌游戏为这类研究提供了可控测试平台。

Method: 开发了一个自监督强化学习框架，在统一的环境、输入表示、训练预算和评估协议下，系统比较了PPO、Monte Carlo Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning在Big 2中的表现。

Result: PPO在随机、贪心和启发式Big 2对手上均优于其他算法；适度熵正则化能防止策略过度确定性；当前策略自对战比检查点自对战或固定对手训练在有限预算下提供更优课程。

Conclusion: Big 2可作为研究深度强化学习中非完美信息、多人交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集的有效可控环境。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [233] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于VAE的世界模型在无语言监督的物理探索中，其潜在空间自发形成与物理几何结构对齐的空间语义结构；预测性能与语义对齐共同演化，且KL正则化强度是关键调控因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有语言监督的情况下，世界模型从物理探索中学习到的表征的组织原则是什么。

Method: 在随机具身探索数据上训练VAE-based世界模型，通过方向准确率、位置RSA等指标评估潜在空间的几何结构对齐程度；设置20个时间检查点分析预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系；通过KL正则化（beta=0.1 vs 0.001）的双重敲除实验验证共享驱动假说。

Result: 训练后的编码器方向准确率0.677±0.029（随机基线0.547），位置RSA 0.192±0.047（随机基线0.029，提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐显著相关（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）；高KL正则化（beta=0.1）导致两者同时崩溃至接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则，KL正则化强度是控制几何结构访问和语义对齐能力的关键因素，对设计语义基础的具身智能体有直接启示。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [234] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COM策略，通过在初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束，保留时间序列token的连续性和序数性，显著提升了基于token的时间序列大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于token的时间序列大语言模型忽略了时间序列token固有的连续性和序数性，严重限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter），一种连续性和序数性感知策略，在初始化和训练阶段整合几何约束。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上，COM一致性地提升了基于token的TS-LLM性能，取得了有竞争力的结果和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保留时间序列token的连续性和序数性对基于token的TS-LLM的有效性至关重要，COM策略有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [235] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow提出了一种基于Koopman动力学的专家流匹配方法，通过置信度感知的赢家通吃机制训练多个专业化的残差校正专家，有效解决了标准流匹配在异构时序分布中的谱收缩和模式覆盖不足问题，在多个基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时序信号常呈现多模态和多尺度动态模式，标准流匹配（Flow Matching）使用单一全局向量场估计器，在异构时序分布中不同模态可能经过相近流态但需要不兼容的条件速度，单一估计器在ℓ₂损失下会学习过度平滑的局部传输场近似，导致谱失真和模式覆盖差。

Method: 提出PrismFlow方法，核心包括：(1) Koopman启发的动力学专家，每个专家在潜在空间中学习残差校正，将局部非线性时序演化近似为线性转移；(2) 置信度感知的赢家通吃（Winner-Take-All）目标，仅更新与每个样本最对齐的专家，鼓励专家模式特异性专业化；(3) 采样时，选中的专家向全局传输场添加残差动态校正，保留FM稳定性同时恢复精细高频时序结构。

Result: 在多个基准上，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准FM的谱收缩问题，Context-FID提升15.6%，Discriminative Score提升38.6%，在低数据场景下保持鲁棒性，并在预测和插补任务中同样有效。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过多专家残差校正机制解决了标准流匹配在多模态时序生成中的局限性，显著提升了生成质量和模式覆盖能力。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [236] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出平衡多模态标签重塑（BMLR）方法，从标签侧设计来解决多模态学习中因模态间映射难度差异导致的优化不平衡问题，通过重塑跨模态标签空间以均衡各模态的映射难度，从而提升多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓解模态不平衡的方法主要通过增强弱模态或调整优化梯度，但往往以牺牲强模态的优化能力为代价，且未从模态层面分析学习速率差异的根源。本文基于理论和实验发现，学习速率差异源于各模态特征空间到共享标签空间映射难度的不同，因此需要从标签侧设计来平衡这种差异。

Method: 提出BMLR（Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping），这是首个从标签侧设计促进多模态平衡的方法。通过重塑跨模态标签空间，均衡不同模态间的映射难度，从而促进模态交互并将更丰富的类间信息注入各模态。

Result: 在多种架构上的大量实验表明，BMLR能够持续提升多模态性能，并且与多种模型设计具有良好的兼容性。

Conclusion: BMLR通过从标签侧均衡映射难度，有效解决了多模态学习中的模态不平衡问题，为多模态学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [237] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏差和噪声三部分统计框架研究柏拉图表示假说（PRH），提出线性表示假说（LRH）是跨模态对齐的核心机制，并通过稀疏自编码器、中心化与归一化方法以及词频与对齐的正相关性等实验提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同AI模型间表示对齐（Platonic Representation Hypothesis）背后的统计机制，理解为何不同架构和训练方式下模型仍能产生相似的内部表示。

Method: 将表示分解为信号、偏差、噪声三部分：信号部分通过稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征验证LRH与PRH的关系；偏差部分通过中心化和归一化改善跨模型对齐；噪声部分通过词频与对齐的相关性分析揭示数据稀疏性的影响。最后综合三部分构建统计模型。

Result: 稀疏表示比稠密表示表现出更强的跨模态对齐；中心化和归一化持续改善跨模型对齐；词频与对齐之间存在强且一致的正相关关系，表明表示噪声由数据稀缺驱动。

Conclusion: 提出的统计模型精炼了线性表示假说，并能解释现代多样化AI架构中涌现的表示对齐现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [238] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将经典配对二项式样本量计算方法（Miettinen, 1968）适配到量化基准测试的保守最小可检测效应（MDE）界，通过一个五线预注册模板帮助基准设计者在运行前评估量化声明的可靠性，并在四个模型和四个基准上进行了无配对试点审计验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试中，量化方法之间的性能差异往往被基准本身的噪声（如二项式采样噪声、提示模板变化等）所掩盖，研究者难以判断观察到的差异究竟是真实的量化效应还是随机波动。现有方法缺乏一个简单、可操作的统计预算来预先设定量化声明的可靠性阈值。

Method: 将经典配对样本量计算公式适配到量化基准测试场景，推导出最小可检测效应（MDE）界 δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)，其中 m 为配对项数，ρ_d 为 FP16-NF4 不一致率。在四个模型和四个基准上（k=5 个分割，n=100）进行试点审计，并以 MMLU 提示模板研究作为并行对照，将量化噪声尺度与提示噪声尺度进行比较。

Result: 假设 ρ_d=0.10 时，所有观测到的 NF4-FP16 差异均低于隐含的 MDE 界；大部分分割标准差落在二项式参考值 √(p(1-p)/n) 的 ±1.5 个百分点范围内；OPT-WinoGrande 单元在 ρ_d=0.10 时低于 MDE 但在 ρ_d=0.05 时高于 MDE；MMLU 提示模板变化范围为 2-10 个百分点，达到或超过最大观测量化差异（3.2 个百分点）。

Conclusion: 量化基准测试中 n=100 子样本报告的大部分"基准不可靠性"方差实际上是二项式采样噪声；提示模板变化引入的噪声可能完全淹没量化差异信号，因此量化审计必须先固定提示模板；所提出的 MDE 界为基准设计者提供了一个明确的事前规划工具，配合五线预注册模板可提高量化声明的统计严谨性。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [239] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文蒸馏形式化为隐式记忆管理问题：为每个上下文蒸馏出独立的LoRA适配器形成模块化记忆库，通过检索、路由和自门控机制（Self-Gating）实现显式记忆选择与安全激活，并引入缓存共享提升推理效率。实验表明该方法显著优于检索基线，自门控通过停用不必要记忆提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法将上下文信息压缩到模型参数中，但忽略了在非理想（non-oracle）场景下多个蒸馏后的隐式记忆应如何存储、检索和安全激活的问题。

Method: （1）将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器，构建模块化记忆库；（2）给定查询时，检索候选记忆并将查询路由到最匹配的适配器；（3）使用Self-Gating机制决定是否激活该隐式记忆；（4）引入缓存共享降低推理时的管理开销。

Result: 所提方法在检索设置下显著优于基线方法；Self-Gating机制通过停用不必要的隐式记忆提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏视为隐式记忆管理问题是有效的，模块化记忆库+检索路由+自门控的组合实现了更安全、鲁棒的隐式记忆激活，缓存共享进一步提升了实际部署效率。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [240] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）研究LoRA微调对大型语言模型内部表示几何结构的影响，发现LoRA诱导的特征与预训练SAE字典之间存在较弱的几何对齐，表明LoRA更新在残差流中占据了部分独特的表示结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已成为广泛采用的微调方法，但其内部表示变化尚不明确，理解这些变化对于可解释性和安全审计至关重要。

Method: 提出delta激活框架，在Gemma-2-9B上使用秩4、8、16、32的LoRA，训练适配器专用的SAE，通过余弦相似度、主角度分析和CKA评估特征对齐。

Result: LoRA特征字典与预训练SAE特征几何对齐较弱；适配器专用SAE重建delta激活效果更好；特征密度随秩和深度增加，几何发散在秩间保持稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调可诱导未被预训练可解释性字典完全捕获的特征结构，对机械可解释性、适应分析和安全审计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [241] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的固有几何结构控制扩散模型，通过条件期望算子的奇异函数实现无需重新训练或反向传播的稳定高保真控制，在CIFAR-10上条件准确率提升37个百分点，速度提升4倍，并揭示了有效引导的最佳时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在噪声逐步破坏数据的过程中，仅少数特征对控制保持信息量，如何有效识别并利用这些特征来实现无需重新训练或反向传播的稳定控制是核心动机。

Method: 将有效控制特征刻画为条件期望算子的奇异函数，通过自监督目标学习该基函数，然后将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）直接投影到采样轨迹上。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，条件准确率比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍；同一表示支持标签、CLIP和空间掩码引导，无需辅助模型。

Conclusion: 揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，精准定位了有效引导的最佳时间窗口，为扩散模型控制提供了高效、稳定的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [242] [Cycle-Space Informed Detection of Autoencoded Blind False Data Injection Attacks on Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28912)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AI驱动数据中心和储能系统面临的盲虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），提出了一种拓扑感知的循环空间检测器（CSD）。该检测器利用电网拓扑的循环空间施加结构约束，通过最小循环基（MCB）优化检测泛化误差，无需精确线路参数即可有效检测利用零空间逃逸传统残差检测器和时序异常检测器的数据驱动FDIA。在IEEE 14至118节点系统上的仿真验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于统计或数据驱动的检测方法易被攻击者利用相同数据结构构造微扰绕过。自编码器可学习测量流形并沿雅可比零空间生成扰动，使攻击逃避残差检测器和时序异常检测器，亟需新检测方法。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的循环空间检测器（CSD），利用电网拓扑的循环空间施加结构约束以增强零空间估计；证明使用最小循环基（MCB）可使CSD实现最优检测泛化误差；该方法不依赖精确线路参数，通过拓扑导出的循环约束改善正常与受攻击测量之间的分离度。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、57、118节点系统上的仿真表明，所提方法在真实噪声条件下能有效检测数据驱动的FDIA。

Conclusion: 利用拓扑循环约束而非仅依赖数值零空间估计，CSD能有效检测利用零空间逃避传统检测的数据驱动FDIA，且无需精确线路参数，具有较好的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI-driven data centers and large-scale energy storage systems is increasing the reliance of power system operation on real-time measurement data and automated decision-making. However, many existing detection methods rely on statistical or data-driven analysis of measurements and can fail when attackers exploit the same data structure to craft stealthy perturbations. To illustrate this limitation, we demonstrate a blind False Data Injection Attack (FDIA) in which an Autoencoder learns the measurement manifold and generates perturbations aligned with the Jacobian null space, thereby allowing the attack to evade both residual-based baddata detectors and time-series anomaly detectors. To mitigate data-driven FDIAs which exploit the null space, we propose a topology-informed Cycle-Space Detector (CSD) that leverages the Cycle-Space of the network to impose structural constraints that enhance null space estimation. In addition, we prove that by using the Minimum Cycle Basis (MCB), the proposed CSD achieves the optimal generalization error for attack detection. By exploiting topology-derived cycle constraints rather than relying solely on numerical null space estimation, the proposed method does not require precise line parameters and improves the separation between normal and attacked measurements. Simulation results on IEEE 14-, 30-, 57-, and 118-bus systems demonstrate that the proposed method effectively detects data-driven FDIAs under realistic measurement noise.

</details>


### [243] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM生成的奖励塑造视为调试而非一次性生成，通过诊断驱动的迭代细化方法，在稀疏结构化强化学习任务中显著提升了性能（如DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%），并揭示了奖励泛滥和语义/API误解两种主要失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对稀疏结构化强化学习任务中LLM生成的奖励函数存在失败模式（奖励泛滥、语义/API误解），提出将奖励塑造重新框定为调试过程而非一次性生成，以提高奖励函数质量和训练效果。

Method: 采用诊断驱动的迭代细化方法：首先通过训练诊断和失败模式分类法识别问题，然后针对性地修订奖励函数。使用PPO算法，以MiniGrid为主要评估环境，MuJoCo为边界压力测试。包括多组件消融实验、预算匹配对比、Best-of-3比较等控制实验。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%。控制实验表明：仅指标重新提示效果大幅下降；静态词汇表控制恢复了大部分差距（87.6%；70.7%），表明分类法提示是主要机制，动态标签仅提供部分隔离的增量证据。但连续控制结果展示了方法边界：基于成功的诊断在密集奖励运动任务中可能失效。

Conclusion: LLM生成的奖励塑造更适合采用调试范式而非一次性生成，诊断驱动的迭代细化在稀疏结构化任务中有效。该方法受限于具有可靠接口的稀疏结构化任务及PPO算法，在连续控制等场景中效果有限。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [244] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CosmicFish-HRM，一种通过层次化推理模块(HRM)动态分配计算资源的轻量级语言模型，证明自适应推理深度可作为替代单纯扩大参数规模的新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽推理能力强，但参数规模大、推理成本高。探索在紧凑模型中通过自适应推理深度来替代单纯依赖参数规模提升推理能力。

Method: 构建层次化推理模块(HRM)，在推理时动态分配计算资源：模型根据输入复杂度，在高层次和低层次推理循环间迭代，学习何时停止。同时结合Grouped Query Attention、RoPE和SwiGLU等现代Transformer组件。

Result: 模型学会了非均匀推理行为，在不同任务和输入上分配不同数量的推理步数。额外推理基础设施在小规模下带来开销，但随模型规模增大，HRM核心的相对成本降低。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度是提升推理能力的有前景的替代方案，相比单纯依赖参数规模可能更高效。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [245] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）在基于InceptionTime架构的EEG抑郁症检测中的应用，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶和后部区域（尤其是右半球）存在部分一致的归因模式，但方法间存在显著差异，表明事后可解释性在精神科应用中的有用性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在EEG-based抑郁症检测中表现优异，但其高容量模型的决策过程难以解释，限制了在临床精神科领域的可信应用。因此需要系统性研究事后可解释性方法的适用性和一致性。

Method: 采用InceptionTime架构进行EEG-based MDD检测，在受试者级别分层5折交叉验证框架下，应用五类事后可解释性方法（基于Shapley的DeepSHAP、基于梯度的Integrated Gradients和GradCAM、基于扰动的Occlusion和Permutation Feature Importance），通过全局归因聚合对EEG片段和受试者进行分析。

Result: 不同可解释性方法在额叶、颞叶和后部EEG区域（尤其是右半球）呈现部分收敛的归因模式；梯度法与扰动法之间一致性较高，而DeepSHAP的归因分布与其他方法差异显著；方法间的变异性凸显了不同方法论假设对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 事后可解释性方法在EEG深度学习模型的抑郁症检测中能捕捉部分重叠的相关性结构，与先前EEG研究一致，但结果应为探索性而非确定性神经生理生物标志物或临床应用证据；研究揭示了事后可解释性在精神科黑箱分类器解释中的价值与局限性。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [246] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSimSiam是一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，通过仅使用正样本对从fMRI数据中学习鲁棒且通用的特征，在多种下游任务中超越有监督基线，接近大规模模型性能，适用于数据有限的神经影像应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据采集成本高、精神科评分量表主观性强，导致样本量小且标签质量不稳定，加上fMRI高维度特性，模型过拟合风险大。现有fMRI基础模型需要大量计算资源进行预训练和微调，难以广泛应用。

Method: 提出BrainSimSiam轻量级自监督表示学习框架，利用仅正样本对（positive-only data pairs）进行自监督预训练，学习鲁棒且通用的fMRI特征表示。

Result: 在多个下游分类和回归任务中取得强性能，超越全监督基线方法，性能接近大规模预训练模型。

Conclusion: BrainSimSiam展示了轻量级自监督方法在数据有限的神经影像应用中的潜力，能够高效学习通用fMRI表示，降低对大规模计算资源的依赖。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [247] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现MathCheck基准测试中存在3.1%的语义错误改写，修正后GPT-4o排名从第2降至第4；提出FormInv审计协议，揭示标准基准无法检测的语义一致性差距（SCR跨度50-82% vs 准确率86-96%），并证明存在"无免费基准"推论——基准设计者通过选择改写族隐含决定了模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏对语义等价改写质量的审计，导致评估结果不可靠；标准单模型评估无法检测因改写错误引起的排名变化，且存在更深层的测量缺口——模型间语义一致性差异远大于准确率差异。

Method: 通过跨模型一致性检测自动识别语义错误的改写（MathCheck中>=3/4模型一致认为错误，主评估中>=6/9）；提出FormInv审计协议、SCR（语义一致性率）和每定理Cochran's Q作为主要不变性度量；FormInvSelector用于基于机制的模型选择。

Result: MathCheck中3.1%的改写语义错误；修正后GPT-4o排名下降；自主数据集中47%的连词变体改写语义错误；9个模型的准确率仅跨86-96%，但SCR跨50-82%；存在无免费基准推论，即对任意目标排名都存在对应的改写族权重配置。

Conclusion: 当前基准测试因改写语义错误和忽视语义一致性而存在根本性缺陷；FormInv协议提供了可复现的审计方法，揭示了标准评估无法看到的模型真实表现差异；基准设计者需要意识到改写族选择隐含的排名偏见。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [248] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST是一种因果智能方法，通过学习上下文特定的因果图与结构因果模型，结合约束感知的多目标优化，实现从数据中设计最优干预策略以驱动系统状态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型缺乏机制性洞察和 principled 决策框架，难以通过靶向干预驱动系统从一种状态转换到另一种状态。

Method: COAST包含特征选择、因果发现、因果建模、干预识别与评估等模块化组件，学习上下文因果图和SCM，将分布偏移归因于机制级因果驱动因素，并通过约束感知多目标优化平衡转换效力、干预复杂度和目标状态稳定性。

Result: 在合成基准和真实生物数据上，COAST能够恢复关键因果驱动因素，识别稳健的单靶点和多靶点干预策略，实现期望的状态转换。

Conclusion: COAST提供了一个透明、可解释的因果驱动干预设计框架，并附带机制性解释以指导实验验证。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [249] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型熵（CME）——用独立验证器模型对生成器响应的平均对数似然作为无标签奖励信号，替代传统RLHF中对真实奖励或人工偏好的依赖，在开放指令遵循任务上显著提升多族模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练受限于奖励信号瓶颈：基于真实验证的方法仅适用于数学、代码等自动可验证领域；依赖人工偏好的方法成本高且易被奖励攻击；无标签方法（如多数投票、token熵）依赖模型自身输出，会强化模型自身错误。需要一种不依赖真实标签、不受限于特定领域、且无法被生成器利用一致性作弊的通用奖励信号。

Method: 定义跨模型熵（CME）为生成器输出在独立验证器模型下的平均对数似然，作为无标签奖励信号。将CME集成到GRPO训练循环中，不改变其他训练流程，使得无标签RL得以扩展到开放式指令遵循任务。验证器与生成器相互独立，避免通过自一致性进行奖励攻击。

Result: 在UltraFeedback提示集上，以AlpacaEval 2.0评估，CME奖励在跨四个模型族（Qwen、Llama、Gemma、OLMo）和三种训练阶段（预训练、SFT、指令微调）的LLM-as-Judge对比中均优于未训练基线，经平局调整后胜率范围为52.5%至71.4%。

Conclusion: CME作为连续、无需训练、独立于生成器的奖励信号，有效将无标签RL扩展到传统自参照信号不适用或效果差的开放指令遵循场景，为RL后训练提供了可推广的奖励方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models with reinforcement learning is bottlenecked by the reward signal. Existing approaches require either ground-truth verifiable rewards, restricting training to domains with automatic correctness checks (e.g., mathematics, code execution), or human preference labels, which are expensive to collect and prone to reward hacking. Recent label-free methods replace ground-truth verifiers with self-referential signals like majority voting or token entropy over a model's own outputs, but risk reinforcing a model's own errors. In this work we propose Cross-Model Entropy (CME), the mean log-likelihood of a generator's response under a separate verifier model, as a label-free reward signal for RL post-training. CME is continuous, training-free, and grounded in the principle that responses a verifier finds unsurprising are likely correct or high quality. Because the verifier is independent of the generator, the signal cannot be gamed through self-consistency. We integrate CME into GRPO with no other changes to the training loop, extending label-free RL to open-ended instruction following -- a regime where self-referential signals are inapplicable or poorly suited. On open-ended instruction following (UltraFeedback prompts, evaluated on AlpacaEval 2.0), CME rewards beat the untrained base in head-to-head LLM-as-Judge comparisons across four model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo) and three training regimes (pretrained, SFT, and instruction-tuned), with tie-adjusted win rates ranging from 52.5% to 71.4%. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [250] [Momentum Based Reward Design for Low Emission Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29693)
*Chinmay Mundane,Amith Manoharan,Arun Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于动量的奖励函数（MBRF）用于深度强化学习交通信号控制，在SUMO仿真中相比传统延迟/队列奖励和经典控制器，实现了更好的吞吐量-排放权衡和更稳定的学习行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通信号控制系统难以适应动态交通状况，而现有DRL方法中基于延迟和队列的奖励函数往往导致短视或不稳定的策略，需要更有效的奖励设计来平衡通行效率与环境影响。

Method: 提出动量奖励函数（MBRF），鼓励车辆持续移动而非单纯惩罚拥堵；基于深度强化学习框架，在SUMO仿真环境中进行评估，对比延迟奖励、队列奖励、Max Pressure和LQF等基线方法。

Result: MBRF在等待时间、队列长度、吞吐量和CO2排放等标准指标上均优于基线方法，实现了更好的吞吐量-排放折衷，且学习过程更稳定。

Conclusion: 基于动量的奖励函数能有效提升DRL交通信号控制的性能，在缓解拥堵和减少排放之间取得更优平衡，为自适应交通信号控制提供了有前景的奖励设计方案。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion is a growing global issue contributing significantly to long commute times and environmental pollution. Traditional traffic signal control systems often fail to adapt to dynamic traffic conditions. Adaptive traffic signal control can improve urban traffic without changing road infrastructure. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown strong performance for this task, but existing delay and queue-based rewards often produce short-sighted or unstable policies. This paper proposes a Momentum-Based Reward Function (MBRF) that encourages vehicles to keep moving rather than penalizing congestion alone. The method is evaluated in SUMO (Simulation of Urban MObility) using standard traffic metrics such as waiting time, queue length, throughput, and CO2 emissions. Results show that the proposed reward produces better throughput-emission trade-offs and more stable learning behavior than delay or queue-based rewards, as well as classical controllers such as Max Pressure and LQF.

</details>


### [251] [Theoretical Foundations and Effective Algorithms for Policy-Aware Simulator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29032)
*Christoph Dann,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于战略鲁棒性而非预测准确性的模型学习目标，通过零和极小极大博弈形式化，并提供了理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MBRL智能体通过最小化预测损失学习世界模型，但强化学习优化器会利用模型微小不准确性，导致模拟器利用和现实差距问题。

Method: 将学习模拟器的目标设定为战略鲁棒性，形式化为模型玩家与对抗策略玩家之间的零和极小极大博弈。提供了三项理论分析：在线学习保证（次线性遗憾界）、基于评论家的简化（局部评论家损失界定额策略价值差距）、误差-MDP对偶性（最坏情况策略等价于标准RL问题）。基于对偶性设计了可证明收敛的主动数据选择算法。

Result: 在连续控制任务中，战略重要区域的预测误差降低1.5-2.2倍，纯仿真训练的策略达到了接近最优的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: 战略鲁棒性替代预测准确性作为模型学习目标，可有效缩小仿真与现实的差距，使仿真训练策略能迁移到真实环境。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) agents typically learn world models by minimizing predictive loss. However, powerful RL optimizers inevitably exploit minor model inaccuracies, leading to simulator exploitation and a reality gap where policies succeed in simulation but fail in the real world. We propose that the objective for learning simulators should be strategic robustness rather than predictive accuracy, and formulate this as a zero-sum minimax game between a model player and an adversarial policy player. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis: (1) an online learning guarantee showing the game is learnable with sublinear regret bounds; (2) a tractable critic-based simplification bounding the global policy-value gap by the local critic's loss; and (3) an Error-MDP duality, proving that finding the worst-case policy is formally dual to a standard RL problem where the reward is the one-step critic error. This duality yields a provably convergent active data selection algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate that our approach reduces prediction error in strategically important regions by $1.5$-$2.2\times$ and enables policies trained purely in simulation to match near-optimal real-world performance.

</details>


### [252] [Moment Matching Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29033)
*Yiyan,Liang,Sifei Liu,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL)框架，利用最大均值差异（MMD）技术匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，加速基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中的推理过程，在D4RL任务中实现更高效的计算性能，并在离线到在线强化学习任务中展现出更强的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中推理延迟过长，迭代采样过程造成严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出MoMa QL框架，采用统计假设检验中的最大均值差异（MMD）技术，匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，对条件分数函数施加强正则化以保证分布级收敛。

Result: 在D4RL任务中计算效率更高且性能相当，在离线到在线RL任务中因加速流策略的动作采样过程而展现出更优性能。

Conclusion: MoMa QL通过矩匹配机制有效加速生成模型在RL中的推理，在多种超参数下保持稳定，兼具计算效率与竞争性能。

Abstract: Score-based and flow-based generative models exhibit remarkable expressive capacity in capturing complex distributions, and have been extensively deployed in tasks ranging from image generation to reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, these models suffer from prolonged inference latency, which imposes a significant computational bottleneck in RL with iterative sampling. To overcome this limitation, we propose a new framework named Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL), which utilizes a technique from statistical hypothesis testing known as maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) that intend to match all orders of statistics between the original and target distribution. By enforcing strong regularization on all moment statistics, this algorithm guarantees distribution-level convergence for conditional score function and remains stable under various hyperparameters. Empirically, we show that our method MoMa QL is more computationally efficient with a comparable if not competitive performance in various D4RL tasks. Remarkably, by accelerating the action sampling process for flow-based policies, MoMa QL demonstrates superior performance in offline-to-online RL tasks because of faster and stronger adaptability for online interactive finetuning.

</details>


### [253] [Model Merging by Output-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29101)
*Bethan Evans,Benjamin Etheridge,Stephen Roberts,Jared Tanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将模型融合形式化为一个关于残差更新的凸二次规划（QP）问题，通过校准输入和微调模型输出最小化平方输出校准目标，统一了现有启发式方法，并给出了一个闭式诊断指标来预测融合质量。实验表明QP在单层设置中匹配或超越现有方法，并在多层融合中通过逐层顺序算法在语言和视觉基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型融合方法（如任务算术、模型汤、TIES、DARE）计算高效且经验成功，但依赖启发式设计选择，缺乏形式化的最优性保证。作者希望建立一个有理论基础的融合框架，既能统一现有方法，又能提供最优性保证和可解释的诊断工具。

Method: 将模型融合表述为一个关于残差更新的凸二次规划问题，目标是最小化平方输出校准误差。该框架将现有方法作为特例纳入，并导出一个闭式诊断指标（残差能量被所选基捕获的比例）来预测融合质量。针对多层融合，提出了逐层顺序融合算法。

Result: 在单层设定中，QP方法匹配或优于现有方法；当最优基相对于廉价的对角QP有显著收益时，文中给出了刻画条件。在多层融合中，顺序逐层算法在语言和视觉基准上取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 凸二次规划框架为模型融合提供了理论最优性保证，统一了现有启发式方法，并提供了可解释的融合质量诊断工具。该框架在多个基准上验证了有效性，为更鲁棒、可解释的模型融合铺平了道路。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model without retraining. Existing methods - such as task arithmetic, model soups, TIES, and DARE - are computationally efficient and empirically successful, but rely on heuristic design choices and lack formal optimality guarantees. We show that merging can be formulated as a convex quadratic programme over residual updates, yielding weights that minimise a squared-output calibration objective using calibration inputs and fine-tuned model outputs, and subsuming existing methods as special cases. Our framework yields a closed-form diagnostic - the fraction of residual energy captured by a chosen basis - that predicts downstream merge quality using only the calibration set. Empirically, the QP matches or outperforms existing methods in the single-layer setting, and we characterise when the optimal basis provides significant gains over the cheaper diagonal QP. We extend to multi-layer merging via a sequential layer-wise algorithm and demonstrate consistent gains across language and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [254] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性探针可以近乎完美地解码表征，但该表征方向可能与模型实际使用的计算方向正交，导致探针完全无法反映模型真实推理过程。跨模型规模、家族和领域均观察到这种“读出一中介正交性”，直接威胁探针作为安全监控的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑线性探针解释性的可靠性——探针能高精度解码表征，但该表征是否真正参与模型的计算过程？如果探针学到的方向与模型实际使用的因果子空间正交，则探针给出的解释是误导性的。

Method: 在日历日期时长推理任务上，对比线性探针（sin/cos探针恢复一年中的第几天）与分布式对齐搜索（DAS）找到的四维因果子空间；测量两个子空间之间的角度并与Haar均匀随机子空间的零分布比较；通过逆向工程电路（注意力头利用±30和±61天的QK偏移路由月份上下文，MLP将绝对日期转换为时长）揭示因果路径；使用稀疏自编码器分解验证探针对齐特征与DAS对齐特征的语义分离。

Result: 探针方向和DAS因果子空间之间的角度与随机子空间之间的角度无显著差异（正交）；消融探针方向不影响模型表现，消融DAS四维子空间则完全破坏性能；该正交性在1.5B-9B四个规模、两个模型家族以及空间位移和符号算术两个额外领域均得到复现。

Conclusion: 探针可以学到与模型实际计算完全正交的方向，导致探针解释具有误导性；这一发现直接否定了将探针作为运行时安全监控的提议，因为探针可能在高置信度报告一个模型已"静默放弃"的方向。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [255] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了蒸馏和量化是扩展LLM模型家族至新规模和硬件格式的经济有效方法，基于Apertus 8B模型生产了Apertus-v1.1系列（最高4B参数，1.7T token训练），在覆盖广泛硬件需求的同时实现了成本效益和强精度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛用于各类应用场景（如聊天助手、数据标注），需要满足不同的预算和硬件约束。当前模型发布趋势是以批量的方式释放不同大小的相似模型以覆盖尽可能广的约束范围，但高效扩展模型家族至新规模和硬件格式仍面临挑战。

Method: 采用蒸馏（Distillation）和量化（Quantization）作为经济有效的方法，基于开源的Apertus 8B LLM，生产Apertus-v1.1模型家族（最高4B参数），在1.7T许可token上进行训练。

Result: 该方法在覆盖大范围硬件和系统需求时展现出成本效益和强精度性能，成功将模型家族扩展到新的规模和硬件格式。

Conclusion: 蒸馏和量化是扩展LLM模型家族至新规模和硬件格式的经济有效途径，能够在不显著牺牲精度的情况下满足多样化的硬件约束需求。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [256] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对完全信息下具有事件级纯差分隐私的随机决策在线学习问题，提出了一个无时间依赖的算法，证明了遗憾界为O(log K/Δ_min + log K/ε)，回答了Hu和Mehta提出的COLT开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hu和Mehta在COLT上提出的开放问题：确定在纯事件级差分隐私下，随机决策在线学习的最优间隙相关遗憾率。已知下界为Ω(log K/min{Δ_min, ε})，需要设计算法匹配该下界。

Method: 算法将时间划分为指数增长的块，每个块内执行单一动作；通过指数机制基于前一块的数据无关随机前缀选择下一动作；利用随机前缀将块遗憾转化为所有前缀长度上的softmax选择误差之和；采用单一熵-势参数控制所有隐私主导的大间隙动作。

Result: 证明了显式的遗憾界Reg_T ≤ 1000·(log K/Δ_min + log K/ε)，适用于任意时间T，匹配已知下界（常数因子未优化）。

Conclusion: 该算法在纯差分隐私约束下达到了最优的间隙相关遗憾率，解决了该开放问题；算法是无时间依赖（horizon-free）的，即不需预设时间T。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [257] [RUBRIC-ARROW: Alternating Pointwise Rubric Reward Modeling for LLM Post-training in Non-verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29156)
*Haoxiang Jiang,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Wanying Wang,Ran Xu,Tony Yu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RUBRIC-ARROW 提出了一种交替训练框架，联合训练 rubric 生成器和基于 rubric 的条件判断器，在强化学习阶段仅使用成对偏好数据，通过概率评分规则减少平局，并采用交替 GRPO 方案训练逐点评估器，在奖励建模准确率和下游策略训练中均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逐点奖励建模在主观、不可验证的场景中难以进行绝对评分；基于 rubric 的方法虽有改进，但依赖前沿大模型且布尔聚合方式容易产生平局问题。

Method: 提出 RUBRIC-ARROW 交替框架：(1) 联合训练 rubric 生成器与 rubric 条件判断器；(2) 强化学习阶段仅使用成对偏好数据；(3) 引入基于概率的评分规则减少平局；(4) 设计阶段特定的基于偏好的奖励；(5) 采用交替 GRPO 方案共同训练逐点评估器。

Result: 实验结果表明 RUBRIC-ARROW 在奖励建模准确率上具有竞争力，并且在下游策略后训练中带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RUBRIC-ARROW 通过交替训练框架有效解决了主观场景中逐点奖励建模的评分困难和平局问题，为 LLM 后训练提供了一种更可靠的评估方法。

Abstract: Pointwise reward modeling offers critical signals for LLM post-training, yet struggles with absolute scoring in subjective, non-verifiable settings. Rubric-based methods address this by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, but existing approaches typically depend on frontier LLMs and suffer from ties caused by hard Boolean aggregation. We present RUBRIC-ARROW, an alternating framework that jointly trains a rubric generator and a rubric-conditioned judge, with its RL stage using only pairwise preference data. Our method couples a probability-based scoring rule that reduces ties with phase-specific preference-based rewards and an alternating GRPO scheme that together train the pointwise evaluator. Extensive experiments show that RUBRIC-ARROW achieves competitive reward-modeling accuracy and yields consistent gains for downstream policy post-training.

</details>


### [258] [Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157)
*Yifei Zuo,Dhruv Pai,Zhichen Zeng,Alec Dewulf,Shuming Hu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallax，一种可扩展的局部线性注意力机制（Local Linear Attention），通过参数化设计替代数值求解器，并配合硬件感知算法提升计算强度。在0.6B和1.7B规模上的预训练表明，Parallax在困惑度和下游任务上均实现帕累托改进，且Muon优化器能解锁其能力，首次展示了注意力机制中架构与优化器的强协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力使用局部常数估计（local constant estimate），在联想记忆中的偏差-方差权衡存在不足。局部线性注意力（LLA）理论上能提供更优的权衡，但由于计算复杂度和数值稳定性问题，尚未在大规模LLM预训练中得到验证。本文旨在解决LLA的可扩展性问题。

Method: 1) 参数化局部线性注意力：用可学习的类Query投影器替代LLA中的数值求解器，用于探测KV协方差；2) 将Parallax置于由带宽、探针构造和仿射结构连接的注意力机制统一家族中；3) 提出硬件感知算法，提升计算强度（arithmetic intensity），使注意力进入更偏计算受限（compute-bound）的状态；4) 原型解码核在多种batch size和上下文长度下与FlashAttention 2/3相当或更优。

Result: 在0.6B和1.7B规模上预训练，Parallax持续获得困惑度改进，并迁移至下游基准任务。在参数匹配和计算匹配条件下均表现优越，实现帕累托改进。预训练消融实验发现Muon优化器能解锁Parallax的能力，首次在注意力机制中展示架构-优化器协同设计（codesign）经验证据。

Conclusion: Parallax成功将局部线性注意力扩展至LLM预训练规模，在保持计算效率的同时提升了模型质量。硬件感知算法使其在推理阶段与FlashAttention性能相当或更优。架构与优化器的协同设计为未来注意力机制改进提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the central paradigm in artificial intelligence, yet the core computational primitive of attention has remained structurally unchanged. Local Linear Attention (LLA) is an attention mechanism derived from nonparametric statistics in the test-time regression framework.
