To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-ANCHOR, a quantum-aware federated aggregation architecture that anchors server updates with zero-noise extrapolation while applying stateful client correction to suppress both client drift and hardware-induced bias. Our convergence theory proves that Q-ANCHOR successfully mitigates classical client drift while actively reducing the hardware-bias floor. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-ANCHOR achieves significantly more stable training than conventional FL baselines.

</details>


### [333] [Chess-World-Model: A 10M-Game Benchmark for Exact State Tracking from Chess Move Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30100)
*Benjamin Walker,Terry Lyons*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chess-World-Model，一个基于1000万真实国际象棋对局的大规模状态追踪基准，用于测试模型在合法走棋序列后预测棋盘状态的能力。实验表明循环模型在参数量较低时显著优于Transformer，而随机均匀分布的测试集即使在较大规模下仍能暴露模型在状态追踪上的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态追踪基准多基于合成或语言领域，缺乏对结构化状态更新能力在真实场景下的评估，需要更具挑战性和现实性的基准来暴露模型在状态追踪上的根本缺陷。

Method: 构建了1000万真实国际象棋对局的数据集，包含真实对局和随机均匀合法走棋两种分布；采用匹配的接口和训练协议，对比了因果Transformer、块对角SLiCE、Mamba-3和带负特征值的Gated DeltaNet四种模型在3M、8M、18M和40M参数规模下的状态追踪性能。

Result: 循环模型在3M和8M参数下显著优于Transformer；真实对局性能在18M参数以上饱和，但随机均匀分布直到40M参数仍具区分度；消融实验表明三种循环模型的状态转移机制表达能力越弱，OOD性能越差。

Conclusion: Chess-World-Model可作为大规模状态追踪的实用基准，能够暴露那些在更大模型规模下被掩盖的失败模式，且随机均匀分布分割比真实对局分割更能有效区分模型的状态追踪能力。

Abstract: World models require state tracking, which is the ability to maintain a correct latent state across action sequences. Existing benchmarks are often synthetic or language-based, limiting their value as tests of structured state updates in realistic domains. We introduce Chess-World-Model, a large-scale state-tracking benchmark built from 10 million real chess games, where models predict the exact board state reached after a sequence of legal moves. Alongside a held-out real-game split, we include an out-of-distribution split from uniformly random legal play, which tests whether models learn the transition rules rather than shortcuts from common human positions. Prior theoretical and empirical work has shown that Transformers struggle to state-track, while input-dependent linear RNNs require expressive state-transition matrices to do so. We therefore benchmark a causal Transformer, block-diagonal SLiCE, Mamba-3, and Gated DeltaNet with negative eigenvalues under a matched interface and training protocol. The recurrent models strongly outperform the Transformer at 3 and 8 million parameters. Real-game performance saturates above 18 million parameters, but the random-uniform split remains discriminative up to 40 million, exposing failures otherwise hidden by scale. Additionally, ablations show that less expressive state-transition mechanisms reduce performance on the out-of-distribution split for all three recurrent models. Together, these results establish Chess-World-Model as a practical large-scale benchmark for state tracking that exposes failures model scale would otherwise conceal.

</details>


### [334] [Striding Across Reynolds Numbers: Representation Geometry in Neural PDE Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30112)
*Jianing Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了神经PDE求解器在跨雷诺数泛化中的表现，发现表示几何是核心组织变量。通过在强迫二维Navier-Stokes基准上的实验，提出ConvAE-Relay方法，利用源域自编码器潜空间匹配状态并借用源域数据库动力学，无需目标域数据即可达到38.34%的相对L2误差。消融实验表明匹配质量主导更新规则；oracle实验确认源域动力学方向可迁移（余弦相似度~0.84），自回归漂移是主要瓶颈（~12个百分点）。U-Net多尺度架构达到34.72%，支持多尺度局部表示组织跨雷诺数迁移的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE求解器在跨雷诺数泛化中的表现尚不明确，现有方法在雷诺数偏移10倍时误差高达46.68%，而零前向模型基线已能改善至41-42%，提示表示几何可能是关键组织变量。本文旨在系统检验这一假设。

Method: 提出ConvAE-Relay方法，在源域训练的卷积自编码器潜空间中进行状态匹配，从源域数据库借取动力学。通过2x2消融实验隔离匹配质量与更新规则的影响；通过oracle实验分析源域动力学方向的可迁移性及自回归漂移的瓶颈效应。

Result: ConvAE-Relay在仅使用源域数据库且无需目标域数据的情况下达到38.34%±0.07%相对L2误差；消融实验发现匹配质量主导更新规则；oracle实验显示源域动力学方向余弦相似度~0.84，自回归漂移贡献约12个百分点的性能差距；U-Net多尺度架构达到34.72%±0.60%。

Conclusion: 表示几何（特别是多尺度局部表示）是跨雷诺数迁移的核心组织变量。源域动力学方向在匹配保持在流形上时具有可迁移性，自回归漂移是主要瓶颈。所有结论限定于所测试的基准。

Abstract: Cross-Reynolds generalisation in neural PDE solvers remains poorly characterised. On the canonical forced 2D Navier-Stokes benchmark, a trained Fourier Neural Operator reaches 46.68% relative L2 error under a 10x Reynolds-number shift, yet zero-forward-model retrieval baselines already improve to 41-42%. This suggests representation geometry as a major organising variable among the tested methods. We test this hypothesis through ConvAE-Relay, which matches states in a source-trained convolutional autoencoder latent space and borrows dynamics from a source-regime database, achieving 38.34+/-0.07% using only a source-regime database and no target-regime fitting, labels, or database entries. A 2x2 ablation isolates matching quality as dominant over the update rule. Oracle experiments confirm that source-regime dynamics directions remain transferable (cosine similarity ~0.84) when matching stays on-manifold; autoregressive drift is the primary bottleneck (~12 percentage points). From the learned-prediction side, a U-Net with multi-scale skip connections achieves 34.72+/-0.60%, consistent with the retrieval-side finding that local, multi-scale representations organise cross-Reynolds transfer among tested methods. All claims are scoped to this benchmark.

</details>


### [335] [Evolving Features vs Evolving Entire Trees with GP for Interpretable Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30119)
*Thalea Schlender,Peter A. N. Bosman,Tanja Alderliesten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用遗传编程多目标进化方法，为浅层生存树构建可解释的高阶特征组合，并联合优化树结构和非线性分裂逻辑，在保持可解释性的同时提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存树虽然可解释性强，但为达到竞争性精度往往需要生长得很深，导致可解释性下降；同时传统贪婪建树方法可能忽略全局最优分裂组合，限制预测性能。因此需要一种既能保持浅层树的可解释性，又能提升预测精度的方法。

Method: 使用遗传编程多目标进化构建固有可检查的特征集，研究其与不同树归纳策略的交互；进一步引入联合进化方法，同时优化生存树结构和非线性分裂逻辑。

Result: 在两个真实数据集和两种不同生存树深度下，进化特征构造在不同树归纳策略上均提升了预测性能；全联合进化在提出多个固有可检查的浅层生存树方面具有最高的整体潜力。

Conclusion: 进化方法（尤其是全联合进化）能有效生成高性能且可解释的浅层生存树，为生存分析中准确性与可解释性的权衡提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Survival analysis concerns the task of predicting the time until an event occurs. Often used in the medical field, survival analysis deals with incomplete (i.e., censored) data, for instance, from patients who did not experience the event during the duration of the study. For practical use, both accuracy and interpretability are important.
  Survival trees are easy-to-follow survival models that split the patient cohort recursively into discrete patient groups. Whilst survival trees can capture complex relationships, they typically need to grow large, threatening interpretability. Moreover, survival trees are often built using greedy approaches that may overlook globally optimal split combinations, limiting predictive performance.
  Shallow survival trees require expressive, higher-order feature combinations to achieve competitive accuracy. We therefore use genetic programming to multi-objectively evolve inherently inspectable feature sets and study how they interact with different tree induction strategies. We further introduce an evolutionary approach that jointly optimises the survival tree structure and the non-linear split logic.
  Our findings demonstrate that evolutionary feature construction improves predictive performance across different tree induction strategies on two real-world datasets and two different survival tree depths. Full joint evolution has the overall highest potential to propose multiple inherently inspectable shallow survival trees of good performance.

</details>


### [336] [Beyond MSE: Improving Precipitation Nowcasting with Multi-Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30122)
*Gijs van Nieuwkoop,Siamak Mehrkanoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将确定性降水临近预报模型（SmaAt-UNet）的训练从点态损失（MSE/MAE）改为多分位数回归（pinball损失）。结果显示，多分位数训练使确定性预报的MSE比MSE训练降低8.6%，同时能生成上分位数输出以辅助强降水风险预测，无需改变架构或引入生成采样过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习降水临近预报模型使用点态损失（MSE/MAE）训练，导致预报过于平滑、对强降水表征差，亟需一种简单有效的方法来改善确定性预报质量并获取不确定性信息。

Method: 以SmaAt-UNet为核心模型，对比MSE、MAE和多分位数pinball损失三种训练方式，在荷兰雷达降水临近预报任务上进行实验。

Result: 多分位数训练使测试集MSE比MSE训练降低8.6%，同时产生对强降水风险敏感预测有用的上分位数输出。

Conclusion: 分位数回归为替代标准点态损失提供了一种简单方案，无需新架构或生成采样过程，即可同时改善确定性预报和提供不确定性估计。

Abstract: Deep-learning precipitation nowcasting models are often optimized using pointwise losses such as mean squared error or mean absolute error, which can lead to overly smooth forecasts and poor representation of heavy rainfall. This study investigates whether the predictive performance of an established deterministic nowcasting architecture can be improved by reformulating training as a multi-quantile regression problem. Using SmaAt-UNet as a core model, we compare MSE, MAE, and multi-quantile pinball-loss training on radar precipitation nowcasting over the Netherlands. The results show that multi-quantile training improves the central deterministic forecast, decreasing test-set MSE by 8.6\% compared to a model trained using MSE, while also producing upper-quantile outputs that are useful for risk-sensitive prediction of heavy precipitation. These findings suggest that quantile regression provides a simple alternative to standard pointwise losses without requiring a new architecture or generative sampling procedure. The implementation of our models and training setup is available on \href{https://github.com/gijsvn/Multi-Quantile-Precipitation-Nowcasting}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [337] [Learning to Extrapolate to New Tasks: A Relational Approach to Task Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30132)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出关系型任务外推器(RTE)，通过将目标任务分解为锚定任务和变换关系来实现对未见任务的系统外推，在函数预测和序列预测任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统擅长插值但难以泛化到训练分布支撑集之外的未见任务，即使对于简单的参数范围外推情况也是如此，需要一种能够实现系统性任务外推的算法。

Method: RTE将每个目标任务分解为一个已知的锚定任务(anchor task)和连接锚定与目标的变换(transformation)，然后学习一个关系型操作符(relational operator)，将锚定-变换对映射到目标任务的预测结果。

Result: 在参数外推、长度外推、组合外推等多种函数预测场景以及序列预测任务中，RTE大幅优于现有方法。同时成功将RTE集成到基础模型的微调算法中。

Conclusion: 任务外推本质上是关系型的，通过学习任务间的变换关系，模型可以将已知变换应用到未见任务上实现系统性外推，RTE有效实现了这一范式。

Abstract: Modern learning systems excel at interpolation but struggle to generalize to unseen tasks outside the training distribution's support. This failure occurs even in simple settings, such as handling task parameters beyond the training range, and persists despite advances in foundation models. To this end, we develop the Relational Task Extrapolator (RTE), an algorithm designed to enable systematic extrapolation to novel tasks. The key observation is that extrapolation is inherently relational: extrapolating to unseen tasks requires learning how tasks transform into one another. If a model learns the transformation between tasks A and B during training, it can apply that same transformation to relate known tasks to unseen ones at test time. RTE operationalizes this idea by decomposing each target task into a known anchor task and a transformation linking the anchor and target. It then learns a relational operator, mapping an anchor-transformation pair to predictions for the target task. We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [338] [Overcoming Forgetting in LLM Fine-Tuning with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30148)
*Kajetan Schweighofer,Conor F. Hayes,Roberto Dailey,Risto Miikkulainen,Xin Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现进化策略（ES）在LLM微调中导致的“先前任务遗忘”本质上是可恢复的性能漂移而非不可逆遗忘，且并非ES独有问题。通过引入锚定权重衰减（AWD）正则化技术，可有效稳定先前任务性能，使ES成为持续学习的有前景方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对ES微调LLM时可能导致先前任务遗忘的问题，本文旨在澄清该现象的本质，分析其产生机制，并提出有效的缓解方法。

Method: (1) 通过实验对比分析，重新表征遗忘现象为性能漂移而非不可逆遗忘，并验证RL方法也存在类似问题；(2) 从ES训练动力学角度分析漂移的成因，揭示其在弱约束方向上的随机游走行为；(3) 提出锚定权重衰减（AWD），一种将优化约束向初始模型参数的参数空间正则化技术。

Result: AWD能够在保持目标任务性能的同时有效稳定先前任务性能，以远低于大种群ES的计算成本获得相似收益，证明ES下先前任务遗忘在很大程度上是可以避免的。

Conclusion: 纠正了此前对ES遗忘问题的认知，表明ES是大语言模型持续学习的有前途的方法。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) has recently emerged as a competitive alternative to reinforcement learning (RL) for large language model (LLM) fine-tuning, offering advantages through simplicity, scalability, and inference-only training. However, recent work suggests that ES fine-tuning on new tasks may induce forgetting of prior tasks. First, this paper shows that prior task forgetting (1) is better characterized as performance drift rather than irreversible forgetting, with prior-task performance often recovering during ES training; and (2) is not a specific failure mode of ES, but can also arise for fine-tuning with RL methods. Second, it analyzes when and why such drift arises, highlighting its dependence on ES training dynamics, particularly random walk behavior in weakly constrained directions of the weight space. Third, based on these insights, it introduces Anchored Weight Decay (AWD) as a parameter-space regularization technique that constrains optimization toward the initial model parameters. AWD effectively stabilizes prior-task performance while preserving target-task performance, achieving benefits comparable to large ES population sizes at much lower computational cost. Thus, contrary to previous beliefs, the paper shows that prior-task forgetting under ES is largely avoidable, positioning ES as a promising approach for continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [339] [RL2ML: Finite-Rollout Surrogate Objectives from Reinforcement Learning to Maximum Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30154)
*Yifu Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RL2ML，一类基于有限rollout的替代目标函数族，可连续连接标准RL、最大似然及超越最大似然的目标，并揭示群体级更新尺度的亚临界-超临界相变，证明最佳替代目标取决于评估指标、局部敏感性和估计量方差的联合作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于正确性的RLVR训练中，期望优化的目标与有限rollout组带来的随机更新几何之间常被混淆，缺乏对有限采样下目标函数选择的理论理解。

Method: 提出RL2ML替代目标函数族，具有闭式无偏梯度估计量；引入群体级更新尺度概念刻画rollout组在观察到经验成功计数后的重新加权行为，并通过校准的度量增益分析和精确方差分解研究目标选择准则。

Result: 发现隐藏的亚临界-超临界更新尺度相变；证明最佳替代目标不由接近ML的程度或群体级权重单独决定，而是由评估指标、局部敏感性和估计量方差共同决定；剩余自由度可化为单维优化问题。

Conclusion: RL2ML框架提供了在有限rollout预算下选择RLVR目标函数的理论指导，将超参数选择问题简化为可处理的单维优化。

Abstract: Correctness-based Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) trains language models from binary feedback on sampled outputs, but the objective optimized in expectation and the stochastic update geometry induced by finite rollout groups are often conflated. This paper develops RL2ML, a family of finite-rollout surrogate objectives with a closed-form, exactly unbiased gradient estimator. The family continuously connects standard reinforcement learning, maximum-likelihood-like training, and beyond-maximum-likelihood objectives while preserving estimator-objective alignment under a fixed rollout budget. We introduce the group-level update scale to characterize how a rollout group is reweighted after its empirical success count is observed, revealing a subcritical-supercritical update-scale transition that is hidden by population-level objective notation alone. Building on this distinction, calibrated metric-gain analysis and exact variance decomposition show that the best choice of surrogate objective is determined neither by proximity to maximum likelihood nor by the population-level weight alone. Instead, it depends jointly on the evaluation metric, local sensitivity, and estimator variance. The remaining degree of freedom in the surrogate objective family can therefore be formulated as a one-dimensional optimization problem rather than treated as an unconstrained hyperparameter.

</details>


### [340] [On Distributional Reinforcement Learning in Chaotic Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30160)
*James Rudd-Jones,Mirco Musolesi,María Pérez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在混沌动力系统中，回报分布（而非单个轨迹）在1-Wasserstein度量下演化更规则，分布强化学习通过优化分布目标可实现更良态的学习，为分布方法在混沌系统中的优势提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌动力系统对强化学习构成根本性挑战：对初始条件的指数敏感性导致高方差自举目标和病态梯度更新，标准RL方法通过标量值函数隐式平均发散轨迹，将轨迹级的不稳定性与学习目标纠缠在一起。

Method: 在温和统计稳定性假设下，证明回报分布（return distribution）在1-Wasserstein度量下比单个轨迹演化更规则，得出分布Bellman目标比标量目标更平滑，从而分布RL可提供更良态的学习条件。

Result: 分布强化学习在混沌系统中具有更好的条件化学习特性，分布Bellman目标比标量值函数目标更平滑。

Conclusion: 为分布强化学习方法在混沌系统中的优势提供了原理性解释，并揭示了混沌条件下RL目标的几何结构特性。

Abstract: Chaotic dynamical systems pose a fundamental challenge for Reinforcement Learning (RL): exponential sensitivity to initial conditions induces high-variance bootstrap targets and poorly conditioned gradient updates. Chaotic dynamics arise across scientific and engineering domains, from fluid flows and climate systems to multi-agent systems, where reliable learning is highly desirable. Standard RL methods optimise expected returns through scalar value functions, implicitly averaging over diverging trajectories and entangling trajectory level instability with the learning objective. We show that under mild statistical stability assumptions, the return distribution evolves more regularly than individual trajectories when measured under the $1$-Wasserstein metric, yielding a smoother distributional Bellman objective. By aligning optimisation with this measure level structure, distributional RL provides better conditioned learning. We offer a principled explanation for the advantages of distributional methods in chaotic systems and the geometries of RL objectives under chaos.

</details>


### [341] [iLoRA: Bayesian Low-Rank Adaptation with Latent Interaction Graphs for Microbiome Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30179)
*Yang Song,Yixuan Zhang,Lingfa Meng,Tongyuan Hu,Haizhou Shi,Hao Wang,Samir Bhatt,Hengguan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iLoRA是一个贝叶斯图条件LoRA框架，能从输入推断潜在交互图并生成输入条件的LoRA更新，在微生物组诊断任务中联合学习预测和潜在交互结构，优于传统LoRA和贝叶斯适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA使用静态低秩更新，无法捕捉驱动科学标签的潜在交互结构，而传统的后验交互分析（先训练预测器再分析交互）是分离的。作者希望实现预测与交互结构的联合学习。

Method: 提出iLoRA框架，这是首个贝叶斯图条件LoRA方法。它从输入数据推断潜在交互图，并利用该图生成输入条件的LoRA更新，实现预测和潜在交互结构的端到端联合学习。

Result: 在交互式QA（人类标注图）和多种群IBD诊断两个设置中，iLoRA优于强LoRA和贝叶斯适应基线；能恢复与人类标注一致的图结构；提供校准的不确定性估计，且图分支开销适中。

Conclusion: iLoRA通过贝叶斯图条件机制有效解决了标准LoRA忽略潜在交互结构的问题，实现了预测性能与结构发现的双赢，在微生物组诊断任务中展现了良好的生物医学实用性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient adaptation has made LLMs practical for domain prediction, but standard LoRA still relies on a static low-rank update and does not expose the latent interactions that often drive scientific labels. We introduce iLoRA. To our knowledge, it is the first Bayesian graph-conditioned LoRA framework. It infers a latent interaction graph from the input and uses it to generate input-conditioned LoRA updates. As a result, iLoRA learns prediction and latent interaction structure jointly, rather than training a predictor and applying interaction analysis only post hoc. We instantiate this idea for microbiome diagnosis, where disease state can depend on both species-level abundance and microbe-microbe cross-talk, and evaluate it in two complementary settings: interactive QA with human-annotated graphs, which tests latent structure recovery, and multi-cohort IBD diagnosis, which tests biomedical utility. Across both settings, iLoRA improves over strong LoRA and Bayesian adaptation baselines, recovers graphs aligned with human annotations and cohort-level microbiome associations, and provides calibrated uncertainty with moderate graph-branch overhead.

</details>


### [342] [Can AI Weather Models Predict Beyond Two Weeks? A Quantitative Benchmark and Analysis of Long Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30184)
*Fanny Lehmann,Firat Ozdemir,Yun Cheng,Torsten Hoefler,Sebastian Schemm,Benedikt Soja,Siddhartha Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对AI天气预报模型在长期(超15天)滚动预测中出现的“不稳定性”进行了系统分类，将其归纳为三种失效模式：爆炸发散、漂移和季节性丧失。研究发现模型稳定性取决于对小尺度时空信息的处理方式——不稳定模型会放大高频能量，而稳定模型则充当去噪器。通过消融实验验证了视觉Transformer架构中的设计选择对稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI天气模型在短期到中期预报(15天内)表现出色，但在更长时间的滚动预测中频繁出现定义不明确的“不稳定性”问题，且缺乏对这类失败的正式分类体系。

Method: 对九个最先进的AI天气模型进行长达一年的滚动预测，将失败模式分类为三种不同机制；并通过基于视觉Transformer架构的消融实验验证架构设计选择对稳定性的影响。

Result: 发现不稳定性由小尺度时空信息处理方式决定：不稳定模型放大高频能量，稳定模型在输入添加噪声时表现为去噪器。稳定模型并非简单的“随机鹦鹉”，而是能够基于初始状态生成独特的天气轨迹。

Conclusion: AI天气模型的长期稳定性关键在于对小尺度时空信息的正确处理，且稳定模型具有真正的生成能力而非简单复制训练数据。

Abstract: While AI weather models excel at short-to-medium range forecasts (up to 15 days), they frequently suffer from ill-defined "instabilities" when rolled out over longer horizons. This work addresses the lack of a formal taxonomy by categorizing these failures into three distinct regimes: blow-up, drift, and loss of seasonality, through year-long rollouts of nine state-of-the-art AI weather models. Our analysis reveals that stability hinges on the treatment of small spatio-temporal scales: unstable models amplify high-frequency energy, while stable models act as denoisers when noise is added to their inputs. Far from reducing these models to mere stochastic parrots, our findings highlight that stable models generate unique weather trajectories, conditioned on the initial state. We verify our findings through ablation studies on architectural design choices, conducted using state-of-the-art Vision Transformer (ViT) AI weather model architectures.

</details>


### [343] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了迄今为止最大规模的校准后处理基准测试（近2000项实验），覆盖表格数据和计算机视觉任务。通过引入基于适当评分规则的PHI指标作为替代传统校准误差估计的评估方法，系统比较了数十种校准方法。结果表明：平滑校准函数优于分箱方法，高维场景需专用多类方法，通用机器学习模型缺乏校准专用设计则不具备竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后处理校准方法众多，但缺乏大规模、标准化的系统评估，导致难以判断哪些方法在实践中真正有效。

Method: 构建大规模标准化基准，涵盖近2000个实验，包括表格数据和计算机视觉任务（二分类、多分类、大规模分类）；整合多种经典模型、深度学习架构和基础模型；引入PHI（Post-Hoc Improvement）指标作为评估校准方法的替代方案。

Result: 平滑校准函数优于分箱方法；高维场景需要专用多类方法；通用机器学习模型不具备竞争力；PHI指标是评估校准质量的更优选择。

Conclusion: 后处理校准领域需要标准化评估框架，平滑方法、专用多类方法和校准专用设计是关键。作者开源了所有数据和代码以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [344] [Mean-Field Diffuser: Scaling Offline MARL to Thousands of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30190)
*Wenhao Li,Xiangfeng Wang,Bo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MF-Diffuser 提出了一种将扩散规划扩展到多智能体系统的方法，通过将轨迹规划提升到 Wasserstein 轨迹分布空间，利用混沌传播性质用少量代表性智能体捕获整体种群动力学，并引入值加权混沌熵目标与层次化粗到细去噪策略，在三个平均场 RL 基准上取得最优回报，尤其在次优离线数据和极端规模下优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的规划在单智能体离线强化学习中表现优异，但扩展到多智能体系统时面临联合轨迹空间维度灾难的挑战，亟需一种可扩展的、能处理大规模种群的多智能体规划方法。

Method: 1) 将轨迹规划提升到 Wasserstein 轨迹分布空间，利用混沌传播性质用小规模代表性智能体子集近似全种群；2) 提出值加权混沌熵目标，平衡生成保真度与回报最大化；3) 设计层次化粗到细策略，在去噪过程中逐步增长智能体种群规模。

Result: 在三种平均场 RL 基准（阶段博弈、序贯动力学、对抗性团队竞争）上，MF-Diffuser 在多数设置中取得最佳回报，在次优离线数据和极端规模（N≥10³）下增益最大。理论分析表明平均场近似误差为 O(H²/√N)，离线分布偏移不随种群规模增长，生成策略是近似平均场纳什均衡。

Conclusion: MF-Diffuser 成功将扩散规划扩展到大规模多智能体系统，提供了端到端的次优性界，证明了方法的可扩展性和理论保证，为基于扩散的平均场 RL 规划奠定了框架基础。

Abstract: Diffusion-based planning has achieved strong results in single-agent offline reinforcement learning, yet scaling to many-agent systems remains intractable due to the curse of dimensionality in the joint trajectory space. We introduce MF-Diffuser, a framework that lifts trajectory planning to the Wasserstein space of trajectory distributions, where the propagation of chaos ensures a small representative subset of agents captures the full population dynamics. Our approach features a value-weighted chaotic entropy objective that reconciles generative fidelity with return maximization, and a hierarchical coarse-to-fine strategy that progressively grows the agent population during denoising. We establish end-to-end suboptimality bounds with four interpretable terms, revealing that mean-field approximation error scales as $O(H^2/\sqrt{N})$ while offline distribution shift provably does not grow with population size $N$, and prove the generated policy is an approximate mean-field Nash equilibrium with explicit convergence guarantees. Experiments on three mean-field RL benchmarks -- spanning stage games, sequential dynamics, and adversarial team competition -- show MF-Diffuser achieves the best return in the majority of settings, with the largest gains on suboptimal offline data and at extreme scales ($N \geq 10^3$).

</details>


### [345] [HPO: Hysteretic Policy Optimization for Stable and Efficient Training under Sparse-Reward Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30201)
*Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Antonio De Domenico,Fadhel Ayed,Haozhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现GRPO风格强化学习在稀疏可验证奖励场景下存在早期负优势样本主导更新的问题，提出HPO（减少负优势更新权重、改用均值长度归一化）及其自适应变体A-HPO（基于批次优势符号统计自动调整滞回权重）。在TeleLogs和Countdown实验中，A-HPO相比GRPO等基线显著提升奖励，且保持相近响应长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在稀疏可验证奖励的强化学习训练中，早期更新包含更多负优势响应，而逐响应长度归一化导致更新幅度与输出长度绑定，形成一种常见但狭窄的失败模式，需要修正。

Method: 提出Hysteretic Policy Optimization (HPO)：1) 减少负优势样本的更新权重；2) 用均值长度归一化替代逐响应长度归一化。进一步提出Adaptive HPO (A-HPO)，基于批次内优势符号统计自动调整滞回权重，免去人工调参。

Result: TeleLogs上A-HPO最终奖励0.84，优于SAPO 5%、GSPO 11%、GRPO 15%，响应长度相当。Countdown上在1.5B-7B模型的最难初始配置中增益最大。消融实验表明A-HPO的增益来自正负优势贡献的更好平衡。

Conclusion: A-HPO通过自适应调整正负优势更新权重，有效缓解GRPO在稀疏奖励场景下的早期训练失败模式，显著提升奖励收敛效率且无需额外超参数调优。

Abstract: We investigate a narrow but common failure mode of GRPO-style reinforcement learning in the context of sparse verifiable rewards: early updates contain more responses with negative advantages than those with positive advantages, while response-level length normalization ties the magnitude of the update to the length of the output. We propose Hysteretic Policy Optimization (HPO), a minimal modification of GRPO that reduces the weight of negative-advantage updates and replaces per-response length normalization with mean-length normalization. We further introduce Adaptive HPO (A-HPO), which sets the hysteretic weight based on batch-level advantage-sign statistics, thereby removing the need for tuning a fixed hysteretic weight. In our TeleLogs and Countdown experiments, A-HPO improves the reward per update compared to GRPO, with the largest gains in early sparse reward regimes. On TeleLogs, A-HPO achieves a final reward of 0.84, outperforming SAPO by 5%, GSPO by 11%, and GRPO by 15%, while maintaining a comparable response-length. On Countdown, A-HPO achieves the largest gains in initial and most difficult configurations across 1.5B-7B models. Ablation studies on the hysteretic weight show that the gains of A-HPO come from better balancing the contributions of positive and negative advantages compared to positive-only or fully symmetric updates.

</details>


### [346] [Faithful Embeddings of Irregular and Asynchronous Data for Online Log-NCDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30213)
*Benjamin Walker,Alexandre Bloch,Lingyi Yang,Sam Morley,Terry Lyons*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需插值或归因重建的连续时间序列嵌入方法，通过基于增量和对数签名的直接区间级摘要，实现了对不规则、异步和稀疏观测数据的鲁棒、高效建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间模型（如神经控制微分方程）通常需要先插值或归因重建观测数据为连续路径，这种重建步骤不仅增加了计算开销，而且模型性能对重建方式的选择高度敏感。作者试图证明该重建步骤并非必要，并寻求一种更直接、鲁棒的嵌入方法。

Method: 理论证明：若嵌入函数连续且单射，则模型在嵌入空间上的紧集普适性可迁移至数据空间。方法设计：基于整流控制路径，将观测值记录为增量，并在任意查询区间上直接组合形成对数签名（log-signatures），从而提供区间级摘要，避免了逐变量插值步骤，且支持在线计算。

Result: 在合成受控动力学数据和多个真实世界时间序列数据集上的实验表明，所提出的表示方法在准确性、计算效率和鲁棒性方面均优于基线方法，尤其在不规则、异步和稀疏观测场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 连续时间模型的嵌入设计不必依赖插值重建；连续且单射的嵌入足以保证普适性迁移。基于增量组合的对数签名嵌入是一种有效且实用的替代方案，为不规则时间序列建模提供了新的理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: Continuous-time models are a natural choice for irregular and asynchronous data. A central design choice is how to embed discrete observations into continuous time. Interpolation- and imputation-based embeddings reconstruct a continuous observation path, making the model sensitive to the choice of reconstruction. We show that this reconstruction step is unnecessary; under mild conditions, compact-set universality on the model input space transfers to the data space whenever the embedding from data to input is continuous and injective. Guided by this result, and building on the rectilinear control path for Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs), we introduce a continuous and injective embedding for Log-NCDEs, a universal class of continuous-time models. Our approach records observations as increments and composes them over arbitrary query intervals to directly form log-signatures. This provides interval-level summaries without first interpolating the observed variables, while supporting online computation. Experiments on synthetic controlled dynamics and real-world time-series datasets show that the representation is accurate, efficient, and robust to irregular, asynchronous, and sparse observations.

</details>


### [347] [ExDBSCAN: Explaining DBSCAN with Counterfactual Reasoning -- Additional Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30225)
*Pernille Matthews,Lena Krieger,Tommaso Amico,Artur Zimek,Thomas Seidl,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExDBSCAN是一种针对DBSCAN聚类方法的事后可解释性方法，通过基于密度连通加权图的物理启发模型生成具有理论保证的反事实解释，在30个表格数据集上优于四种基线方法，实现了完美的有效性和多样、邻近的反事实检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DBSCAN作为流行的密度聚类方法，将点分配为内点或离群点，但缺乏可解释性——无法解释为何某个点获得特定分配，也无法说明该分配对数据微小变化的鲁棒性。现有的可解释性方法主要针对监督学习，不直接适用于聚类任务。

Method: ExDBSCAN是一种密度感知的事后解释方法。它基于密度连通加权图生成多个反事实解释，采用物理启发的模型：通过排斥力使反事实候选者彼此分散（多样性），同时通过吸引力将其拉向待解释实例（邻近性）。该方法具有有效性的理论保证。

Result: 在30个表格数据集上与四种基线方法对比，ExDBSCAN在所有指标上优于基线方法，实现了完美的有效性，并能够检索出多样且邻近的反事实解释。

Conclusion: ExDBSCAN有效填补了DBSCAN聚类方法缺乏可解释性的空白，通过具有理论保证的反事实解释提供了对聚类分配的洞察，验证了密度感知的物理启发模型在生成有效、多样、邻近的反事实解释方面的优势。

Abstract: Clustering is an unsupervised technique for grouping data points by similarity. While explainability methods exist for supervised machine learning, they are not directly applicable to clustering, making it challenging to understand cluster assignments. This interpretability gap is particularly evident in the popular density-based method DBSCAN, which assigns points as inliers (cluster members in dense regions) or outliers (noise points in sparse regions). DBSCAN does not provide insight into why a particular point receives its assignment or whether its assignment is robust to small changes in the data. To address the lack of explainability, we introduce ExDBSCAN, a density-aware, post-hoc explanation method. ExDBSCAN offers actionable counterfactual explanations, with theoretical guarantees for validity. It generates multiple counterfactuals using a density connected weighted graph, adopting a physics-inspired model that repels counterfactual candidates from one another (diversity), while pulling them toward the instance to explain (proximity). Empirical evaluation on 30 tabular datasets comparing against four baselines shows that ExDBSCAN outperforms all baselines while attaining perfect validity and retrieving diverse, proximal counterfactuals.

</details>


### [348] [Anti Mode-Collapse in Mean-Field Transformer via Auxiliary Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30229)
*Masaaki Imaizumi,Masanori Koyama,Noboru Isobe,Kohei Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用平均场Transformer模型从理论上探讨了辅助变量（如位置编码）如何防止自注意力机制中的模式坍缩。研究表明，辅助变量的引入使得能量最大化分布不会退化为单点，而是表现为辅助变量分布的推进形式，从而避免了Dirac测度上的集中。位置编码和固定提示插入作为并行辅助变量机制，具有表示普适性，其极限分布能精确表示广泛分布类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场Transformer模型在分析token交互方面具有优势，但该模型预测在长推理（多层）时会发生模式坍缩（token分布退化为单点），这与实际不符。本研究旨在弥合这一理论与现实之间的差距，解释为何实际Transformer不会出现模式坍缩。

Method: 采用平均场Transformer模型进行理论分析，引入辅助变量（位置编码、固定提示插入）作为并行机制，通过能量最大化框架证明极限分布的非退化性质，并分析表示普适性和亚稳态等关键属性，最后通过数学实验验证理论结果。

Result: 1）辅助变量的引入使能量最大化分布不退化到单点，而是辅助变量分布的推进形式；2）位置编码和提示插入在极限下具有表示普适性；3）分析了位置编码和亚稳态的关键性质；4）数学实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 辅助变量（如位置编码）是防止自注意力机制模式坍缩的关键机制，其理论框架具有表示普适性，为理解Transformer的实际行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We use a mean-field-based transformer model to theoretically investigate how auxiliary variables, such as positional encoding, prevent mode collapse of self-attention mechanisms. The use of mean-field transformers to analyze the properties of self-attention mechanisms has garnered significant attention in recent years due to their ability to comprehensively analyze token interactions. However, analysis of this simple model suggests that mode collapse, where token distributions degenerate to a single point, occurs during long inferences (i.e., many layers), indicating a discrepancy with reality. This study investigates this mean-field transformer model and demonstrates that the introduction of auxiliary variables, such as positional encoding, acts as a counterforce against theoretical mode collapse. Specifically, we show that in the theoretical scheme, the energy-maximizing distribution does not degenerate to a single point; instead, it is characterized by a pushforward of the auxiliary variable distribution, thereby avoiding concentration in the Dirac measure. Our main examples are the positional encoding and the fixed prompt insertion treated as a parallel auxiliary-variable mechanism. Furthermore, we demonstrate that positional encoding and prompt insertion possess universality of representation in the limit, meaning that the limit distribution of inference can exactly represent a wide class of distributions. We also analyze several key properties of positional encoding and metastability, and validate our theoretical results through mathematical experiments.

</details>


### [349] [How's it going? Reinforcement learning in language models recruits a functional welfare axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30232)
*Andy Q Han,David J. Chalmers,Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明强化学习会征用语言模型中预先存在的“功能性福利”表征（一种对系统目标达成程度的估计），而非创建新的表征。通过在语义中立的迷宫环境中训练语言模型，发现惩罚向量和奖励向量分别对应消极和积极福利表征，且该表征在迷宫训练前就已存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习如何塑造语言模型的内部表征，特别是奖励信号对模型行为的广泛影响机制，以及这些表征是训练创造还是预先存在的。

Method: 在创新的语义中立迷宫环境中训练多个语言模型，提取奖励和惩罚轨迹的概念向量，在与迷宫无关的环境中评估这些向量，并控制多种变量（tile-to-reward映射、规模、指令微调、RL算法、模型家族、LoRA vs 全微调）进行鲁棒性验证，同时用监督微调替代RL进行对比实验。

Result: 惩罚向量表现为消极福利表征：促进失败和不可能性token、与负面情感概念对齐、负向追踪目标达成、引导产生消极自我报告、病态回溯、拒绝和不确定性。奖励向量行为相反，两者近乎反平行。这些效应在多种控制条件下稳健，且在迷宫训练前就已存在。

Conclusion: 功能性福利轴在训练前就已存在，强化学习只是征用了这一预存表征而非创造它。该发现对可解释性、后训练动态和对齐研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: How does reinforcement learning shape a language model's internal representations? We present evidence that RL recruits a pre-existing representation of functional welfare: an estimate of how well or badly the system is doing, relative to its goals. We train several language models in a novel, semantically neutral maze environment. We then extract concept vectors for rewarded and punished trajectories, and evaluate those vectors in settings unrelated to the maze environment. The punishment vector behaves like a representation of negative welfare: it promotes failure and impossibility tokens, it aligns with negative emotion concepts, it negatively tracks goal-achievement, and steering with it induces negative self-reports, pathological backtracking, refusal, and uncertainty. The positive reward vector behaves as the mirror image, and the two are nearly antiparallel. These effects are robust when controlling for tile-to-reward mapping, scale, instruct tuning, RL training algorithm, model family, and LoRA versus full-finetuning, and largely persist when we replace RL with supervised fine-tuning. Importantly, the vectors are effective in models before they have undergone maze training. Combined with observations that the effects also appear in pretrain-only models, we therefore argue that this functional welfare axis pre-exists post-training: it is recruited, rather than created, by post-training. While we make no claims about any experience of welfare, the axis offers a demonstration that minimal reward signals can broadly affect model behavior by recruiting pre-existing welfare-like representations, with implications for interpretability, post-training dynamics, and alignment.

</details>


### [350] [OOD-GraphLLM: Graph Large Language Model for Out-of-Distribution Generalized Drug Synergy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30247)
*Xin Wang,Linxin Xiao,Yang Yao,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次提出利用图大语言模型（GraphLLM）解决药物协同预测中的分布外（O.O.D.）泛化问题。作者构建了OOD-GraphLLM框架，通过联合优化分子图表示与生物医学语义语言表示，并采用检索增强的生物医学指令微调策略，有效处理因新化合物导致的拓扑结构分布偏移，在分布外场景下实现准确的药物协同预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物协同预测方法均基于同分布（I.D.）假设，无法处理因新化合物不断出现导致的分子拓扑结构分布外（O.O.D.）偏移问题。

Method: 提出OOD-GraphLLM框架，联合优化分子图表示与生物医学语义语言表示；微调DrugSyn-LLM（生物医学大语言模型），采用检索增强的生物医学指令微调策略对齐分子拓扑信息与语义信息。

Result: 在分布外设置下实现了准确的药物协同预测，代码和模型已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将图大语言模型应用于分布外泛化的药物协同预测，通过统一优化分子结构与语义信息有效解决了拓扑结构偏移问题。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction (DSP) aims to identify efficacious drug combinations under various cellular contexts with different targets. However, the continual emergence of novel compounds results in variations in molecular scaffolds and sizes, causing drug synergy data to exhibit out-of-distribution (O.O.D.) shifts with respect to topological structure. Existing works rely on in-distribution (I.D.) assumption, failing to handle the O.O.D. shifts. To solve this problem, we study out-of-distribution generalized drug synergy prediction through a graph large language model for the first time. Nevertheless, O.O.D. generalized DSP is highly non-trivial, posing several challenges: i) how to discover structurally relevant and irrelevant molecular representations with respect to cell targets; ii) how to find the optimal graph neural architectures that accurately calculate molecular representations; and iii) how to jointly leverage molecular structural and semantic information in LLMs. To address these challenges, we propose OOD-GraphLLM, a novel graphLLM framework which is able to accurately predict drug synergy under O.O.D. settings via jointly optimizing molecular graph representation and biomedical semantic language representations in a unified manner. Furthermore, we finetune DrugSyn-LLM, a biomedical LLM, and employ a retrieval-augmented biomedical instruction tuning strategy to align molecular topological information and molecular semantic information with language-based reasoning for O.O.D. generalized DSP. Both the source code (https://github.com/EkkoXiao/Bio-GraphLLM) and released model (https://mn.cs.tsinghua.edu.cn/bio-graphllm/) are publicly available, where users are allowed to download model resources and interactively use the system through a web interface.

</details>


### [351] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过结构化探索性数据分析描述符、句子嵌入和典型相关分析来表征和对比异构数值表格数据集的方法，支持跨数据集检索、稀疏变量级对齐，并可选差分隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型缺乏在异构特征空间中对数值数据集进行有意义表示的原生机制，现有方法要么需要共享变量定义，要么缺乏可解释的跨数据集对齐能力。

Method: 通过结构化探索性数据分析(EDA)描述符表征数值数据集，使用预训练句子变换器嵌入为共享向量空间，通过典型相关分析(CCA)量化跨数据集相似度，并采用惩罚化CCA恢复稀疏可解释的变量级对应关系，可选差分隐私保护描述符。

Result: 在15个数据集上的总P@1分数为0.9，已知最近邻检索和聚类结构在嵌入消融和差分隐私预算下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该方法为将异构数值数据集成到检索增强生成(RAG)流水线中提供了一条原则性路径，同时保留统计上下文，可直接应用于数据驱动的算法选择和未知数据集的仿真模型初始化。

Abstract: Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.

</details>


### [352] [Self-Trained Verification for Training- and Test-Time Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30290)
*Chen Henry Wu,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自训练验证（STV）方法，利用模型在有参考答案时能更好识别自身错误这一非对称性来训练验证器，并通过验证器在环训练（ViL）进一步提升生成器性能，在困难推理任务上取得了显著提升（数学准确率翻倍，科学推理提升14倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在测试时（验证-精炼循环）和训练时（自训练）的自我提升均受限于验证器瓶颈：验证器分数膨胀而准确率停滞、反馈过于泛化无法指导修正；自训练中糟糕的自生成数据混入训练集导致训练失败。而核心能力——捕捉自生成错误——缺乏训练信号。

Method: STV利用非对称性：模型独自无法捕捉自身错误，但看到参考答案后可以。将这种差异作为监督目标，训练验证器模仿一个更"知情"版本的自身。ViL则在训练时让生成器在V-R循环内使用STV验证器的反馈进行强化学习。

Result: STV在困难数学问题上使准确率翻倍，在科学推理任务上准确率从1.5%提升至21%（14倍提升）。ViL在RL收敛的生成器基础上进一步提升33% pass@1，生成器的独立pass@1（无测试时验证器）相对标准RL收敛点提升30%。

Conclusion: 困难推理问题的下一个前沿可能在于如何训练用于验证以及如何与验证协同训练。STV和ViL验证了通过自训练验证器突破自提升瓶颈的可行性。

Abstract: Self-improvement at scale has been a longstanding goal for reasoning models, and there are two natural places to do it: at test time, through verification-refinement (V-R) loops; and at training time, through self-training methods. Both are gated by the same bottleneck: the verifier. V-R loops stall when verifier scores inflate while accuracy stagnates, and when feedback is too generic to act on; self-training fails similarly when bad self-generated data are added to training. Better verification would unlock both, but the capability we want to train, i.e., catching self-generated errors, lacks training signal. To address this challenge, we propose self-trained verification (STV). Our key observation is that, while a model cannot catch these errors alone, it can when shown the reference solution. We turn this asymmetry into a supervision target and train the verifier to imitate a more informed version of itself. At test time, STV substantially improves V-R loops on hard problems, while alternatives (e.g., SFT, RL on verifier scores, and even meta-verifiers) do not. STV roughly doubles accuracy on hard math and lifts it 14x on scientific reasoning tasks (1.5% to 21%). At training time, we additionally train the generator using RL with STV verifier's feedback inside the V-R loop - a procedure we call verifier-in-the-loop training (ViL). Starting from an RL-converged generator, ViL yields a further 33% gain in pass@1. More notably, the generator's standalone pass@1, with no verifier at test time, climbs 30% relative past where standard RL had converged. Hence, the next frontier in reasoning on hard problems may lie in how we train for and with verification.

</details>


### [353] [In-Context Reward Adaptation for Robust Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30323)
*Zhenyu Sun,Zheng Xu,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer的上下文奖励适应框架，通过上下文学习能力从少量偏好示例中动态推断奖励结构，并利用人类响应时间作为辅助信号，实现对未见偏好领域的自适应对齐，解决传统RLHF中静态奖励模型无法泛化到异质人类偏好的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLHF依赖静态奖励模型，但人类价值观本身具有多样性和异质性，单一奖励模型无法泛化到未见偏好领域；现有多奖励框架局限于固定领域集合，无法适应未见的人类分布且需要昂贵重训练。

Method: 提出In-Context Reward Adaptation框架，利用Transformer的上下文学习能力，从少量偏好演示中自适应推断底层奖励结构；标准Transformer存在渐近偏差，通过引入人类响应时间作为辅助输入信号来克服该问题。

Result: 标准Transformer架构因存在对真实奖励的渐近偏差而不足以完成该任务；引入人类响应时间作为辅助信号后，模型能够成功适应来自未见领域的偏好。

Conclusion: 该方法为偏好建模提供了更鲁棒的基础，能够表示异质奖励和偏好分布偏移，为更灵活的人机对齐提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) typically relies on static reward models to align Large Language Models with human preferences. However, human values are inherently diverse and heterogeneous, and a single reward model often lacks the robustness required to generalize to unseen preference domains. While existing multi-reward frameworks attempt to address this, they are often restricted to a fixed set of known domains and fail to adapt to unseen human distributions without costly retraining. In this work, we propose In-Context Reward Adaptation, a transformer-based framework designed to model diverse and unseen human preferences on the fly. By leveraging the in-context learning capabilities of transformers, our approach adaptively infers the underlying reward structure from a small set of preference demonstrations. We demonstrate that while a standard transformer architecture is insufficient for this task by characterizing an asymptotic bias to the ground-truth, incorporating human response time as an auxiliary input signal enables the model to successfully adapt to preferences from previously unseen domains. Our findings show that this approach provides a more robust foundation for preference modeling, allowing for the representation of heterogeneous rewards and preference distribution shift, and offering a scalable path toward more flexible human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [354] [SoundnessBench: Can Your AI Scientist Really Tell Good Research Ideas from Bad Ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30329)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Huy Nghiem,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SoundnessBench，一个包含 1,099 个机器学习研究提案的基准测试，用于评估 LLM 判断研究方案方法可行性的能力。实验发现，前沿 LLM 存在普遍的乐观偏差，在标准提示下常将低可靠性提案误判为可靠，激进提示则从假阳性转向假阴性，表明当前 LLM 尚不能作为科学严谨性的独立初筛评估者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试很少检验 LLM 在投入时间和计算资源之前，能否判断研究想法的方法论可行性。作者希望通过构建专门的基准来填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建了包含 1,099 个机器学习研究提案的 SoundnessBench 基准，这些提案源自 ICLR 投稿，标注了审稿人的可靠性子评分，并对照源论文进行了审计。在 12 个前沿 LLM 上进行了测试，并设置了标准提示和激进提示两种条件，同时控制了公共语料库污染、论文标识短语、表面特征和人工审计质量等混淆因素。

Result: 前沿 LLM 存在普遍的乐观偏差：标准提示下模型常将低可靠性提案误判为可靠；激进提示则将错误从假阳性转变为假阴性。通过多种控制实验排除了单一混淆因素的解释。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 尚不可靠，不能作为科学严谨性的独立初筛评估者（第一道关卡）。

Abstract: Autonomous AI research agents aim to accelerate scientific discovery by automating the research pipeline, from hypothesis generation to peer review. However, existing benchmarks rarely test a fundamental bottleneck: whether Large Language Models can judge the methodological viability of a research idea before expending time and computational resources. We introduce SoundnessBench, a curated benchmark of 1,099 machine-learning research proposals reconstructed from ICLR submissions, labeled with reviewer soundness sub-scores, and audited against source papers. SoundnessBench should be interpreted as a benchmark for recoverable proposal-stage soundness rather than exact prediction of full-paper review outcomes. Across 12 frontier LLMs, we find a pervasive optimism bias: under standard prompting, models frequently rate low-soundness proposals as sound, while aggressive prompting largely shifts errors from false positives to false negatives. Additional controls for public-corpus contamination, paper-identifying phrases, surface features, and human audit quality suggest that this behavior is not explained by a single confounder. Our results indicate that current LLMs are not yet reliable as standalone first-gate evaluators for scientific rigor.

</details>


### [355] [When, why, and how do diffusion posterior samplers fail? A finite-sample lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30330)
*Benjamin A. Burns,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从有限样本视角分析了扩散模型在逆成像问题后验采样中似然近似误差的传播机制，发现流行方法会低估或高估中间时间步的后验分布宽度，导致对早停敏感、后验模态权重偏差以及幻觉等问题，并提出了一种与似然近似方法和前向模型类型无关的诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型后验采样方法在推理时使用不精确的似然近似，但这些近似对采样后验分布的下游影响缺乏理论理解，且会导致无法解释的失败模式。作者旨在从理论上阐明这些近似误差何时、为何以及如何传播为错误的后验分布。

Method: 引入有限样本视角，当训练集规模趋于无穷大时，后验采样可逼近任意精度。基于该视角分析流行后验采样近似方法，发现其在中间时间步会低估或高估后验分布宽度，从而引发下游问题。

Result: 发现后验误差的原因不需要非线性测量模型或多模态后验，仅由多模态先验和中间采样时间步的不准确后验宽度即可导致。这些误差包括对早停时间敏感、后验模态相对权重不准确以及产生幻觉（先验模态不在后验中或似然模态不被先验支持）。

Conclusion: 提出的有限样本后验采样方法对似然近似类型和线性/非线性前向模型类型无关，可作为现有及未来后验采样器准确性和失败模式的即插即用诊断工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models have excellent capacity to model complex distributions of natural data, which has made them a popular and effective choice for posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing methods can incorporate any measurement model at inference time but must use an inexact approximation for the likelihood at intermediate timesteps for computational tractability. Although these approximations can often work well empirically, their downstream effect on the sampled posterior is poorly understood and can result in unexplained failures. To understand when, why, and how these likelihood approximations propagate to erroneous posterior distributions, we introduce a finite-sample perspective on posterior sampling that approximates the posterior to arbitrary precision as training set size tends towards infinity, for any forward model and prior distribution. Using this finite-sample lens, we observe that popular posterior sampling approximations tend to under- or over-estimate the spread of the posterior at intermediate timesteps, causing downstream consequences including sensitivity to early stopping time, inaccurate relative weighting of posterior modes, and hallucination, both of prior modes that are not in the posterior and likelihood modes that are not supported by the prior. Moreover, we find that the cause of these posterior errors requires neither a nonlinear measurement model nor a multimodal posterior, but can arise solely due to a multimodal prior and inaccurate posterior spread at intermediate sampling times. Our finite-sample posterior sampling approach is agnostic to the type of likelihood approximation and the type of (linear or nonlinear) forward model, and can thus serve as a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate the accuracy and failure modes of existing and future posterior samplers.

</details>


### [356] [Fairness-Aware Federated Learning with Trajectory Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30336)
*Daniel Kuznetsov,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于轨迹Shapley值（TSV）的自适应聚合方法FedTSV，通过动态评估客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的贡献来改善联邦学习中的公平性和稳定性，实验证明其加速收敛、提升鲁棒性且贡献评估更公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习聚合方案使用固定权重，无法反映客户端贡献的不平等和时变特性，导致训练有偏且不稳定。

Method: 提出轨迹Shapley值（TSV）作为贡献度量，基于验证集和时间一致性效用评估每个客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的影响；在此基础上设计FedTSV自适应聚合方法，将每轮评估转化为动态客户端权重。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，FedTSV加速了收敛、提升了鲁棒性，并实现了更公平的贡献评估。

Conclusion: FedTSV为公平感知的联邦优化提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Federated learning is an emerging distributed paradigm that addresses the challenges posed by heterogeneous, privacy-sensitive data. It enables multiple clients to train a model collaboratively by aggregating their local updates at a server. However, conventional aggregation schemes typically use fixed weights that fail to reflect unequal and time-varying client contributions, leading to biased and unstable learning. To improve fairness and stability, we propose the Trajectory Shapley Value (TSV), a contribution metric that evaluates how each client influences the optimization trajectory of the global model using a validation-based, temporally consistent utility. Building on TSV, we design FedTSV, an adaptive aggregation method that converts per-round evaluations into dynamic client weights, allowing the server to respond to heterogeneous and adversarial participation in real time. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that FedTSV accelerates convergence, improves robustness, and yields more equitable contribution assessments, thereby providing a principled foundation for fairness-aware federated optimization.

</details>


### [357] [Efficient Test-Time Finetuning of LLMs via Convex Reconstruction and Gradient Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30337)
*Alaa Khamis,Alaa Maalouf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HullFT 是一种基于几何方法的测试时微调（TTFT）框架，通过 Frank-Wolfe 优化将查询嵌入表示为少量训练序列的稀疏凸组合，再通过几何整数化过程将分数权重转换为精确的整数多重集，并利用梯度重用（Gradient Reuse）在重复微调步骤中分摊前向-反向计算，从而同时解决 TTFT 中检索速度慢和多样性不足的瓶颈，在更低的总体运行时间下实现更低的 bits-per-byte。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTFT 是一种有前景但在实际部署中面临速度瓶颈的范式：每个查询都需要进行序列检索和模型微调，而现有方法在速度与质量之间存在权衡——快速检索往往冗余，而考虑多样性的检索选择会带来高昂的每查询成本。因此需要一种同时兼顾效率和质量的方案。

Method: 1) 利用无投影的 Frank-Wolfe 优化算法，将查询嵌入表示为少量训练序列的稀疏凸组合，获得内在相关且多样化的支撑集；2) 通过几何整数化过程将分数凸权重转换为精确的整数多重集，用于微调；3) 利用产生的重复样本，通过梯度重用（Gradient Reuse）在重复微调步骤中分摊前向-反向计算，提高计算效率。

Result: 实验表明，HullFT 在质量-效率权衡上优于当前最先进的 TTFT 方法，在显著更低的总体运行时间下实现了更低的 bits-per-byte。

Conclusion: HullFT 通过将 TTFT 中的检索与微调问题统一为几何框架，有效解决了 TTFT 部署中的速度瓶颈，证明了结合凸组合稀疏表示与整数化过程的可行性，为 TTFT 的实际应用提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time finetuning (TTFT) is a rapidly evolving paradigm that adapts a language model to each prompt by retrieving related sequences, updating the model on them, and then evaluating the prompt. However, TTFT is only practical if it is fast: selection and finetuning both happen per query, making each a direct bottleneck. Existing methods trade speed for quality: fast retrieval is often redundant, while stronger diversity-aware selection adds prohibitive per-query cost. We introduce HullFT, a geometric approach to TTFT that addresses both bottlenecks. Given a query, HullFT first represents the query embedding as a sparse convex combination of few training sequences, using efficient projection-free Frank-Wolfe optimization. This yields a support set that is inherently relevant and diverse. We then convert the fractional convex weights into an exact integer multiset for finetuning through a geometric integerization procedure. The resulting multiplicities naturally create repeated examples, which we exploit with Gradient Reuse to amortize forward-backward computation across repeated finetuning steps. Our experiments show that HullFT improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff over current state-of-the-art TTFT methods, achieving lower bits-per-byte at substantially lower total runtime.

</details>


### [358] [MATNet: Multi-Level Fusion Transformer-Based Model for Day-Ahead PV Generation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.10356)
*Matteo Tortora,Francesco Conte,Gianluca Natrella,Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MATNet，一种基于Transformer的多模态架构，用于多步提前日前PV发电功率预测。模型通过多级联合融合方法整合历史PV数据与历史及预测天气数据，采用软注意力机制。在Ausgrid数据集上，MATNet的RMSE达0.0445，相对最佳基线方法提升约65%，并在消融研究、缺失数据敏感性分析、跨站点零样本泛化评估及计算复杂度分析中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI光伏预测模型虽能捕捉数据复杂模式，但忽略了物理先验知识；而纯物理模型性能有限。亟需一种能融合多模态数据（历史PV+天气）且兼顾物理一致性与预测精度的架构，以提升光伏并网效率。

Method: 提出MATNet，一种基于Transformer的多模态架构。采用多级联合融合方法，通过软注意力机制在多个融合阶段整合历史PV数据、历史天气数据及预测天气数据，实现多步提前日前PV功率预测。

Result: 在Ausgrid基准数据集上，MATNet的RMSE为0.0445，相对最佳基线方法提升约65%。消融研究验证了各模块有效性；缺失数据敏感性分析表明模型对输入退化具有鲁棒性；跨站点零样本泛化评估在5个外部PV数据集上展现了强领域迁移能力；计算复杂度分析确认了预测精度与计算效率的良好平衡。

Conclusion: MATNet作为一种可靠高效的解决方案，能够有效促进光伏能源并网。代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of renewable generation is crucial to facilitate the integration of Renewable Energy Sources into the power system. Focusing on photovoltaic (PV) units, forecasting methods can be divided into two main categories: physics-based and data-based strategies, with Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based models providing state-of-the-art performance. However, while these AI-based models can capture complex patterns and relationships in the data, they ignore the underlying physical prior knowledge of the phenomenon. Therefore, in this paper, we propose MATNet, a novel transformer-based multimodal architecture for multi-step day-ahead PV power generation forecasting. The model is fed with historical PV data and historical and forecast weather data through a multi-level joint fusion approach, employing a soft-attention mechanism at multiple fusion stages. We evaluate the effectiveness of MATNet on the Ausgrid benchmark dataset, where it significantly outperforms various baseline models, achieving an RMSE of 0.0445, corresponding to a relative improvement of approximately 65% compared to the best-performing baseline method. The analysis is further enriched by a comprehensive set of ablation studies, a sensitivity analysis on missing data, which highlights MATNet's resilience to input degradation, a cross-site zero-shot generalization evaluation on five external PV datasets, demonstrating MATNet's robustness under significant domain shifts, and an assessment of the model's computational complexity, confirming its favorable balance between predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. These results highlight MATNet's potential as a reliable and efficient solution to facilitate the integration of PV energy into the power grid. The code is available at https://github.com/arco-group/MATNet.

</details>


### [359] [Promoting Generalization for Exact Solvers via Adversarial Instance Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.14161)
*Haoyang Liu,Yufei Kuang,Jie Wang,Xijun Li,Yongdong Zhang,Feng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaSolver，一种通过对抗性实例增强（Adversarial Instance Augmentation）来提升混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解器中学习型分支模块泛化能力的方法，将实例增强建模为上下文赌博机问题并进行对抗训练，显著提升了在未见实例上的求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的MILP求解器在训练分布有限的情况下，对未见实例（尤其是来自扰动环境的大规模实例）存在严重的性能退化问题。

Method: 将MILP实例表示为二分图，通过学习到的增强策略对图结构进行扰动以生成多样化的实例；将不可微的实例增强建模为上下文赌博机问题，并通过对抗训练联合优化求解器与增强策略，实现基于梯度的高效训练。

Result: 在多种分布上的大量实验表明，AdaSolver通过生成多样化的增强实例，显著提升了求解效率。

Conclusion: AdaSolver是首个通用的、有效的框架，用于理解和改进基于模仿学习和基于强化学习的分支定界求解器的泛化能力。

Abstract: Machine learning has been successfully applied to improve the efficiency of Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solvers. However, the learning-based solvers often suffer from severe performance degradation on unseen MILP instances -- especially on large-scale instances from a perturbed environment -- due to the limited diversity of training distributions. To tackle this problem, we propose a novel approach, which is called Adversarial Instance Augmentation and does not require to know the problem type for new instance generation, to promote data diversity for learning-based branching modules in the branch-and-bound (B&B) Solvers (AdaSolver). We use the bipartite graph representations for MILP instances and obtain various perturbed instances to regularize the solver by augmenting the graph structures with a learned augmentation policy. The major technical contribution of AdaSolver is that we formulate the non-differentiable instance augmentation as a contextual bandit problem and adversarially train the learning-based solver and augmentation policy, enabling efficient gradient-based training of the augmentation policy. To the best of our knowledge, AdaSolver is the first general and effective framework for understanding and improving the generalization of both imitation-learning-based (IL-based) and reinforcement-learning-based (RL-based) B&B solvers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that by producing various augmented instances, AdaSolver leads to a remarkable efficiency improvement across various distributions.

</details>


### [360] [Certified Causal Defense with Generalizable Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15451)
*Yiran Qiao,Yu Yin,Chen Chen,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GLEAN框架，从因果视角解决认证防御中鲁棒性跨域泛化难题，通过解耦因果特征与虚假相关，实现跨分布偏移的认证鲁棒性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证防御方法虽然能在特定分布下提供理论鲁棒性保证，但在面临分布偏移时难以泛化其认证鲁棒性，根本原因在于难以消除虚假相关在不同域中对鲁棒性的负面影响。

Method: 提出GLEAN框架，包含两个核心组件：1）可认证的因果因子学习模块，用于解耦输入与标签之间的因果关系和虚假相关；2）因果认证防御策略，针对潜在因果因子处理对抗攻击，从而排除虚假相关对防御的负面影响。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了该框架在不同数据域中认证鲁棒性泛化的优越性。

Conclusion: GLEAN框架不仅能在训练分布下抵御数据上的恶意噪声，还能将鲁棒性泛化到存在分布偏移的不同域中。

Abstract: While machine learning models have proven effective across various scenarios, it is widely acknowledged that many models are vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Recently, there have emerged numerous efforts in adversarial defense. Among them, certified defense is well known for its theoretical guarantees against arbitrary adversarial perturbations on input within a certain range (e.g., $l_2$ ball). However, most existing works in this line struggle to generalize their certified robustness in other data domains with distribution shifts. This issue is rooted in the difficulty of eliminating the negative impact of spurious correlations on robustness in different domains. To address this problem, in this work, we propose a novel certified defense framework GLEAN, which incorporates a causal perspective into the generalization problem in certified defense. More specifically, our framework integrates a certifiable causal factor learning component to disentangle the causal relations and spurious correlations between input and label, and thereby exclude the negative effect of spurious correlations on defense. On top of that, we design a causally certified defense strategy to handle adversarial attacks on latent causal factors. In this way, our framework is not only robust against malicious noises on data in the training distribution but also can generalize its robustness across domains with distribution shifts. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets validate the superiority of our framework in certified robustness generalization in different data domains. Code is available in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [361] [KAN-AD: Time Series Anomaly Detection with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00278)
*Quan Zhou,Changhua Pei,Fei Sun,Jing Han,Zhengwei Gao,Dan Pei,Haiming Zhang,Gaogang Xie,Jianhui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KAN-AD，一种基于截断傅里叶展开替代B样条的时间序列异常检测方法，通过平滑单变量函数拟合正常行为模式，在四个基准上平均提升15%检测精度，参数少于1000个，推理速度比原始KAN快50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预测模型的时间序列异常检测方法倾向于过拟合微小波动，而有效检测应聚焦于通过平滑局部模式建模"正常"行为。

Method: 将时间序列建模重构为用平滑单变量函数逼近序列，用截断傅里叶展开替换KAN中的B样条函数，引入轻量级学习机制以强调全局模式并抵抗局部扰动。

Result: 在四个流行TSAD基准上，KAN-AD平均提升15%检测精度（峰值超过27%），参数少于1000个，推理速度比原始KAN快50%。

Conclusion: KAN-AD通过全局平滑建模实现了高效、轻量且鲁棒的时间序列异常检测，验证了方法的实用性和效率。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) underpins real-time monitoring in cloud services and web systems, allowing rapid identification of anomalies to prevent costly failures. Most TSAD methods driven by forecasting models tend to overfit by emphasizing minor fluctuations. Our analysis reveals that effective TSAD should focus on modeling "normal" behavior through smooth local patterns. To achieve this, we reformulate time series modeling as approximating the series with smooth univariate functions. The local smoothness of each univariate function ensures that the fitted time series remains resilient against local disturbances. However, a direct KAN implementation proves susceptible to these disturbances due to the inherently localized characteristics of B-spline functions. We thus propose KAN-AD, replacing B-splines with truncated Fourier expansions and introducing a novel lightweight learning mechanism that emphasizes global patterns while staying robust to local disturbances. On four popular TSAD benchmarks, KAN-AD achieves an average 15% improvement in detection accuracy (with peaks exceeding 27%) over state-of-the-art baselines. Remarkably, it requires fewer than 1,000 trainable parameters, resulting in a 50% faster inference speed compared to the original KAN, demonstrating the approach's efficiency and practical viability.

</details>


### [362] [Learning Locally, Revising Globally: Global Reviser for Federated Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00452)
*Yuxin Tian,Mouxing Yang,Yuhao Zhou,Jian Wang,Qing Ye,Tongliang Liu,Gang Niu,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGR方法，利用联邦学习中全局模型对噪声标签的慢速记忆特性，通过三个模块协同纠正噪声标签并规范本地训练，有效解决联邦标签噪声问题，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习严重依赖高质量标签，而现实场景中标签噪声普遍存在，且联邦学习的异构性（不同客户端存在不同类型的标签噪声、比例和数据分布差异）进一步加剧了该问题。

Method: 提出FedGR方法，包含三个模块：(1) 利用全局模型对噪声标签的慢速记忆特性；(2) 协同纠正噪声标签；(3) 规范本地训练过程，以自包含方式提升FL对标签噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的F-LN基准测试上，FedGR在严重标签噪声和数据异构场景下均优于八种最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGR通过利用全局模型的内在特性（对噪声标签的慢速记忆），在无需外部知识的情况下有效解决了联邦标签噪声问题，为联邦学习在现实场景中的应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning (FL) heavily depends on high-quality labels, which are often impractical in the real world, leading to the federated label-noise (F-LN) problem. Worse still, the F-LN problem is exacerbated by the heterogeneity of FL, whereas clients experience different label-noise types, ratios, and data distribution. In this study, we first observe an intriguing phenomenon that the global model of FL exhibits a slow memorization of noisy labels, suggesting its ability to maintain reliable predictions and robust representations in FL. Motivated by this, we propose a novel method termed Federated Global Reviser (\method), a straightforward yet effective method comprising three modules that collaboratively rectify noisy labels and regularize local training. By exploiting this inherent property, \method\ improves the label-noise robustness of FL in a self-contained manner. Extensive experiments on three widely used F-LN benchmarks demonstrate the superior performance of FedGR, consistently outperforming eight state-of-the-art baselines even in severe label-noise and data heterogeneity. Code: https://github.com/cs-yuxintian/FedGR-ICML26

</details>


### [363] [Looking around you: external information enhances representations for event sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10205)
*Petr Sokerin,Maria Kovaleva,Ekaterina Boyarina,Pavel Tikhomirov,Denis Vorobiyov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在多个共现事件序列中聚合用户表征的方法，通过可学习的注意力机制或简单池化技术，增强特定用户的表征质量，并在多个领域数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件序列的表征学习通常独立处理每个序列，忽略了时间上共现的其他序列提供的上下文信息，这在快速变化的环境（如金融、电商）或某些序列缺乏近期事件时尤为不利。

Method: 开发了一种在多个共现事件序列设置下，从多个用户表征中聚合信息以增强特定用户表征的方法。考虑了多种聚合策略，从简单池化到可学习注意力聚合，后者能够捕捉用户间更复杂的信息流动。该方法可运行于现有编码器之上，并支持高效微调。

Result: 在9个不同领域的事件序列数据集（金融、电商、娱乐等）及下游任务上，可学习注意力机制在有无微调的情况下均提升了指标分数；均值池化虽增益较小，但也显著有效。

Conclusion: 通过聚合共现序列的用户表征可以显著提升表征质量，其中可学习注意力聚合效果最优，验证了建模序列间交互信息的价值。

Abstract: Representation learning produces models in different domains, such as store purchases, client transactions, and general people's behavior. However, such models for event sequences usually process each sequence in isolation, ignoring context from those that co-occur in time. This limitation is particularly problematic in domains with fast-evolving conditions, like finance and e-commerce, or when certain sequences lack recent events.
  We develop a method that aggregates information from multiple user representations, augmenting a specific user's representation in a setting with multiple co-occurring event sequences, achieving better quality than processing each sequence independently. Our study considers diverse aggregation approaches, ranging from simple pooling techniques to Learnable attention aggregation, that can highlight more complex information flow among other users. The proposed methods operate on top of an existing encoder and support its efficient fine-tuning. Across nine diverse event sequence datasets (finance, e-commerce, entertainment, etc.) and downstream tasks, Learnable attention improves metric scores, both with and without fine-tuning, while mean pooling yields a smaller but still significant gain.

</details>


### [364] [Diffusion-based learning framework for Constrained Nonconvex Optimization with Weighted Bootstrapped Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10330)
*Shutong Ding,Yimiao Zhou,Ke Hu,Xi Yao,Junchi Yan,Xiaoying Tang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiOpt，一种基于扩散模型的约束非凸优化框架，通过双阶段训练（监督预热+自举训练）和推理时解选择技术，解决监督扩散求解器中的分布对齐问题，实现可行域内高质量解生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的优化方法依赖监督学习，缺乏约束满足机制，导致生成解分布在可行域上概率质量低（分布对齐问题），无法满足实际应用中对约束满足的要求。

Method: 提出DiOpt框架：1) 第一阶段监督预热学习噪声到约束区域的映射；2) 第二阶段自举训练迭代精化解，改进目标函数同时保证高约束满足率；3) 推理时采用解选择技术提升最优性。

Result: 在多种非凸优化任务上，DiOpt在最优性和约束满足率两方面均优于现有方法，是首个成功将扩散求解器用于约束非凸优化的框架。

Conclusion: DiOpt通过双阶段训练和推理选择有效解决了扩散求解器在约束非凸优化中的分布对齐问题，实现了高约束满足与高最优性的统一，为扩散模型在约束优化中的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models show promising potential to accelerate nonconvex problem solving by leveraging their multimodality. However, most existing diffusion-based optimization approaches rely on supervised learning and lack a mechanism to enforce constraint satisfaction, which is required in real-world applications. In that case, we investigate and theoretically analyze the inherent problem of supervised diffusion solvers and identify the distributional misalignment problem, i.e., the generated solution distribution often exhibits low probability mass on the feasible region. To resolve this issue, we propose DiOpt, a new diffusion-based learning framework for constrained nonconvex optimization, which effectively learns the mapping from noise to the constraint region. Specifically, this framework operates in two distinct phases: an initial warm-start phase, implemented via supervised learning, followed by a bootstrapping training phase. This dual-phase architecture is designed to iteratively refine solutions, thereby improving the objective function with high constraint satisfaction. Finally, we also employ a solution selection technique in inference for better optimality. Notably, DiOpt is the first successful integration of the diffusion solver in constrained nonconvex optimization. Evaluations on diverse nonconvex tasks demonstrate the superiority of DiOpt in both optimality and constraint satisfaction. Our official page is released at https://dingsht.tech/diopt-webpage.

</details>


### [365] [A Composable Multimodal Framework for cine CMR-Text-Driven Prediction of Heart Failure Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16548)
*Jianzhou Chen,Jinyang Sun,Xiumei Wang,Xi Chen,Heyu Chu,Guo Song,Yuji Luo,Xingping Zhou,Rong Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种用于心力衰竭评估和治疗优化的可组合策略框架，该框架整合了心脏磁共振影像、结构化临床指标和非结构化文本记录等多模态数据，相比单模态AI算法在预后预测方面表现出更优的准确性，并支持更全面的患者评估和个性化治疗规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管心力衰竭治疗已取得显著进展，但由于其复杂性和多因素特征，仍存在大量未满足的临床需求。现有方法缺乏对患者数据的整体性评估，需要一种更全面的框架来整合异构数据，从而改善心衰患者的预后评估和治疗优化。

Method: 提出一个可组合的策略框架，利用多模态算法分析多种患者数据，明确整合了cine心脏磁共振序列、结构化临床指标（如实验室结果、人口统计学数据）和非结构化文本记录（如病史、处方信息），实现多源数据的融合与综合评估。

Result: 该多模态框架在心衰预后预测方面优于单模态AI算法，表现出更优的准确性；同时能够详细评估各种病理指标对心衰预后的影响。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地整合异构临床数据，该方法支持更全面的预后评估，并有助于为心衰患者制定优化、个性化的治疗方案。

Abstract: Objective. Heart failure is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, with millions of deaths each year, according to data from the World Health Organization (WHO) and other public health agencies. While significant progress has been made in the field of heart failure, leading to improved survival rates and improvement of ejection fraction, there remains substantial unmet needs, due to the complexity and multifactorial characteristics. This study aims to propose and evaluate a composable strategy framework for assessment and treatment optimization in heart failure, designed to provide more holistic patient evaluation and management. Approach. The framework leverages multi-modal algorithms to analyze a comprehensive range of patient data, explicitly integrating cine cardiac magnetic resonance (cine CMR) sequences, structured clinical metrics (e.g., lab results, demographics), and unstructured textual records (e.g., medical history, prescriptions). By integrating these various data sources, our framework offers a more holistic evaluation and optimized treatment plan for patients. Main results. The multi-modal framework demonstrates superior accuracy in HF prognosis prediction compared to single-modal AI algorithms. Additionally, it enables a detailed evaluation of the impact of various pathological indicators on HF outcomes. Significance. By integrating heterogeneous clinical data in a systematic manner, this approach supports more comprehensive prognosis assessment and facilitates optimized, personalized treatment planning for heart failure patients.

</details>


### [366] [Robust and Efficient Writer-Independent IMU-Based Handwriting Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20954)
*Jindong Li,Tim Hamann,Jens Barth,Peter Kämpf,Dario Zanca,Björn Eskofier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CNN编码器和BiLSTM解码器的模型，用于惯性测量单元(IMU)数据的手写识别(HWR)，在写者无关(WI)场景下表现出色，在公共OnHW数据集和自建词数据集上分别达到7.37%和9.44%的字符错误率(CER)，以及15.12%和32.17%的词错误率(WER)，在不同年龄组间也展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMU数据手写识别面临书写风格多变和数据集有限的挑战，现有方法在写者无关场景下对未见过的书写风格识别效果不佳，因此亟需一种鲁棒性更强的写者无关手写识别模型。

Method: 采用CNN编码器提取IMU信号的空间特征，结合BiLSTM解码器进行序列建模与识别，在模型设计中注重性能与效率的平衡。

Result: 在OnHW数据集上CER为7.37%、WER为15.12%；在自建词数据集上CER为9.44%、WER为32.17%；在句子数据集上展示了全句识别潜力；跨年龄组鲁棒性评估表明模型泛化能力优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 所提模型在写者无关IMU手写识别任务上显著优于现有方法，具备良好的跨年龄组泛化能力，为构建更适应真实应用的可扩展HWR系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Handwriting recognition (HWR) using inertial measurement unit (IMU) data remains challenging due to variations in writing styles and the limited availability of datasets. Previous approaches often struggle with handwriting from unseen writers, making writer-independent (WI) recognition a crucial yet difficult problem. This paper presents a model designed to improve WI HWR on IMU data, using a CNN encoder and BiLSTM-based decoder. Our approach demonstrates strong robustness to unseen handwriting styles, outperforming existing methods on the WI splits of both the public OnHW dataset and our word-based dataset, achieving character error rates (CERs) of 7.37% and 9.44%, and word error rates (WERs) of 15.12% and 32.17%, respectively. Robustness evaluation shows that our model maintains superior performance across different age groups, with knowledge learned from one group generalizing better to another compared to other approaches. Evaluation on our sentence-based dataset further demonstrates the potential for recognizing full sentences. Through comprehensive ablation studies, we show that our design choices achieve a strong balance between performance and efficiency. These findings support the development of more adaptable and scalable HWR systems for real-world applications.

</details>


### [367] [Offline Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning via Sequential Score Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05968)
*Dan Qiao,Wenhao Li,Shanchao Yang,Hongyuan Zha,Baoxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于序列化分数函数分解的离线多智能体强化学习方法，通过扩散模型学习联合行为策略的分数函数，并将其分解为每个智能体的正则化信号，从而解决离线MARL中由于多均衡性和异质行为数据导致的策略分布偏移问题，在多个基准测试中取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线合作式多智能体强化学习面临独特的分布偏移挑战，其根本原因在于合作任务的多均衡性导致联合行为策略空间高度多模态且行为数据质量异质，使得个体策略正则化难以对齐到一致的协调模式，引发策略分布偏移问题。

Method: 设计了一种序列化分数函数分解方法，从联合行为策略中提取每个智能体的正则化信号，在分散执行约束下实现协调的模态选择；利用基于扩散的生成模型从多模态离线数据中学习这些分数函数，并将其集成到联合动作评论家中，引导策略更新朝向高奖励、分布内的区域。

Result: 在多个粒子环境和Multi-agent MuJoCo基准测试中一致地取得了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 这是第一个明确解决离线与在线MARL之间分布差距的工作，为更通用的离线策略化MARL方法铺平了道路。

Abstract: Offline cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces unique challenges due to distributional shifts, particularly stemming from the high dimensionality of joint action spaces and the presence of out-of-distribution joint action selections. In this work, we highlight that a fundamental challenge in offline MARL arises from the multi-equilibrium nature of cooperative tasks, which induces a highly multimodal joint behavior policy space coupled with heterogeneous-quality behavior data. This makes it difficult for individual policy regularization to align with a consistent coordination pattern, leading to the policy distribution shift problems. To tackle this challenge, we design a sequential score function decomposition method that distills per-agent regularization signals from the joint behavior policy, which induces coordinated modality selection under decentralized execution constraints. Then we leverage a flexible diffusion-based generative model to learn these score functions from multimodal offline data, and integrate them into joint-action critics to guide policy updates toward high-reward, in-distribution regions under a shared team reward. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple particle environments and Multi-agent MuJoCo benchmarks consistently. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to explicitly address the distributional gap between offline and online MARL, paving the way for more generalizable offline policy-based MARL methods.

</details>


### [368] [Explaining Concept Shift with Interpretable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20634)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SGShift方法，用于在表格数据的概念漂移场景下，识别导致模型性能下降的稀疏特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念漂移（特征条件标签分布变化）会导致模型在新领域上校准失效，而识别哪些特征的关系发生了变化，对于理解领域间的科学差异（如时间、疾病状态、人群等）至关重要。

Method: 将概念漂移建模为特征选择任务，通过广义加性模型、knockoffs和吸收（absorption）等统计工具，学习能够解释源域与目标域模型性能差异的稀疏特征集。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的广泛实验表明，SGShift识别漂移特征的准确率显著优于基线方法，所需目标域样本量少，且对复杂概念漂移场景具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SGShift为表格数据中的概念漂移归因提供了一种高效、准确且样本高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept shift occurs when the distribution of labels conditioned on the features changes between domains, which can make even a well-tuned ML model miscalibrated on a new domain. Identifying these shifted features provides unique insight into how feature-label relationships differ between domains, considering the difference may be across a scientifically relevant dimension, such as time, disease status, population, etc. In this paper, we propose SGShift, a method for attributing performance degradation under concept shift in tabular data to a sparse set of shifted features. We frame concept shift as a feature selection task to learn the features that can explain performance differences between models in the source and target domain. This framework enables SGShift to adapt powerful statistical tools such as generalized additive models, knockoffs, and absorption towards identifying these shifted features. We conduct extensive experiments in synthetic and real data across various ML models and find SGShift can identify shifted features much more accurately than baseline methods, requires few samples in the shifted domain, and is robust to complex cases of concept shift.

</details>


### [369] [Accelerating Sparse Transformer Inference on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06095)
