*Wenhao Dai,Haodong Deng,Mengfei Rong,Xinyu Yang,Hongyu Liu,Fangxin Liu,Hailong Yang,Qianwen Cao,Qingxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STOF框架，通过稀疏Transformer的行/块级核映射和下游算子的两阶段编译搜索，实现GPU上的灵活掩码与算子融合，MHA加速1.6倍，端到端推理加速1.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏Transformer研究缺乏性能优化，静态算子融合方案无法适应多样化场景，亟需针对稀疏Transformer的GPU优化框架。

Method: 1) MHA部分：根据分析建模将计算映射到行式或块式核，配合独特存储格式；2) 下游算子：将融合方案映射到编译模板，通过两阶段搜索确定最优运行配置。

Result: 相比最先进工作，STOF在MHA计算上实现1.6倍加速，端到端推理实现1.4倍加速。

Conclusion: STOF通过灵活掩码与算子融合显著提升了稀疏Transformer在GPU上的性能，验证了分析建模与两阶段搜索相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are popular around the world due to their powerful understanding capabilities. As the core component of LLMs, accelerating Transformer through parallelization has gradually become a hot research topic. Mask layers introduce sparsity into Transformer to reduce calculations. However, previous works rarely focus on the performance optimization of sparse Transformer. In addition, current static operator fusion schemes fail to adapt to diverse application scenarios. To address the above problems, we propose STOF, a framework that incorporates optimizations for Sparse Transformer that enables flexible masking and Operator Fusion on GPU. For multi-head attention (MHA) structure, STOF maps the computation to row-wise or blockwise kernels with unique storage formats according to analytical modeling. For downstream operators, STOF maps the fusion scheme to compilation templates and determines the optimal running configuration through two-stage searching. The experimental results show that compared to the stateof-the-art work, STOF achieves maximum speedups of 1.6x in MHA computation and 1.4x in end-to-end inference.

</details>


### [370] [TrojanTO: Action-Level Backdoor Attacks against Trajectory Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12815)
*Yang Dai,Oubo Ma,Longfei Zhang,Xingxing Liang,Xiaochun Cao,Shouling Ji,Jiaheng Zhang,Jincai Huang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrojanTO，首个针对轨迹优化（TO）模型的动作级后门攻击方法，通过交替训练、轨迹过滤和批量投毒实现高效、隐蔽的攻击，仅需0.3%轨迹即可成功植入后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后门攻击依赖奖励操纵，对具有序列建模特性的TO模型效果不佳；高维动作空间也增加了动作操纵的难度。TO模型在后门攻击下的脆弱性尚未被充分研究。

Method: TrojanTO采用交替训练增强触发器与目标动作的关联性；通过轨迹过滤精确投毒以保持正常性能，利用批量投毒保证触发器一致性。

Result: 在多种任务和攻击目标下，仅需0.3%轨迹即可有效植入后门攻击；在DT、GDT、DC等多种TO模型架构上均表现出广泛适用性。

Conclusion: TrojanTO是首个面向TO模型的动作级后门攻击方法，兼具高效性、隐蔽性和可扩展性，揭示了TO模型在安全方面的脆弱性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Trajectory Optimization (TO) models have achieved remarkable success in offline reinforcement learning. However, their vulnerabilities against backdoor attacks are poorly understood. We find that existing backdoor attacks in reinforcement learning are based on reward manipulation, which are largely ineffective against the TO model due to its inherent sequence modeling nature. Moreover, the complexities introduced by high-dimensional action spaces further compound the challenge of action manipulation. To address these gaps, we propose TrojanTO, the first action-level backdoor attack against TO models. TrojanTO employs alternating training to enhance the connection between triggers and target actions for attack effectiveness. To improve attack stealth, it utilizes precise poisoning via trajectory filtering for normal performance and batch poisoning for trigger consistency. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TrojanTO effectively implants backdoor attacks across diverse tasks and attack objectives with a low attack budget (0.3\% of trajectories). Furthermore, TrojanTO exhibits broad applicability to DT, GDT, and DC, underscoring its scalability across diverse TO model architectures.

</details>


### [371] [Solved in Unit Domain: JacobiNet for Differentiable Coordinate-Transformed PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02537)
*Xi Chen,Jianchuan Yang,Junjie Zhang,Runnan Yang,Xu Liu,Hong Wang,Tinghui Zheng,Ziyu Ren,Wenqi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JacobiNet是一种端到端可微分的坐标变换PINN框架，通过自动微分实现雅可比矩阵直接计算，解决不规则边界域中PINN的不稳定性和收敛缓慢问题，在多种PDE上平均精度提升15.6倍，且在血管状域中实现毫秒级映射推理和超过10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在不规则边界域上存在三个核心问题：几何各向异性导致的归一化不一致、边界条件施加不精确、损失项竞争失衡。现有映射方法依赖特定案例网格、在预定点上定义雅可比、通过链式法则重构PDE，与现代自动微分和张量框架不兼容。

Method: 提出JacobiNet，一种基于学习的坐标变换PINN框架，将域映射和PDE求解统一在端到端可微分架构中。利用自动微分直接计算雅可比矩阵，与下游PINN共享计算图，无需特定案例网格、显式雅可比计算/存储及手动PDE重构，同时支持几何编辑操作。

Result: 在多种PDE上，相对L2误差从0.11-0.73降至0.01-0.09，平均精度提升15.6倍。在变形状血管状域中，对未见几何形状实现毫秒级映射推理，预测精度平均提升3.65倍，速度提升超10倍。

Conclusion: JacobiNet通过将物理建模与几何复杂性分离，有效解决了原始各向异性坐标中的归一化问题、促进了边界条件的硬施加、缓解了损失项长期存在的失衡问题，展现出强泛化能力、高精度和高效率。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a powerful framework for solving PDEs by embedding physical laws into the learning process. However, when applied to domains with irregular boundaries, PINNs often suffer from instability and slow convergence, which stems from (1) inconsistent normalization due to geometric anisotropy, (2) inaccurate boundary enforcement, and (3) imbalanced loss term competition. A common workaround is to map the domain to a regular space. Yet, conventional mapping methods rely on case-specific meshes, define Jacobians at pre-specified fixed nodes, reformulate PDEs via the chain rule-making them incompatible with modern automatic differentiation, tensor-based frameworks. To bridge this gap, we propose JacobiNet, a learning-based coordinate-transformed PINN framework that unifies domain mapping and PDE solving within an end-to-end differentiable architecture. JacobiNet enables direct Jacobian computation via autograd, shares computation graph with downstream PINNs, thereby avoiding case-specific meshing, explicit Jacobian computation/storage, and manual PDE reformulation while unlocking geometric-editing operations. Separating physical modeling from geometric complexity, JacobiNet (1) addresses normalization challenges in the original anisotropic coordinates, (2) facilitates the hard enforcement of boundary conditions, and (3) mitigates the long-standing imbalance among loss terms. Evaluated on various PDEs, JacobiNet reduces the relative L2 error from 0.11-0.73 to 0.01-0.09, achieving an average 15.6x improvement in accuracy. In vessel-like domains with varying shapes, JacobiNet enables millisecond-level mapping inference for unseen geometries, improves prediction accuracy by an average of 3.65x, while delivering over 10x speedup-demonstrating strong generalization, accuracy, and efficiency.

</details>


### [372] [Multi-level Collaborative Distillation Meets Global Workspace Model: A Unified Framework for OCIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08677)
*Shibin Su,Guoqiang Liang,De Cheng,Shizhou Zhang,Lingyan Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于全局工作空间理论（GWT）的增量学习方法，通过构建全局工作空间模型（GWM）作为共享隐式记忆，融合多个学生模型参数，并采用多层级协作蒸馏机制，在在线类增量学习（OCIL）中有效平衡稳定性与可塑性，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线类增量学习（OCIL）面临非独立同分布数据流、样本仅能观察一次等严格约束，加剧了稳定性与可塑性之间的平衡难题；同时，真实场景中的内存缓冲区限制使得现有基于回放的方法效果不佳，而集成方法虽能提升可塑性，但稳定性不足。

Method: 1）受全局工作空间理论（GWT）启发，构建全局工作空间模型（GWM），通过在每个训练批次中融合多个学生模型的参数形成共享隐式记忆，捕获历史学习轨迹；2）GWM周期性地重新分发给学生模型，类似GWT的广播机制，稳定学习并促进跨任务一致性；3）提出多层级协作蒸馏机制，强制学生间保持对等一致性，并通过每个学生与GWM的对齐保留历史知识。

Result: 在三个标准OCIL基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在不同内存预算下为多种OCIL模型带来了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过GWM共享隐式记忆与多层级协作蒸馏，学生模型既能适应新任务又能保持已有知识，在稳定性与可塑性之间取得了更好的平衡，有效解决了OCIL中的核心挑战。

Abstract: Online Class-Incremental Learning (OCIL) enables models to learn continuously from non-i.i.d. data streams. Since samples of the data streams can be seen only once, it is more suitable for real-world scenarios compared to offline learning. However, this constraint intensifies the challenge for OCIL in maintaining an appropriate balance between stability and plasticity. Moreover, under stricter memory buffer constraints in real world, current replay-based methods are less effective. While ensemble methods improve plasticity, they often struggle with stability. Inspired by the Global Workspace Theory (GWT), we propose a novel approach that enhances ensemble learning through a Global Workspace Model (GWM)-a shared, implicit memory that guides the learning of multiple student models. The GWM is formed by fusing the parameters of all students within each training batch, capturing the historical learning trajectory and serving as a dynamic anchor for knowledge consolidation. Like the broadcasting mechanism of GWT, the GWM is redistributed periodically to students, stabilizing learning and promoting cross-task consistency. In addition, we introduce a multi-level collaborative distillation mechanism. It enforces peer-to-peer consistency among students and preserves historical knowledge by aligning each student with the GWM. As a result, student models remain adaptable to new tasks while maintaining previously learned knowledge, striking a better balance between stability and plasticity. Extensive experiments on three standard OCIL benchmarks show that our method delivers significant performance improvement for several OCIL models across various memory budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/susususushi/GWM.

</details>


### [373] [Prescribe-then-Select: Adaptive Policy Selection for Contextual Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.08194)
*Caio de Prospero Iglesias,Kimberly Villalobos Carballo,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prescribe-then-Select (PS)框架，先构建可行候选策略库，再学习元策略根据协变量选择最优策略，在情境随机优化中优于单一最佳策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在情境随机优化中，多种候选策略在协变量空间上表现异质，没有任何单一策略能全局占优，需要根据具体情境动态选择最优策略。

Method: PS框架分为两步：1) 构建可行候选策略库；2) 使用基于交叉验证训练的最优策略树集成学习元策略，完全数据驱动地选择策略。

Result: 在单阶段报童问题和两阶段运输计划两个基准问题上，PS在协变量空间异质区域持续优于最佳单一策略，在无异质性时收敛于占优策略。

Conclusion: PS框架是一种模块化、数据驱动的策略选择方法，能有效处理CSO中策略性能异质性问题，提升决策质量。

Abstract: We address the problem of policy selection in contextual stochastic optimization (CSO), where covariates are available as contextual information and decisions must satisfy hard feasibility constraints. In many CSO settings, multiple candidate policies--arising from different modeling paradigms--exhibit heterogeneous performance across the covariate space, with no single policy uniformly dominating. We propose Prescribe-then-Select (PS), a modular framework that first constructs a library of feasible candidate policies and then learns a meta-policy to select the best policy for the observed covariates. We implement the meta-policy using ensembles of Optimal Policy Trees trained via cross-validation on the training set, making policy choice entirely data-driven. Across two benchmark CSO problems--single-stage newsvendor and two-stage shipment planning--PS consistently outperforms the best single policy in heterogeneous regimes of the covariate space and converges to the dominant policy when such heterogeneity is absent. All the code to reproduce the results can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Prescribe-then-Select-TMLR.

</details>


### [374] [GRPO is Secretly a Process Reward Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21154)
*Michael Sullivan,Alexander Koller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明，使用结果奖励模型(ORM)的GRPO强化学习算法实际上等价于使用蒙特卡洛过程奖励模型(PRM)的RL目标。基于这一发现，作者识别出GRPO目标在处理不平衡过程步骤和奖励时存在缺陷，并提出λ-GRPO简单修正方案。实验表明，λ-GRPO在推理任务上优于标准GRPO，且训练成本几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示GRPO算法中隐含的PRM结构，并利用这一结构在不引入显式PRM的情况下提升模型性能，同时指出并修复GRPO目标函数中存在的影响探索与利用的缺陷。

Method: 1) 理论证明ORM与PRM在GRPO框架下的等价性；2) 识别GRPO目标在处理不平衡步骤和奖励时的缺陷；3) 提出λ-GRPO修正算法，通过简单修改来缓解该缺陷。

Result: λ-GRPO训练的LLM在下游推理任务上优于标准GRPO训练的LLM，且达到峰值性能的速度更快，训练时间和成本几乎不受影响。

Conclusion: 可以利用标准GRPO算法中隐藏的内置PRM结构来提升模型性能，无需显式使用PRM，且对训练效率影响极小。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) allow for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning (RL), and seemingly contrast with outcome reward models (ORMs), which assign a single reward to an entire trajectory. However, we provide theoretical proof in this work that the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) RL algorithm equipped with an ORM is in fact equivalent to a PRM-aware RL objective equipped with a non-trivial, Monte-Carlo-based PRM (given mild assumptions). Leveraging the framework of GRPO-as-a-PRM, we identify a flaw in the GRPO objective that interacts with imbalanced process steps and rewards to hinder both exploration and exploitation (under different conditions). We propose a simple modification to the algorithm to mitigate this defect ($λ$-GRPO), and show that LLMs tuned with $λ$-GRPO outperform LLMs tuned with standard GRPO on downstream reasoning tasks\textemdash and reach peak performance more rapidly. These results show that we can leverage the hidden, built-in PRM structure within the vanilla GRPO algorithm to boost model performance without employing an explicit PRM, and with a negligible impact on training time and cost.

</details>


### [375] [Towards Foundation Models for Zero-Shot Time Series Anomaly Detection: Leveraging Synthetic Data and Relative Context Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21190)
*Tian Lan,Hao Duong Le,Jinbo Li,Wenjun He,Meng Wang,Chenghao Liu,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeRCD 是一种基于相对上下文差异（RCD）预训练范式的时间序列异常检测基础模型，通过检测相邻时间窗口间的显著差异来识别异常，在零样本场景下显著优于现有重建式基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测基础模型主要依赖重建式目标，存在根本性目标失配问题：难以识别微妙异常，同时常将复杂正常模式误判为异常，导致高假阴性和假阳性率。

Method: 提出 RCD 预训练范式，模型不学习重建输入，而是显式训练检测相邻时间窗口间的相对上下文差异；采用标准 Transformer 架构实现；构建大规模多样化合成语料库，提供 token 级异常标签作为监督信号。

Result: 在零样本 TSAD 任务中，TimeRCD 在多个多样化数据集上显著优于现有通用和异常检测专用基础模型。

Conclusion: 验证了 RCD 范式的优越性，为构建鲁棒且泛化性强的时间序列异常检测基础模型建立了新的有效路径。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) is a critical task, but developing models that generalize to unseen data in a zero-shot manner remains a major challenge. Prevailing foundation models for TSAD predominantly rely on reconstruction-based objectives, which suffer from a fundamental objective mismatch: they struggle to identify subtle anomalies while often misinterpreting complex normal patterns, leading to high rates of false negatives and positives. To overcome these limitations, we introduce \texttt{TimeRCD}, a novel foundation model for TSAD built upon a new pre-training paradigm: Relative Context Discrepancy (RCD). Instead of learning to reconstruct inputs, \texttt{TimeRCD} is explicitly trained to identify anomalies by detecting significant discrepancies between adjacent time windows. This relational approach, implemented with a standard Transformer architecture, enables the model to capture contextual shifts indicative of anomalies that reconstruction-based methods often miss. To facilitate this paradigm, we develop a large-scale, diverse synthetic corpus with token-level anomaly labels, providing the rich supervisory signal necessary for effective pre-training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \texttt{TimeRCD} significantly outperforms existing general-purpose and anomaly-specific foundation models in zero-shot TSAD across diverse datasets. Our results validate the superiority of the RCD paradigm and establish a new, effective path toward building robust and generalizable foundation models for time series anomaly detection.

</details>


### [376] [In-Place Feedback: Reliable Refinement for Multi-Turn Expert-LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00777)
*Youngbin Choi,Minjong Lee,Saemi Moon,Seunghyuk Cho,Chaehyeon Chung,MoonJeong Park,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"原位反馈"交互范式，让用户直接编辑LLM的上次回复并继续生成，相比标准多轮反馈在推理任务中更准确、更省token，专家用户满意度更高、疲劳度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的草稿常包含细微事实或逻辑错误，而现有模型难以可靠地整合旨在修复这些错误的多轮反馈。

Method: 提出原位反馈（in-place feedback）交互范式：用户直接编辑模型的上一次回复，模型从编辑后的上下文继续生成。

Result: 在五个推理密集型基准上，原位反馈一致优于标准多轮反馈且需更少token；细粒度分析表明其更可靠地应用修正并将修正传播到后续推理；专家用户研究报告了更高的最终输出满意度和更低的疲劳度，混合策略在所有维度上得分最高。

Conclusion: 直接编辑错误是专家与LLM协作的更有效范式。

Abstract: LLM-generated drafts often contain subtle factual or logical errors, yet prior work shows that models struggle to reliably integrate multi-turn feedback aimed at fixing them. We propose in-place feedback, an interaction paradigm in which the user directly edits the model's previous response and the model continues generation from the edited context. In-place feedback consistently outperforms standard multi-turn feedback across five reasoning-intensive benchmarks while requiring fewer tokens, and our fine-grained analysis shows that it applies corrections more reliably and propagates them to subsequent reasoning. A user study with domain experts refining LLM-generated summaries corroborates these findings: participants report higher final-output satisfaction and substantially lower fatigue with in-place feedback, and a mixed strategy combining in-place and multi-turn feedback scores highest on every measured dimension. These results suggest that editing errors directly is a more effective paradigm for expert-LLM collaboration.

</details>


### [377] [Distributional Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03013)
*Feiyang Wu,Ye Zhao,Anqi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分布化离线逆强化学习框架，通过最小化一阶随机占优（FSD）违反并整合扭曲风险度量（DRM），联合建模奖励函数和回报分布，实现奖励分布恢复与分布感知策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线IRL方法仅恢复确定性奖励估计或匹配期望回报，无法捕捉专家行为中的更丰富结构（如奖励分布不确定性），限制了行为分析与风险感知模仿学习的应用。

Method: 构建分布化框架联合建模奖励函数与回报分布的完整分布；通过最小化一阶随机占优（FSD）违反来学习奖励分布；将扭曲风险度量（DRM）整合到策略学习中，实现分布感知策略。

Result: 理论层面证明算法具有$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$的迭代收敛复杂度；在合成基准、真实神经行为数据和MuJoCo控制任务上，方法恢复了富有表达力的奖励表征，并达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该分布化IRL框架有效解决了传统方法在奖励分布不确定性建模上的不足，尤其适用于行为分析与风险感知的模仿学习场景。

Abstract: We propose a distributional framework for offline Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) that jointly models uncertainty over reward functions and full distributions of returns. Unlike conventional IRL approaches that recover a deterministic reward estimate or match only expected returns, our method captures richer structure in expert behavior, particularly in learning the reward distribution, by minimizing first-order stochastic dominance (FSD) violations and thus integrating distortion risk measures (DRMs) into policy learning, enabling the recovery of both reward distributions and distribution-aware policies. This formulation is well-suited for behavior analysis and risk-aware imitation learning. Theoretical analysis shows that the algorithm converges with $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iteration complexity. Empirical results on synthetic benchmarks, real-world neurobehavioral data, and MuJoCo control tasks demonstrate that our method recovers expressive reward representations and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [378] [Provable Affine Identifiability of Nonlinear CCA under Latent Distributional Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04758)
*Zhiwei Han,Stefan Matthes,Hao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了非线性典型相关分析（CCA）在特定分布先验下能够恢复真实潜在因子（限于仿射变换）的充分条件，证明了白化的必要性，以及岭正则化经验CCA在有限样本下收敛于总体解，为相关非对比学习方法提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为非线性CCA恢复真实潜在因子的能力建立严格的充分条件，并桥接理论与实践之间的差距，解释基于相关性的非对比学习方法的经验成功。

Method: 将分析从观测空间迁移到源空间，扩展双变量分布正交多项式展开的经典统计结果到表示学习领域；理论证明白化的必要性以及岭正则化经验CCA的有限样本收敛性。

Result: 证明了非线性CCA在特定分布先验下能够实现仿射可识别性；白化对于保证学习映射的有界性和良态是严格必要的；岭正则化经验CCA在有限样本下收敛于其总体对应解。

Conclusion: 为非线性CCA和基于相关性的非对比学习方法提供了严格的理论基础，并通过合成数据与渲染图像实验验证了理论预测的恢复行为及假设违反时的失败模式。

Abstract: In this work, we establish the sufficient conditions under which nonlinear Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA) recovers ground-truth latent factors up to an affine transformation. By transporting the analysis from the observation space to the source space, we extend classical statistical results on orthogonal polynomial expansions of bivariate distributions to representation learning, proving affine identifiability under specific distributional priors. We formally demonstrate that whitening is strictly necessary to ensure the boundedness and well-conditioning of the learned mappings. Furthermore, we bridge the gap between theory and practice by proving that ridge-regularized empirical CCA converges to its population counterpart in the finite-sample regime. Finally, our findings provide a rigorous theoretical foundation explaining the empirical success of recent correlation-based non-contrastive learning methods. Experiments on synthetic and rendered image datasets, alongside systematic ablations, validate the predicted recovery behavior and illustrate the failure modes that arise when the assumptions are violated.

</details>


### [379] [The Impact of Semantic Pairs on Self-Supervised Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08722)
*Mohammad Alkhalefi,Georgios Leontidis,Mingjun Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验研究语义正样本对（同一类别的不同实例）在自监督表示学习中的作用。在ImageNet-1K上构建匹配的增强正样本对和人工筛选的语义正样本对数据集，对比多种SSL方法，发现语义正样本对训练在迁移学习和目标检测任务上持续优于增强正样本对训练，其中对比学习方法（尤其是SimCLR）受益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法通常使用同一图像的不同增强视图作为正样本对，但这会保留背景、纹理、光照等无关相关性。语义正样本对（同一类别的不同实例）有望通过呈现跨不同上下文的物体来减少这些相关性，但以往研究常将语义对与增强正样本或错误邻居混合，难以孤立评估语义配对的效果。

Method: 从ImageNet-1K构建两个匹配的子集：增强正样本对基线数据集和人工筛选的语义正样本对数据集（保持类别组成和训练对数量相同）。在匹配的训练条件下，比较多种对比学习（SimCLR等）和非对比学习（BYOL等）SSL方法。

Result: 语义正样本对预训练在迁移学习和目标检测评估上持续优于增强正样本对预训练。消融实验表明语义对能诱导超越标准变换流水线的不变性。在所评估的方法中，对比学习从语义对中获益最大，SimCLR的相对提升最显著。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了语义正样本对在自监督学习中的作用，为选择和设计能有效利用语义对信息的框架提供了指导。

Abstract: Instance discrimination learns visual representations by treating different augmented views of the same image as positive pairs. While this encourages invariance to handcrafted transformations, same-image positives can preserve nuisance correlations such as background, texture, illumination, and object-specific details. Semantic positive pairs, i.e., different same-class instances, may reduce these correlations by presenting objects across diverse contexts. However, previous studies often combine semantic pairs with augmented positives or false neighbors (i.e., incorrectly mapped semantic pairs), making it difficult to isolate the effect of semantic pairing. We present a controlled empirical study of semantic positive pairs for self-supervised representation learning. From ImageNet-1K, we construct two matched subsets: an augmented-pair baseline and a manually curated semantic-pair dataset with the same class composition and training-pair count. We use these datasets to compare representative contrastive and non-contrastive SSL methods under matched training conditions. Across transfer learning and object detection evaluations, semantic-pair pretraining consistently improves generalisation over augmented-pair pretraining. Additional ablations show that semantic pairs induce invariances beyond the standard transformation pipeline. Among the evaluated methods, contrastive learning benefits most strongly from semantic pairs, with SimCLR showing the largest relative improvement. These results clarify the role of semantic positive pairs in SSL and provide guidance for selecting and designing frameworks that can exploit semantic pair information effectively

</details>


### [380] [Offline Reinforcement Learning with Generative Trajectory Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11499)
*Xinsong Feng,Leshu Tang,Chenan Wang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成轨迹策略（GTPs），一种基于连续时间ODE统一视角的离线强化学习新范式，通过学习ODE的完整解映射来弥合扩散策略（慢但高性能）与一致性策略（快但低性能）之间的差距，在D4RL基准测试中达到SOTA，并在多个困难AntMaze任务上取得完美分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式策略在离线RL中面临两难困境：扩散策略等迭代模型计算昂贵，而一致性策略等单步模型性能退化。作者希望弥合这一差距，在保持高性能的同时提升计算效率。

Method: 提出统一视角，将扩散、流匹配和一致性模型视为学习由ODE控制的连续时间生成轨迹的特例；在此基础上提出GTP，学习ODE的完整解映射；引入两项理论驱动的适应性改进使其适配离线RL。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试上取得SOTA结果，显著优于之前的生成式策略，在多个困难的AntMaze任务上取得完美分数。

Conclusion: 通过ODE统一视角可以设计出同时兼顾性能与效率的生成式策略，GTP验证了这一方向的可行性。

Abstract: Generative models have emerged as a powerful class of policies for offline reinforcement learning (RL) due to their ability to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. However, existing methods face a stark trade-off: slow, iterative models like diffusion policies are computationally expensive, while fast, single-step models like consistency policies often suffer from degraded performance. In this paper, we demonstrate that it is possible to bridge this gap. The key to moving beyond the limitations of individual methods, we argue, lies in a unifying perspective that views modern generative models, including diffusion, flow matching, and consistency models, as specific instances of learning a continuous-time generative trajectory governed by an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE). This principled foundation provides a clearer design space for generative policies in RL and allows us to propose Generative Trajectory Policies (GTPs), a new and more general policy paradigm that learns the entire solution map of the underlying ODE. To make this paradigm practical for offline RL, we further introduce two key theoretically principled adaptations. Empirical results demonstrate that GTP achieves state-of-the-art performance on D4RL benchmarks - it significantly outperforms prior generative policies, achieving perfect scores on several notoriously hard AntMaze tasks.

</details>


### [381] [Beyond Accuracy: Are Time Series Foundation Models Well-Calibrated?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16060)
*Coen Adler,Yuxin Chang,Felix Draxler,Samar Abdi,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五个时间序列基础模型和两个基线模型的校准特性，发现基础模型在校准性能上 consistently 优于基线模型，且不会表现出系统性过度自信或信心不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管时间序列基础模型在预测性能上取得了最先进成果，但其校准特性（即模型置信度与真实正确率之间的匹配程度）尚未得到充分研究，而校准对于许多实际应用至关重要。

Method: 对五个近期时间序列基础模型和两个竞争基线进行系统性评估，包括模型校准（过度自信或信心不足）、不同预测头的影响以及长期自回归预测下的校准表现。

Result: 时间序列基础模型在校准方面 consistently 优于基线模型，且不会出现系统性过度自信或信心不足，这与许多其他深度学习模型常见的过度自信现象形成对比。

Conclusion: 时间序列基础模型不仅预测性能优越，其校准特性也更好，更适合应用于对置信度可靠性有要求的实际场景。

Abstract: The recent development of foundation models for time series data has generated considerable interest in using such models across a variety of applications. Although foundation models achieve state-of-the-art predictive performance, their calibration properties remain relatively underexplored, despite the fact that calibration can be critical for many practical applications. In this paper, we investigate the calibration-related properties of five recent time series foundation models and two competitive baselines. We perform a series of systematic evaluations assessing model calibration (i.e., over- or under-confidence), effects of varying prediction heads, and calibration under long-term autoregressive forecasting. We find that time series foundation models are consistently better calibrated than baseline models and tend not to be either systematically over- or under-confident, in contrast to the overconfidence often seen in other deep learning models.

</details>


### [382] [Leak@$k$: Unlearning Does Not Make LLMs Forget Under Probabilistic Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04934)
*Hadi Reisizadeh,Jiajun Ruan,Yiwei Chen,Soumyadeep Pal,Sijia Liu,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有的大语言模型"遗忘"方法在概率解码下会泄露被删除的敏感信息。本文提出leak@k评估指标和RULE算法，有效解决了此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前几乎所有LLM遗忘方法在确定性（贪婪）解码评估下看似成功，但在实际使用的概率解码下，被"遗忘"的敏感信息会可靠地重新出现，缺乏真正的遗忘保障。

Method: 1) 提出leak@k元评估指标，量化在生成k个样本时遗忘知识重新出现的可能性；2) 在TOFU、MUSE、WMDP三个基准上首次大规模系统研究遗忘可靠性；3) 提出RULE算法（Robust Unlearning under LEak@k metric），通过优化leak@k指标实现鲁棒遗忘。

Result: 知识泄露在现有方法和任务中普遍存在，当前SOTA遗忘技术仅提供有限的遗忘效果。RULE在TOFU基准上实现了大量生成样本下零泄露，在MUSE基准上在多数采样预算k下优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 现有LLM遗忘方法存在根本性缺陷，需要以leak@k为代表的概率解码下的评估标准；RULE算法能有效缓解此问题，但更鲁棒的遗忘仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Unlearning in large language models (LLMs) is critical for regulatory compliance and for building ethical generative AI systems that avoid producing private, toxic, illegal, or copyrighted content. Despite rapid progress, in this work, we show that \textit{almost all} existing unlearning methods fail to achieve true forgetting in practice. Specifically, while evaluations of these `unlearned' models under deterministic (greedy) decoding often suggest successful knowledge removal using standard benchmarks, we show that sensitive information reliably resurfaces when models are sampled with standard probabilistic decoding. To rigorously capture this vulnerability, we introduce \texttt{leak@$k$}, a new meta-evaluation metric that quantifies the likelihood of forgotten knowledge reappearing when generating $k$ samples from the model under realistic decoding strategies. Using three widely adopted benchmarks, TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP, we conduct the first large-scale, systematic study of unlearning reliability using \texttt{leak@$k$} metric. Our findings demonstrate that knowledge leakage persists across methods and tasks, underscoring that current state-of-the-art (SOTA) unlearning techniques provide only limited forgetting. We propose an algorithm, termed Robust Unlearning under LEak@$k$ metric (\texttt{RULE}) to address this concern. We demonstrate that \texttt{RULE} provides an unlearned model for TOFU benchmark with no information leakage for a large number of generation samples. On the MUSE benchmark, \texttt{RULE} outperforms SOTA unlearning methods under the \texttt{leak@$k$} metric across most sampling budgets $k$. Codes are available at https://github.com/OptimAI-Lab/Leak-k.

</details>


### [383] [DiScoFormer: Plug-In Density and Score Estimation with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05924)
*Vasily Ilin,Peter Sushko,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiScoFormer，一个可一次训练、任意推理的等变Transformer模型，能够从i.i.d.样本中同时估计概率密度及其得分，在分布和样本规模上具有泛化能力；理论上证明自注意力可恢复归一化KDE，实验上展示其比KDE更快收敛、精度更高，并可作为高分真度得分用于多种下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密度和得分估计方法存在分岔：经典KDE可跨分布泛化但受维数灾难困扰，现代神经得分模型精度高但需为每个目标分布重新训练。缺乏一个既能跨分布泛化又无需重复训练的通用估计器。

Method: 提出DiSFormer（Density and Score Transformer），一种等变Transformer架构，采用"一次训练、任意推理"范式，直接将i.i.d.样本映射到密度值和得分向量。理论上证明自注意力机制可恢复归一化KDE，注意力头学会多尺度、类核的行为。

Result: DiSFormer比KDE收敛更快、密度估计精度更高；在得分去偏KDE、Fisher信息计算和Fokker-Planck型PDE等下游任务中可作为高保真度的即插即用得分解算器。

Conclusion: 自注意力机制是核方法的函数泛化，DiSFormer弥合了经典KDE与现代神经方法的差距，实现了跨分布泛化与高精度的统一。

Abstract: Estimating probability density and its score from samples remains a core problem in generative modeling, Bayesian inference, and kinetic theory. Existing methods are bifurcated: classical kernel density estimators (KDE) generalize across distributions but suffer from the curse of dimensionality, while modern neural score models achieve high precision but require retraining for every target distribution. We introduce DiScoFormer (Density and Score Transformer), a ``train-once, infer-anywhere" equivariant Transformer that maps i.i.d. samples to both density values and score vectors, generalizing across distributions and sample sizes. Analytically, we prove that self-attention can recover normalized KDE, establishing it as a functional generalization of kernel methods; empirically, individual attention heads learn multi-scale, kernel-like behaviors. The model converges faster and achieves higher precision than KDE for density estimation, and provides a high-fidelity plug-in score oracle for score-debiased KDE, Fisher information computation, and Fokker-Planck-type PDEs.

</details>


### [384] [FarSkip-Collective: Unhobbling Blocking Communication in Mixture of Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11505)
*Yonatan Dukler,Guihong Li,Deval Shah,Jiang Liu,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FarSkip-Collective通过修改MoE模型架构跳过连接，实现计算与通信的重叠，在16B-109B规模模型上保持精度（如Llama 4 Scout精度偏差<1%），并在推理和训练中分别实现32.6%的TTFT加速和88.9%的通信重叠率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式环境中MoE模型的阻塞通信是制约其高效运行的主要瓶颈，现有方法难以在不影响模型性能的前提下实现计算与通信的有效重叠。

Method: 修改模型架构跳过连接（Skip Connections），结合自蒸馏技术对模型进行转换，使通信与计算可重叠；并通过优化实现显式重叠通信与计算。

Result: 1）Llama 4 Scout (109B)转换后精度与指令微调版本偏差<1%；2）DeepSeek-V3推理时TTFT加速32.6%，预填充阶段通信-计算重叠率达97.3%；3）训练时all-to-all通信重叠率达88.9%。

Conclusion: FarSkip-Collective通过架构修改有效解决了MoE分布式推理和训练中的通信瓶颈，在不牺牲模型能力的前提下显著提升效率。

Abstract: Blocking communication presents a major hurdle in running MoEs efficiently in distributed settings. To address this, we present FarSkip-Collective which modifies the architecture of modern models to enable overlapping of their computation with communication. Our approach modifies the architecture to skip connections in the model and it is unclear a priori whether the modified model architecture can remain as capable, especially for large state-of-the-art models and while modifying all of the model layers. We answer this question in the affirmative and fully convert a series of state-of-the-art models varying from 16B to 109B parameters to enable overlapping of their communication while achieving accuracy that is comparable with their original open-source releases. For example, we convert Llama 4 Scout (109B) via self-distillation and achieve average accuracy within 1% of its instruction tuned release averaged over a wide range of downstream evaluations. In addition to demonstrating retained accuracy of the large modified models, we realize the benefits of FarSkip-Collective through optimized implementations that explicitly overlap communication with computation, accelerating both training and inference in existing frameworks. For inference, we demonstrate 32.6% speedup in Time To First Token when serving a converted DeepSeek-V3 architecture with expert parallelism in SGLang and achieve 97.3% communication-computation overlap during the prefill stage. During training, our approach enables 88.9% communication overlap of the all-to-all communication collectives when pre-training DeepSeek-V3 MoE layers with expert parallelism.

</details>


### [385] [MiAD: Mirage Atom Diffusion for De Novo Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14426)
*Andrey Okhotin,Maksim Nakhodnov,Nikita Kazeev,Mikhail Lazarev,Andrey E Ustyuzhanin,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“幻影注入”（mirage infusion）的简单技术，使扩散模型能够在晶体生成过程中改变原子数量（从存在到不存在或反之），基于此技术开发的MiAD模型在MP-20数据集上实现了8.2%的S.U.N.率，较现有最先进方法有显著提升，模型质量提升最高达2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的晶体生成方法大多无法在生成过程中改变晶体中的原子数量，这限制了模型采样轨迹的多样性和生成质量。作者旨在解决这一限制，提升扩散模型在晶体材料生成中的表现。

Method: 提出“幻影注入”（mirage infusion）技术，使扩散模型能够将原子状态在“存在”与“不存在”（幻影）之间切换。基于此构建了MiAD（Mirage Atom Diffusion），一个等变联合扩散模型，用于从头晶体生成，能够在生成过程中动态改变原子数量。

Result: 与未修改的相同模型相比，模型质量提升最高达2.5倍。MiAD在MP-20数据集上实现了8.2%的S.U.N.（同时稳定、独特且新颖）率，大幅超过现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 幻影注入是一种简单但有效的技术，使扩散模型具备原子数量变化的灵活性，显著提升了晶体生成的质量和多样性。MiAD代表了晶体材料生成领域的新标杆。

Abstract: In recent years, diffusion-based models have demonstrated exceptional performance in searching for simultaneously stable, unique, and novel (S.U.N.) crystalline materials. However, most of these models don't have the ability to change the number of atoms in the crystal during the generation process, which limits the variability of model sampling trajectories. In this paper, we demonstrate the severity of this restriction and introduce a simple yet powerful technique, mirage infusion, which enables diffusion models to change the state of the atoms that make up the crystal from existent to non-existent (mirage) and vice versa. We show that this technique improves model quality by up to x2.5 compared to the same model without this modification. The resulting model, Mirage Atom Diffusion (MiAD), is an equivariant joint diffusion model for de novo crystal generation that is capable of altering the number of atoms during the generation process. MiAD achieves an 8.2% S.U.N. rate on the MP-20 dataset, which substantially exceeds existing state-of-the-art approaches. Code: https://github.com/andrey-okhotin/miad.git

</details>


### [386] [NCSAM Noise-Compensated Sharpness-Aware Minimization for Noisy Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19947)
*Jiayu Xu,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化角度研究噪声标签学习，建立了标签噪声与SAM平坦性行为之间的理论联系，并提出NCSAM方法，通过噪声补偿扰动来抵消噪声标签引起的优化偏差，在多个基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有噪声标签学习方法多依赖标签校正或样本选择机制，缺乏从优化视角理解噪声标签对训练过程影响的理论分析，尤其是标签噪声与损失景观平坦性之间的关系尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出噪声补偿的锐度感知最小化（NCSAM），通过设计噪声补偿扰动来修正被噪声标签扭曲的SAM扰动，从而在保持优化方法简洁性的同时减轻对噪声标签的记忆。

Result: 在合成噪声和真实噪声标签基准上，NCSAM一致优于基于SAM的优化基线方法，并与代表性噪声标签学习方法相比保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 从优化角度为噪声标签学习提供了新的理论视角，证明了通过修正优化过程中的扰动可以有效对抗标签噪声，为未来LNL研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Learning from Noisy Labels (LNL) remains a fundamental challenge in deep learning because real-world datasets often contain corrupted annotations. Most existing methods rely on label correction or sample selection mechanisms. In contrast, we study LNL from an optimization perspective by establishing a theoretical connection between label noise and the flatness-seeking behavior of Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM). Based on this analysis, we propose Noise-Compensated Sharpness-Aware Minimization (NCSAM), which uses a noise-compensated perturbation to counteract the optimization bias induced by noisy labels. By correcting distorted SAM perturbations, NCSAM mitigates the memorization of noisy labels during training while preserving the simplicity of optimization-based learning. Experiments on synthetic and real-world noisy-label benchmarks show that NCSAM consistently improves over SAM-based optimization baselines and remains competitive with representative noisy-label learning methods.

</details>


### [387] [BioArc: Discovering Optimal Neural Architectures for Biological Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00283)
*Yi Fang,Haoran Xu,Jiaxin Han,Sirui Ding,Yizhi Wang,Yue Wang,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioArc是一个利用神经架构搜索（NAS）系统性地发现适用于生物学基础模型的高效架构的框架，通过分析架构、分词和训练策略的相互作用，提炼出一套经验性设计原则，并提出了针对新生物学任务的架构预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作直接套用通用AI领域的预训练架构，缺乏针对生物数据独特物理化学和结构特性的系统性设计，导致难以捕捉生物数据中的长程依赖、稀疏信息和复杂语法，性能次优。

Method: 提出BioArc框架，利用神经架构搜索（NAS）系统探索大规模架构设计空间，跨多种生物模态评估架构，并严格分析架构、分词与训练策略之间的相互作用；进一步提出并比较了多种架构预测方法。

Result: 识别出新型高性能架构，提炼出一组经验性设计原则；提出的架构预测方法能有效预测新生物学任务的最优架构。

Conclusion: 该工作为基础资源和方法论，为开发下一代生物学任务特定模型和基础模型提供了指导原则和系统性方法论。

Abstract: Foundation models have revolutionized various fields such as natural language processing (NLP) and computer vision (CV). While efforts have been made to transfer the success of the foundation models in general AI domains to biology, existing works focus on directly adopting the existing foundation model architectures from general machine learning domains without a systematic design considering the unique physicochemical and structural properties of each biological data modality. This leads to suboptimal performance, as these repurposed architectures struggle to capture the long-range dependencies, sparse information, and complex underlying ``grammars'' inherent to biological data. To address this gap, we introduce BioArc, a novel framework designed to move beyond intuition-driven architecture design towards principled, automated architecture discovery for biological foundation models. Leveraging Neural Architecture Search (NAS), BioArc systematically explores a vast architecture design space, evaluating architectures across multiple biological modalities while rigorously analyzing the interplay between architecture, tokenization, and training strategies. This large-scale analysis identifies novel, high-performance architectures, allowing us to distill a set of empirical design principles to guide future model development. Furthermore, to make the best of this set of discovered principled architectures, we propose and compare several architecture prediction methods that effectively and efficiently predict optimal architectures for new biological tasks. Overall, our work provides a foundational resource and a principled methodology to guide the creation of the next generation of task-specific and foundation models for biology.

</details>


### [388] [Rectified LpJEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures with Sparse and Maximum-Entropy Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01456)
*Yilun Kuang,Yash Dagade,Tim G. J. Rudner,Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rectified Distribution Matching Regularization (RDMReg)，通过将表示对齐到Rectified Generalized Gaussian (RGG)分布，在联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)中实现稀疏、非负的表征学习，在保持任务相关信息的同时获得了优异的稀疏性-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法使用各向同性高斯分布进行正则化，倾向于学习稠密表征，未能捕捉高效表征中稀疏性的关键特性。

Method: 提出RDMReg，一种切片双样本分布匹配损失，将表示对齐到Rectified Generalized Gaussian (RGG)分布；RGG通过整流操作实现对期望ℓ₀范数的显式控制，其连续截断部分在期望ℓₚ范数和支撑集约束下具有最大熵特性。

Result: Rectified LpJEPA学习到稀疏、非负的表征，在稀疏性-性能权衡和图像分类下游任务上均取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: RDMReg能够在保持任务相关信息的同时强制执行稀疏性，且提出的Rectified LpJEPA严格泛化了先前基于高斯的JEPA方法。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) learn view-invariant representations and admit projection-based distribution matching for collapse prevention. Existing approaches regularize representations towards isotropic Gaussian distributions, but inherently favor dense representations and fail to capture the key property of sparsity observed in efficient representations. We introduce Rectified Distribution Matching Regularization (RDMReg), a sliced two-sample distribution-matching loss that aligns representations to a Rectified Generalized Gaussian (RGG) distribution. RGG enables explicit control over expected $\ell_0$ norm through rectification, while its continuous truncated component admits a maximum-entropy characterization under expected $\ell_p$ norm and support constraints. Equipping JEPAs with RDMReg yields Rectified LpJEPA, which strictly generalizes prior Gaussian-based JEPAs. Empirically, Rectified LpJEPA learns sparse, non-negative representations with favorable sparsity--performance trade-offs and competitive downstream performance on image classification benchmarks, showing that RDMReg can enforce sparsity while preserving task-relevant information.

</details>


### [389] [E-valuator: Reliable Agent Verifiers with Sequential Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03109)
*Shuvom Sadhuka,Drew Prinster,Clara Fannjiang,Gabriele Scalia,Bonnie Berger,Aviv Regev,Hanchen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出e-valuator方法，将任意黑箱验证器分数转化为具有可控误报率的决策规则，通过序贯假设检验实现智能体轨迹的在线监控，在六个数据集和三个智能体上展现出更好的统计功效和误报率控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的验证器（如LLM裁判、过程奖励模型）虽然能为智能体轨迹中的每个动作提供启发式评分，但这些分数缺乏正确性保证，无法可靠地判断智能体是否会产生成功输出。

Method: 将成功与不成功轨迹的区分问题形式化为序贯假设检验问题，基于e-process理论构建序贯假设检验，在智能体轨迹的每一步都保持统计有效性，支持任意长动作序列的在线监控。

Result: 在六个数据集和三种智能体上，e-valuator比现有策略具有更大的统计功效和更好的误报率控制；同时可用于快速终止问题轨迹以节省token。

Conclusion: e-valuator提供了一种轻量级、模型无关的框架，将验证器启发式评分转化为具有统计保证的决策规则，助力更可靠的智能体系统部署。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems execute a sequence of actions, such as reasoning steps or tool calls, in response to a user prompt. To evaluate the success of their trajectories, researchers have developed verifiers, such as LLM judges and process-reward models, to score the quality of each action in an agent's trajectory. Although these heuristic scores can be informative, there are no guarantees of correctness when used to decide whether an agent will yield a successful output. Here, we introduce e-valuator, a method to convert any black-box verifier score into a decision rule with provable control of false alarm rates. We frame the problem of distinguishing successful trajectories (that is, a sequence of actions that will lead to a correct response to the user's prompt) and unsuccessful trajectories as a sequential hypothesis testing problem. E-valuator builds on tools from e-processes to develop a sequential hypothesis test that remains statistically valid at every step of an agent's trajectory, enabling online monitoring of agents over arbitrarily long sequences of actions. Empirically, we demonstrate that e-valuator provides greater statistical power and better false alarm rate control than other strategies across six datasets and three agents. We additionally show that e-valuator can be used for to quickly terminate problematic trajectories and save tokens. Together, e-valuator provides a lightweight, model-agnostic framework that converts verifier heuristics into decisions rules with statistical guarantees, enabling the deployment of more reliable agentic systems.

</details>


### [390] [DCFO: Density-Based Counterfactuals for Outliers -- Additional Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10659)
*Tommaso Amico,Pernille Matthews,Lena Krieger,Arthur Zimek,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCFO方法，专门为局部异常因子（LOF）算法生成反事实解释，通过在数据空间分区实现高效梯度优化，在50个OpenML数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测中，LOF是广泛使用的无监督方法但缺乏可解释性；现有反事实解释方法未针对异常检测的特殊挑战设计，也未覆盖LOF这类经典算法。

Method: DCFO将数据空间划分为LOF行为平滑的区域，在此基础上进行高效的基于梯度的优化，生成反事实解释。

Result: 在50个OpenML数据集上的实验表明，DCFO在生成反事实的邻近性和有效性方面持续优于对比方法。

Conclusion: DCFO成功为LOF提供了可解释性，填补了经典异常检测算法缺乏反事实解释的空白，具有更优的性能。

Abstract: Outlier detection identifies data points that significantly deviate from the majority of the data distribution. Explaining outliers is crucial for understanding the underlying factors that contribute to their detection, validating their significance, and identifying potential biases or errors. Effective explanations provide actionable insights, facilitating preventive measures to avoid similar outliers in the future. Counterfactual explanations clarify why specific data points are classified as outliers by identifying minimal changes required to alter their prediction. Although valuable, most existing counterfactual explanation methods overlook the unique challenges posed by outlier detection, and fail to target classical, widely adopted outlier detection algorithms. Local Outlier Factor (LOF) is one the most popular unsupervised outlier detection methods, quantifying outlierness through relative local density. Despite LOF's widespread use across diverse applications, it lacks interpretability. To address this limitation, we introduce Density-based Counterfactuals for Outliers (DCFO), a novel method specifically designed to generate counterfactual explanations for LOF. DCFO partitions the data space into regions where LOF behaves smoothly, enabling efficient gradient-based optimisation. Extensive experimental validation on 50 OpenML datasets demonstrates that DCFO consistently outperforms benchmarked competitors, offering superior proximity and validity of generated counterfactuals.

</details>


### [391] [Learning to Solve PDEs on Neural Shape Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21311)
*Lilian Welschinger,Yilin Liu,Zican Wang,Niloy Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无网格的神经局部更新算子方法，用于直接在神经表面表示上求解偏微分方程，无需显式网格提取或逐实例训练，支持端到端流程，在多种形状和拓扑上实现高精度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDE求解器基于多边形/三角网格，而现代3D资产多以神经表示（如隐式神经场）存在，两者不匹配导致无法直接在神经域中求解表面PDE，需显式网格化或逐实例残差训练，阻碍了端到端工作流。

Method: 学习一个以神经局部形状属性为条件的局部更新算子，该算子可直接作用于神经表面表示，无需网格；在单个代表性形状上训练一次，即可泛化到不同形状和拓扑。

Result: 在解析基准（球面上的热扩散和泊松方程）及多样形状和神经表面表示上，精度与经典求解器相当，同时支持统一的端到端流程。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了神经表示与经典PDE求解之间的鸿沟，提供了一种可微、无网格、跨形状泛化的表面PDE求解新范式，适用于神经与传统表面表示的统一管道。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations (PDEs) on shapes underpins many shape analysis and engineering tasks; yet, prevailing PDE solvers operate on polygonal/triangle meshes while modern 3D assets increasingly live as neural representations. This mismatch leaves no suitable method to solve surface PDEs directly within the neural domain, forcing explicit mesh extraction or per-instance residual training, preventing end-to-end workflows. We present a novel, meshfree formulation that learns a local update operator conditioned on neural (local) shape attributes, enabling surface PDEs to be solved directly where the (neural) data lives. The operator integrates naturally with prevalent neural surface representations, is trained once on a single representative shape, and generalizes across shape and topology variations, enabling accurate, fast inference without explicit meshing or per-instance optimization while preserving differentiability. Across analytic benchmarks (heat diffusion and Poisson equations on the sphere) and on diverse shapes and neural surface representations, our method achieves accuracy comparable to classical solvers while enabling a unified, end-to-end pipeline across neural and traditional surface representations. Our source code and project page: https://welschinger.github.io/Learning-to-Solve-PDEs-on-Neural-Shape-Representations/.

</details>


### [392] [When the Same Coefficients Reach Different Places: Asymmetric Realizability in Transplanting Tokenizers across Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00065)
*Xiaoze Liu,Weichen Yu,Matt Fredrikson,Xiaoqian Wang,Jing Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现跨词汇表模型组合中tokenizer移植存在不对称可实现性缺口：同一系数向量在供体锚定跨度中统计惰性，在基座锚定跨度中产生高显著性重建。利用这一几何特性可构造"破坏令牌"，并在多种模型组合下验证其有效性与隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示跨词汇表模型组合中tokenizer移植操作存在的结构性安全隐患，特别是可被利用的几何不对称性，以警示开放权重组合供应链中的潜在滥用风险。

Method: 基于OMP(正交匹配追踪)对65组供体-基座模型对进行分析，利用CLP、WECHSEL、FOCUS等跨算子进行验证，构造单一系数向量(breaker tokens)使其在供体锚定跨度内统计惰性而在基座锚定跨度内高显著性重建。同时结合权重合并、LoRA微调及频谱滤波器进行检测规避验证。

Result: 同一Gemma-2-2B供体检查点可对5个模型家族的13种不同下游基座构造破坏令牌；该植入方向在权重合并中保持未变；LoRA微调仅对训练分布匹配的提示有效抑制破坏令牌；频谱滤波器无法检测该不对称性。

Conclusion: 标准防御措施（如LoRA微调、频谱滤波）无法充分缓解此类攻击，存在被滥用于开放权重组合供应链的实际风险。

Abstract: Tokenizer transplant in cross-vocabulary model composition reconstructs donor-only embedding rows as weighted combinations over shared lexical anchors and reuses those coefficients on the base. We identify a structural geometric property of this reconstruction: the same coefficient vector reaches different sets in the donor and base anchor spans, an \emph{asymmetric realizability} gap. Across 65 donor-base pairs under OMP, with cross-operator validation on CLP, WECHSEL, and FOCUS, we construct \textit{breaker tokens}: single coefficient vectors that remain statistically inert in the donor anchor span while producing a high-salience reconstruction in the base. The same Gemma-2-2B donor checkpoint admits this construction against 13 different downstream bases drawn from five model families. The planted direction passes weight-merging with a clean reference unchanged. In a deployer case study, standard LoRA fine-tuning suppresses the breaker primarily on prompts whose distribution matches the training corpus and is not a sufficient mitigation against this attack family in our setting. The tested spectral filters miss the asymmetry. We discuss potential misuse in the open-weight composition supply chain.

</details>


### [393] [Bridging the Semantic Gap for Categorical Data Clustering via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01162)
*Zihua Yang,Xin Liao,Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BREVE框架，通过外部知识库为定性数据中的每个属性值添加语义嵌入维度，并保留原始独热编码成分，利用自适应权重平衡两者，从而在小样本场景下提升聚类性能，在8个基准数据集上平均ARI排名达1.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定性数据聚类方法依赖数据集内部的共现统计量来衡量属性值间的相似性，但在小样本情况下统计不可靠，且未能充分利用值的语义上下文信息。

Method: 提出BREVE框架：1）利用外部知识库为每个定性值生成稠密语义嵌入作为额外维度；2）保留轻量级独热编码成分以防止原始值身份被稀释；3）通过自适应权重（由聚类紧致度引导）决定语义增强维度在最终表示中的贡献程度。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上，BREVE的平均ARI排名为1.3，优于7个代表性对比方法。

Conclusion: 利用外部语义知识能够有效弥补小样本下共现统计的不足，通过平衡外部语义与原始身份信息的表示设计，可显著提升定性数据聚类效果。

Abstract: Qualitative data are widespread in domains such as healthcare, marketing, and bioinformatics, where clustering offers a fundamental tool for pattern discovery. A core difficulty of qualitative-data clustering lies in measuring similarity among attribute values that carry no inherent ordering or distance. To recover such relationships, existing studies typically rely on within-dataset co-occurrence statistics. This statistical route, however, becomes unreliable once the sample size is small, and the semantic context of each value is therefore left underexploited. Motivated by this limitation, this paper proposes BREVE (Balanced Representation via External Value Enrichment), a clustering framework that enriches each qualitative value with extra semantic dimensions drawn from an external knowledge base. That is, every unique value is expanded by a dense embedding that encodes its semantic content. To prevent the original value identity from being diluted by the added dimensions, a lightweight one-hot component is further appended. An adaptive weight, guided by cluster compactness, then determines how strongly the enrichment dimensions enter the final representation. With this design, experiments on eight benchmark datasets yield an average ARI rank of 1.3 against seven representative competitors.

</details>


### [394] [FedBiCross: Personalized One-Shot Federated Learning on Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01901)
*Yuexuan Xia,Yinghao Zhang,Yalin Liu,Hong-Ning Dai,Yong Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedBiCross是一种面向非独立同分布医疗数据的无数据知识蒸馏一次性联邦学习框架，通过客户端聚类、双层跨簇优化和个性化蒸馏三个阶段，有效解决预测冲突导致的标签弱化问题，在四个医学图像数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无数据知识蒸馏的一次性联邦学习方法在非独立同分布数据下，聚合所有客户端的预测会因冲突预测相互稀释，生成信息量不足的软标签，削弱蒸馏效果，因此需要一种能处理预测冲突的个性化联邦学习框架。

Method: 提出FedBiCross框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 根据模型输出相似性对客户端聚类，形成一致性子集成；(2) 双层跨簇优化，学习自适应权重选择性利用有益的跨簇知识并抑制负迁移；(3) 个性化蒸馏实现客户端特定适应。

Result: 在四个医学图像数据集上，FedBiCross在不同非独立同分布程度下均一致优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedBiCross通过聚类和双层优化有效解决了非独立同分布数据下预测冲突的问题，提升了无数据知识蒸馏一次性联邦学习的性能，适用于隐私敏感的医疗应用场景。

Abstract: Data-free knowledge distillation-based one-shot federated learning (OSFL) trains a model in a single communication round without sharing raw data, making OSFL attractive for privacy-sensitive medical applications. However, existing methods aggregate predictions from all clients to form a global teacher. Under non-IID data, conflicting predictions dilute each other during averaging, yielding less informative soft labels that weaken distillation. We propose FedBiCross, a personalized OSFL framework with three stages: (1) clustering clients by model output similarity to form coherent sub-ensembles, (2) bi-level cross-cluster optimization that learns adaptive weights to selectively leverage beneficial cross-cluster knowledge while suppressing negative transfer, and (3) personalized distillation for client-specific adaptation. Experiments on four medical image datasets demonstrate that FedBiCross consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different non-IID degrees.

</details>


### [395] [Mechanism Shift During Post-training from Autoregressive to Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14758)
*Injin Kong,Hyoungjoon Lee,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析发现，将自回归模型后训练为掩码扩散模型并非表面变化，而是一种内部计算重组——结构上任务依赖（局部因果任务保留原电路，全局任务则放弃继承路径并将计算前移），语义上始终从尖锐局部专业化转向分布式整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后训练的掩码扩散模型是否真正获得了新的计算机制，还是仅仅以非自回归形式重新表达了自回归计算。

Method: 对比分析自回归模型与其从相同骨干后训练得到的掩码扩散模型之间的电路（circuit）。

Result: 发现两个互补的重组轴：结构上，任务依赖——局部因果任务保留自回归电路，全局任务放弃继承路径并将计算前移至早期层；语义上，一致转变——自回归模型的尖锐局部专业化转变为扩散模型的分布式整合。

Conclusion: 扩散后训练不是生成过程的表层改变，而是内部计算的深度重组，其重组深度取决于任务类型。

Abstract: Post-training pretrained autoregressive models (ARMs) into masked diffusion models (MDMs) has emerged as a cost-effective way to overcome the limitations of sequential generation. Yet it remains unclear whether post-trained MDMs acquire genuinely new computational mechanisms or merely re-express autoregressive computation in a non-autoregressive form. Through a comparative circuit analysis of ARMs and their MDM counterparts post-trained from the same backbones, we uncover two complementary axes of reorganization. Structurally, the shift is task-dependent: MDMs preserve autoregressive circuitry on locally causal tasks but abandon inherited pathways and front-load computation into early layers on global tasks. Semantically, the shift is consistent across regimes: sharp, localized specialization in ARMs gives way to distributed integration in MDMs. Together, these findings show that diffusion post-training is not a surface-level change in the generation procedure but a reorganization of internal computation whose depth depends on the task.

</details>


### [396] [Adaptive Exponential Integration for Stable Gaussian Mixture Black-Box Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14855)
*Baojun Che,Yifan Chen,Daniel Zhengyu Huang,Xinying Mao,Weijie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合仿射不变自然梯度预条件、指数积分器和自适应时间步长的黑箱变分推理框架，用于高斯混合族，实现了稳定高效的近似后验分布推断，并在高斯后验下证明了指数收敛和几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准数值优化方法在高斯混合族的黑箱变分推理中常面临不稳定和低效的问题，亟需发展稳定且高效的框架。

Method: 提出三组件框架：(1) 基于自然梯度公式的仿射不变预条件；(2) 无条件保持协方差矩阵正定性的指数积分器；(3) 确保稳定性并适应预热与收敛阶段的自适应时间步长。该方法与流形优化和镜像下降有自然联系。

Result: 在高斯后验下证明了无噪声环境的指数收敛和蒙特卡洛估计下的几乎必然收敛；在多峰分布、Neal多尺度漏斗以及达西流PDE贝叶斯逆问题的数值实验上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了高斯混合族BBVI中的不稳定和低效问题，自适应时间步长是实现收敛的必要条件，方法具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Black-box variational inference (BBVI) with Gaussian mixture families offers a flexible approach for approximating complex posterior distributions without requiring gradients of the target density. However, standard numerical optimization methods often suffer from instability and inefficiency. We develop a stable and efficient framework that combines three key components: (1) affine-invariant preconditioning via natural gradient formulations, (2) an exponential integrator that unconditionally preserves the positive definiteness of covariance matrices, and (3) adaptive time stepping to ensure stability and to accommodate distinct warm-up and convergence phases. The proposed approach has natural connections to manifold optimization and mirror descent. For Gaussian posteriors, we prove exponential convergence in the noise-free setting and almost-sure convergence under Monte Carlo estimation, rigorously justifying the necessity of adaptive time stepping. Numerical experiments on multimodal distributions, Neal's multiscale funnel, and a PDE-based Bayesian inverse problem for Darcy flow demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [397] [Riemannian AmbientFlow: Towards Simultaneous Manifold Learning and Generative Modeling from Corrupted Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18728)
*Willem Diepeveen,Oscar Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Riemannian AmbientFlow框架，能从带噪声或线性损坏的观测数据中同时学习概率生成模型和底层非线性数据流形，结合归一化流、黎曼几何和变分推断，理论证明在适当条件下可恢复数据分布并得到光滑的双Lipschitz流形参数化，实验在合成流形和MNIST上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多科学和成像应用中，干净样本不可用，只能观测到噪声或线性损坏的测量数据，且数据中存在的潜在结构（如流形几何）对后续科学分析至关重要，因此需要同时学习生成模型和底层数据流形。

Method: 基于AmbientFlow的变分推断框架，引入由归一化流诱导的数据驱动黎曼几何，通过拉回度量（pullback metrics）和黎曼自编码器提取流形结构，并结合几何正则化与测量条件。

Result: 理论证明在适当几何正则化和测量条件下，模型能以可控误差恢复底层数据分布，并得到光滑的双Lipschitz流形参数化；该光滑解码器可作为逆问题的生成先验并具有恢复保证；在低维合成流形和MNIST上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: Riemannian AmbientFlow可从损坏观测中有效学习数据流形结构和生成模型，兼具理论保证和实证有效性，为科学逆问题提供了一种有原则的生成先验方法。

Abstract: Modern generative modeling methods have demonstrated strong performance in learning complex data distributions from clean samples. In many scientific and imaging applications, however, clean samples are unavailable, and only noisy or linearly corrupted measurements can be observed. Moreover, latent structures, such as manifold geometries, present in the data are important to extract for further downstream scientific analysis. In this work, we introduce Riemannian AmbientFlow, a framework for simultaneously learning a probabilistic generative model and the underlying, nonlinear data manifold directly from corrupted observations. Building on the variational inference framework of AmbientFlow, our approach incorporates data-driven Riemannian geometry induced by normalizing flows, enabling the extraction of manifold structure through pullback metrics and Riemannian Autoencoders. We establish theoretical guarantees showing that, under appropriate geometric regularization and measurement conditions, the learned model recovers the underlying data distribution up to a controllable error and yields a smooth, bi-Lipschitz manifold parametrization. We further show that the resulting smooth decoder can serve as a principled generative prior for inverse problems with recovery guarantees. We empirically validate our approach on low-dimensional synthetic manifolds and on MNIST.

</details>


### [398] [HE-SNR: Uncovering Latent Logic via Entropy for Guiding Mid-Training on SWE-bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20255)
*Yueyang Wang,Jiawei Fu,Baolong Bi,Xili Wang,Xiaoqing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对SWE-bench基准中大型语言模型在软件工程任务上的评估，提出了一种基于熵压缩假设的新指标HE-SNR，用于指导模型的中期训练，解决了现有指标（如困惑度）与下游任务表现弱相关的问题，并在高达560B参数的模型上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指标（如困惑度）受"长上下文税"影响，与下游SWE性能相关性弱，缺乏能够有效指导中期训练的度量标准。

Method: 提出严格的数据过滤策略；提出熵压缩假设（Entropy Compression Hypothesis），将智能重新定义为将不确定性结构化为低阶熵压缩状态（"合理犹豫"）的能力；基于细粒度熵分析，提出新指标HE-SNR（高熵信噪比）。

Result: 在高达560B参数的模型上，针对32K/128K不同上下文窗口进行了验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为优化LLM在复杂工程领域的潜在能力提供了理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: SWE-bench has emerged as the premier benchmark for evaluating Large Language Models on complex software engineering tasks. While these capabilities are fundamentally acquired during the mid-training phase and subsequently elicited during Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), there remains a critical deficit in metrics capable of guiding mid-training effectively. Standard metrics such as Perplexity (PPL) are compromised by the "Long-Context Tax" and exhibit weak correlation with downstream SWE performance. In this paper, we bridge this gap by first introducing a rigorous data filtering strategy. Crucially, we propose the Entropy Compression Hypothesis, redefining intelligence not by scalar Top-1 compression, but by the capacity to structure uncertainty into Entropy-Compressed States of low orders ("reasonable hesitation"). Grounded in this fine-grained entropy analysis, we formulate a novel metric, HE-SNR (High-Entropy Signal-to-Noise Ratio). We validate our approach on models with up to 560B parameters across different context windows (32K/128K). This work provides both the theoretical foundation and practical tools for optimizing the latent potential of LLMs in complex engineering domains.

</details>


### [399] [Bridging Functional and Representational Similarity via Usable Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21568)
*Antonio Almudévar,Alfonso Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可用信息视角，提出了统一框架量化表示相似性，建立了拼接性能与条件互信息的理论联系，揭示了拼接的固有不对称性，发现重构度量与CKA、RSA等标准工具是可用信息在特定约束下的估计量，证明了表示相似性对功能相似性充分不必要，并通过任务粒度层次统一了这些概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前表示相似性度量方法多样但缺乏统一理论框架，不同方法（如拼接法、CKA、RSA、重构度量）之间的关系不明确，且功能相似性与表示相似性之间的逻辑关系尚未被严格刻画。

Method: 通过条件互信息形式化建立拼接性能与可用信息的理论联系；分析拼接的不对称性并提出双向分析必要性；证明重构度量及CKA、RSA等标准工具在特定约束下可作为可用信息的估计量；通过任务粒度层次（task-granularity hierarchy）统一功能相似性与表示相似性。

Result: 拼接性能与条件互信息存在形式化联系；拼接具有固有不对称性；表示相似性（通过重构度量）是可用信息在最大粒度（输入重构）下的表现，而CKA、RSA等是特定约束下的估计量；表示相似性是功能相似性的充分不必要条件；提出任务粒度层次，复杂任务上的相似性保证更粗粒度任务上的相似性。

Conclusion: 可用信息为表示相似性度量提供了统一的理论框架，揭示了不同度量方法之间的内在联系及局限性，为理解和选择表示相似性度量提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We present a unified framework for quantifying the similarity between representations through the lens of \textit{usable} information, offering a rigorous theoretical and empirical synthesis across three key dimensions. First, addressing functional similarity, we establish a formal link between stitching performance and conditional mutual information. We further reveal that stitching is inherently asymmetric, demonstrating that robust functional comparison necessitates a bidirectional analysis rather than a unidirectional mapping. Second, concerning representational similarity, we find that reconstruction-based metrics and standard tools (e.g., CKA, RSA) act as estimators of usable information under specific constraints. Crucially, we show that similarity is relative to the capacity of the predictive family: representations that appear distinct to a rigid observer may be identical to a more expressive one. Third, we demonstrate that representational similarity is sufficient but not necessary for functional similarity. We unify these concepts through a task-granularity hierarchy: similarity on a complex task guarantees similarity on any coarser derivative, establishing representational similarity as the limit of maximum granularity: input reconstruction.

</details>


### [400] [Server-Proximal Aggregation for Federated Domain-Incremental Learning under Partial Participation: Task-Uniform Convergence and Backward Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22274)
*Longtao Xu,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPECIAL算法，通过在FedAvg中添加服务器端近端项（anchor）实现联邦域增量学习（FDIL），在无需回放缓冲区、合成数据或任务特定头的情况下，提供后向知识迁移保证和部分参与下的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实联邦系统面临数据分布漂移且隐私规则禁止原始数据共享，但现有FDIL方法缺乏后向知识迁移的理论保证和部分参与下跨所有任务的收敛率分析。

Method: SPECIAL算法：在标准FedAvg基础上，服务器在每轮对均匀采样的参与客户端的更新施加一个轻量级近端项，将更新拉向之前的全局模型（anchor），从而抑制累积漂移，无需回放缓冲区、合成数据或任务特定头。

Result: 理论：①后向知识迁移界：先前任务损失的增长被漂移控制项所限制，该项随更多轮次、本地epoch和参与客户端数量而缩小；②首个FDIL部分参与下的非凸收敛率O((E/NT)^(1/2))，匹配单任务FedAvg，并明确分离优化方差与任务间漂移。实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: SPECIAL通过简单的服务器端anchor机制，在保持通信和模型大小不变的前提下，同时实现了对旧任务的知识保留和跨所有任务的高效学习，为FDIL提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Real-world federated systems seldom operate on static data: input distributions drift while privacy rules forbid raw-data sharing. We study this setting as Federated Domain-Incremental Learning (FDIL), where (i) clients are heterogeneous, (ii) tasks arrive sequentially with shifting domains, yet (iii) the label space remains fixed. Two theoretical pillars remain missing for FDIL under realistic deployment: a guarantee of backward knowledge transfer (BKT) and a convergence rate that holds across the sequence of all tasks with partial participation. We introduce SPECIAL (Server-Proximal Efficient Continual Aggregation for Learning), a simple, memory-free FDIL algorithm that adds a single server-side ``anchor'' to vanilla FedAvg: in each round, the server nudges the uniformly sampled participated clients update toward the previous global model with a lightweight proximal term. This anchor curbs cumulative drift without replay buffers, synthetic data, or task-specific heads, keeping communication and model size unchanged. Our theory shows that SPECIAL (i) preserves earlier tasks: a BKT bound caps any increase in prior-task loss by a drift-controlled term that shrinks with more rounds, local epochs, and participating clients; and (ii) learns efficiently across all tasks: the first communication-efficient non-convex convergence rate for FDIL with partial participation, O((E/NT)^(1/2)), with E local epochs, T communication rounds, and N participated clients per round, matching single-task FedAvg while explicitly separating optimization variance from inter-task drift. Experimental results further demonstrate the effectiveness of SPECIAL.

</details>


### [401] [Pushing the Limits of Block Rotations in Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22347)
*Sai Sanjeet,Ian Colbert,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Giuseppe Franco,Yaman Umuroglu,Nicholas J. Fraser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PeRQ框架，通过预旋转前的排列操作重新分配激活值质量，有效抑制块Hadamard旋转后的离群值，在低块大小下显著恢复量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块旋转PTQ方法虽降低了全向量旋转的计算开销，但块结构对离群值抑制的影响缺乏理论理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，首次系统分析块Hadamard旋转的离群值抑制机制。

Method: 1) 首次对块Hadamard旋转的离群值抑制进行非渐近理论分析，揭示旋转后离群值受输入向量几何结构的限制；2) 提出PeRQ框架，在旋转前引入排列操作，通过贪心质量扩散算法校准排列以均衡期望的逐块ℓ1范数；3) 识别Transformer中排列等变的区域，将排列合并到模型权重中以避免推理开销。

Result: PeRQ在所有块大小上一致提升量化精度。在Llama3 1B INT4量化（块大小16）下，恢复全向量旋转困惑度的90%，而基线仅恢复46%。

Conclusion: 排列预处理能有效弥补块旋转在离群值抑制上的几何限制，为低块大小下的高效量化提供了理论指导与实践方案。

Abstract: Recent post-training quantization (PTQ) methods have adopted block rotations to diffuse outliers prior to rounding. While this reduces the overhead of online full-vector rotations, the effect of block structure on outlier suppression remains poorly understood. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic, non-asymptotic analysis of outlier suppression for block Hadamard rotations. Our analysis reveals that outlier suppression is fundamentally limited by the geometry of the input vector. In particular, in the deterministic worst case, post-rotation outliers are minimized when the pre-rotation $\ell_1$ norm mass is evenly distributed across blocks. Guided by these insights, we introduce PeRQ (Permute, Rotate, then Quantize), a PTQ framework that redistributes activation mass via permutations prior to rotation. We propose a greedy mass diffusion algorithm to calibrate permutations by equalizing the expected blockwise $\ell_1$ norms. To avoid adding inference overhead, we identify permutation-equivariant regions in transformer architectures to merge these permutations into model weights before deployment. Experiments show that PeRQ consistently improves accuracy across all block sizes, recovering up to 90% of the full-vector rotation perplexity when quantizing Llama3 1B to INT4 with block size 16, compared to 46% without permutations.

</details>


### [402] [Unsupervised Hierarchical Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23156)
*Damion Harvey,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Benjamin Rosman,Branden Ingram,Steven James*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督技能分割与层次结构发现方法，利用语法从无标签轨迹中提取技能并构建层次化结构，在Craftax和完整Minecraft等像素级环境中验证了其语义层次质量和下游RL任务的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督技能分割方法大多依赖动作标签、奖励或手工标注，限制了其通用性和可扩展性，因此需要一种无需这些监督信号即可从原始轨迹中自动发现技能及层次结构的方法。

Method: 采用基于语法的方法对无标签轨迹进行分割，将轨迹片段归纳为可复用的技能，并在此基础上诱导出技能的层次化结构，同时捕捉低层行为和其向高层技能的组合。

Result: 在高维像素环境（Craftax和完整Minecraft）中，使用技能分割、复用和层次质量指标，所提方法在生成结构化且语义有意义的层次结构方面持续优于现有基线；此外，发现的层次结构能加速并稳定下游RL任务的学习。

Conclusion: 基于语法的无监督方法能够有效从无标签轨迹中自动发现可复用的技能及其层次结构，提升下游任务的训练效率，为无需人工标注的技能学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We consider the problem of unsupervised skill segmentation and hierarchical structure discovery in reinforcement learning. While recent approaches have sought to segment trajectories into reusable skills or options, most rely on action labels, rewards, or handcrafted annotations, limiting their applicability. We propose a method that segments unlabelled trajectories into skills and induces a hierarchical structure over them using a grammar-based approach. The resulting hierarchy captures both low-level behaviours and their composition into higher-level skills. We evaluate our approach in high-dimensional, pixel-based environments, including Craftax and the full, unmodified version of Minecraft. Using metrics for skill segmentation, reuse, and hierarchy quality, we find that our method consistently produces more structured and semantically meaningful hierarchies than existing baselines. Furthermore, as a proof of concept, we demonstrate that these discovered hierarchies accelerate and stabilise learning on downstream reinforcement learning tasks.

</details>


### [403] [Good SFT Optimizes for SFT, Better SFT Prepares for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01058)
*Dylan Zhang,Yufeng Xu,Haojin Wang,Qingzhi Chen,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEAR通过重要性采样重新加权SFT损失，解决了SFT与RL之间数据分布不匹配的问题，使得从较弱SFT检查点初始化的模型在后续RL训练后反而能超越较强SFT检查点初始化的模型，在数学推理任务上Pass@8提升最高达14.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SFT-RL流水线中，SFT阶段独立优化离线数据，与后续在线RL阶段从自身采样学习的数据分布存在显著不匹配，导致更强的SFT检查点在RL后反而表现更差。

Method: 提出PEAR算法，使用重要性采样（Importance Sampling）对SFT损失进行重新加权，包含token级、block级和sequence级三种变体，在保持SFT目标不变的前提下修正数据分布偏差。

Result: 在Qwen 2.5/3和DeepSeek-distilled模型的可验证推理游戏和数学推理任务上，PEAR相比标准SFT一致提升后续RL性能，AIME2025上Pass@8提升达14.6%。

Conclusion: PEAR是迈向更全面LLM后训练的有效步骤，强调了SFT阶段应结合下游RL进行设计和评估，而非孤立优化。

Abstract: Post-training of reasoning LLMs is a holistic process that typically consists of an offline SFT stage followed by an online reinforcement learning (RL) stage. However, SFT is often optimized in isolation to maximize SFT performance alone.
  We show that, after identical RL training, models initialized from stronger SFT checkpoints can significantly underperform those initialized from weaker ones. We attribute this to a mismatch typical in current SFT-RL pipelines: the distribution that generates the offline SFT data can differ substantially from the policy optimized during online RL, which learns from its own rollouts.
  We propose PEAR (Policy Evaluation-inspired Algorithm for Offline Learning Loss Re-weighting), an SFT-stage method that corrects this mismatch and better prepares the model for RL. PEAR uses importance sampling to reweight the SFT loss, with three variants operating at the token, block, and sequence levels. It can be used to augment standard SFT objectives and incurs little additional training overhead once probabilities for the offline data are collected.
  We conduct controlled experiments on verifiable reasoning games and mathematical reasoning tasks on Qwen 2.5 and 3 and DeepSeek-distilled models. PEAR consistently improves post-RL performance over canonical SFT, with pass at 8 gains up to a 14.6 percent on AIME2025. Our results suggest that PEAR is an effective step toward more holistic LLM post-training by designing and evaluating SFT with downstream RL in mind rather than in isolation.

</details>


### [404] [How Far Ahead Do LLMs Plan? Uncovering the Latent Horizon in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02103)
*Liyan Xu,Mo Yu,Fandong Meng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tele-Lens探针方法，发现LLM在链式思维推理中表现出短视的局部规划能力，并基于此改进不确定性估计和CoT路径压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在LLM内部状态与显式CoT推理轨迹之间的关系上存在矛盾：隐藏状态似乎已提前编码未来推理，但显式步骤对组合计算仍至关重要。作者旨在深入理解这种张力，探究LLM的潜在规划能力。

Method: 提出Tele-Lens探针方法，通过探测LLM在不同任务领域的隐藏状态，分析其潜在规划强度；基于观测到的短视特性，提出利用稀疏关键位置进行不确定性估计，并实现自动识别CoT旁路而不损失性能。

Result: 实验表明LLM主要进行增量式局部转换，缺乏精确的全局规划（短视视野）；验证了稀疏关键位置可有效表示整个推理路径的不确定性；实现了CoT旁路的自动识别且无性能下降。

Conclusion: LLM在CoT推理中本质上是短视的，这一特性可用于改进不确定性估计和压缩推理路径；充分挖掘CoT动态特性具有重要价值。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become a central mechanism for eliciting multi-step reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). Yet recent evidence presents a tension: hidden states appear to already encode future reasoning before CoT fully unfolds, while explicit steps still remain crucial for tasks requiring compositional computation. To deepen the understanding between LLM's internal states and its verbalized reasoning trajectories, we investigate the latent planning strength of LLMs, through our probing method, Tele-Lens, applying to hidden states across diverse task domains. Our empirical results indicate that LLMs exhibit a myopic horizon, primarily conducting incremental transitions without precise global planning. Leveraging this characteristic, we propose a hypothesis on enhancing uncertainty estimation of CoT, which we validate that a sparse set of pivot positions can effectively represent the uncertainty of the entire path. We further underscore the significance of exploiting CoT dynamics, and demonstrate that automatic recognition of CoT bypass can be achieved without performance degradation. Our code, data and models are released at https://github.com/lxucs/tele-lens.

</details>


### [405] [Discrete diffusion samplers and bridges: Off-policy algorithms and applications in latent spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05961)
*Arran Carter,Sanghyeok Choi,Kirill Tamogashev,Víctor Elvira,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将离策略训练技术引入离散扩散采样器，以弥补离散空间采样与连续空间采样之间的差距；首次将离散扩散采样器推广到任意两个分布之间的桥接任务（离散域Schrödinger桥训练）；并将其应用于图像生成模型离散潜空间中的无数据后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散空间中的扩散采样器未能充分利用连续空间中常用的技术（如离策略训练），导致性能有限；需要弥合离散与连续扩散采样器之间的差距。

Method: (1) 引入离策略训练技术（off-policy training）用于离散扩散采样器；(2) 提出数据到能量的Schrödinger桥训练（data-to-energy Schrödinger bridge training）首次在离散域实现任意两个分布之间的桥接。

Result: 离策略训练技术提升了离散采样器在已有和新合成基准上的性能；离散扩散采样器成功应用于图像生成模型离散潜空间的无数据后验采样。

Conclusion: 离策略训练和Schrödinger桥泛化有效提升了离散扩散采样器的性能，拓展了其在离散空间中的应用范围。

Abstract: Sampling from a distribution $p(x) \propto e^{-\mathcal{E}(x)}$ known up to a normalising constant is an important and challenging problem in statistics. Recent years have seen the rise of a new family of amortised sampling algorithms, commonly referred to as diffusion samplers, that enable fast and efficient sampling from an unnormalised density. Such algorithms have been widely studied for continuous-space sampling tasks; however, their application to problems in discrete space remains largely unexplored. Although some progress has been made in this area, discrete diffusion samplers do not take full advantage of ideas commonly used for continuous-space sampling. In this paper, we propose to bridge this gap by introducing off-policy training techniques for discrete diffusion samplers. We show that these techniques improve the performance of discrete samplers on both established and new synthetic benchmarks. Next, we generalise discrete diffusion samplers to the task of bridging between two arbitrary distributions, introducing data-to-energy Schrödinger bridge training for the discrete domain for the first time. Lastly, we showcase the application of the proposed diffusion samplers to data-free posterior sampling in the discrete latent spaces of image generative models.

</details>


### [406] [Rare Event Analysis of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06791)
*Jake McAllister Dorman,Edward Gillman,Dominic C. Rose,Jamie F. Mair,Juan P. Garrahan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端框架，用于系统分析大语言模型中的罕见事件（低概率但高影响的行为），涵盖理论、高效生成策略、概率估计和误差分析，并提供了具体实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为概率模型在推理时会表现出罕见事件——这些行为虽不典型但意义重大。由于LLM的部署规模巨大，开发阶段完全未观察到的事件在部署中可能变得显著，因此亟需系统性分析方法。

Method: 提出了一个端到端框架，包含理论支撑、高效生成策略、概率估计方法和误差分析，并通过具体案例进行说明。

Result: 通过具体实例验证了框架的有效性，并展示了其在其他模型和上下文中的扩展与应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架具有通用性，所提出的概念和技术可推广到其他模型和场景中。

Abstract: Being probabilistic models, during inference large language models (LLMs) display rare events: behaviour that is far from typical but highly significant. By definition all rare events are hard to see, but the enormous scale of LLM usage means that events completely unobserved during development are likely to become prominent in deployment. Here we present an end-to-end framework for the systematic analysis of rare events in LLMs. We provide a practical implementation spanning theory, efficient generation strategies, probability estimation and error analysis, which we illustrate with concrete examples. We outline extensions and applications to other models and contexts, highlighting the generality of the concepts and techniques presented here.

</details>


### [407] [Gradient Preconditioning for Efficient and Reliable Reward-Guided Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08646)
*Jisung Hwang,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种梯度预处理方法，将奖励梯度投影到精心设计的白高斯噪声可行集上，使单步生成模型的奖励引导生成既高效又可靠，有效防止奖励黑客攻击，计算复杂度为O(N log N)，在FLUX模型上仅需30%的耗时即可达到与最先进方法相当的Aesthetic Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时噪声优化方法虽然能提升预训练生成模型的奖励引导生成效果，但容易产生奖励黑客攻击导致质量下降，且计算速度过慢难以实际应用。

Method: 设计一个具有逐块范数约束的紧致谱集作为白高斯噪声可行集，将奖励梯度投影到该可行集上，从而将梯度更新重塑为与噪声对齐的方向，实现快速有效的奖励上升同时防止奖励黑客攻击。该投影具有闭式解，计算复杂度与FFT相当。

Result: 在FLUX模型上搭配四种奖励模型进行实验，该方法仅需最先进的基于正则化方法30%的墙钟时间即可达到相当的Aesthetic Score。

Conclusion: 提出的梯度预处理方法在保持高效率的同时有效防止了奖励黑客攻击，为单步生成模型的奖励引导生成提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a gradient preconditioning method that makes reward-guided generation with one-step generative models both efficient and reliable. Test-time noise optimization can unlock substantially better reward-guided generations from pretrained generative models, but it is prone to reward hacking that degrades quality and is often too slow for practical use. We precondition reward gradients by projecting them onto a carefully designed white Gaussian noise feasible set, a compact spectral set with blockwise norm constraints that tightly captures the statistics and spatial uncorrelatedness of white Gaussian noise. This preconditioning reshapes each gradient update into a noise-aligned direction, driving faster and more effective reward ascent while preventing reward hacking. The projection is closed-form and matches the $O(N \log N)$ complexity of FFT, adding negligible overhead in practice. In experiments on FLUX with four reward models, our approach reaches a comparable Aesthetic Score using only 30% of the wall-clock time required by the state-of-the-art regularization-based method.

</details>


### [408] [Computationally Efficient Replicable Learning of Parities and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09499)
*Moshe Noivirt,Jessica Sorrell,Eliad Tsfadia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了可复现PAC学习与差分隐私、统计查询模型之间的计算关系，首次提出了在任意分布上高效可复现的奇偶性学习算法，证明了可复现学习在计算能力上严格超越SQ学习，并接近差分隐私学习，同时证明了可复现性向纯差分隐私的转化需要样本复杂度的严格损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
