Motivation: 探究可复现学习（Replicable Learning）与其他稳定性概念（尤其是差分隐私和统计查询模型）之间的计算关系，此前已知统计上可复现学习与差分隐私学习等价且严格强于SQ学习，但计算上缺乏证据表明可复现学习能超越SQ学习。

Method: 提出首个在任意分布上高效可复现的奇偶性学习算法；设计了一个新的高效可复现算法，给定向量集后输出覆盖大部分向量的线性子空间。

Result: (1) 首个高效可复现的奇偶性学习算法，突破了SQ模型的计算界限；(2) 证明了在RP≠NP假设下，从可复现性转换为纯差分隐私需要样本复杂度的严格损失。

Conclusion: 可复现学习在计算能力上严格超越了SQ学习，更接近差分隐私学习的能力，尽管存在计算上的分离性。

Abstract: We study the computational relationship between replicability (Impagliazzo et al. [STOC `22], Ghazi et al. [NeurIPS `21]) and other stability notions. Specifically, we focus on replicable PAC learning and its connections to differential privacy (Dwork et al. [TCC 2006]) and to the statistical query (SQ) model (Kearns [JACM `98]). Statistically, it was known that differentially private learning and replicable learning are equivalent and strictly more powerful than SQ-learning. Yet, computationally, all previously known efficient (i.e., polynomial-time) replicable learning algorithms were confined to SQ-learnable tasks or restricted distributions, in contrast to differentially private learning.
  Our main contribution is the first computationally efficient replicable algorithm for realizable learning of parities over arbitrary distributions, a task that is known to be hard in the SQ-model, but possible under differential privacy. This result provides the first evidence that efficient replicable learning over general distributions strictly extends efficient SQ-learning, and is closer in power to efficient differentially private learning, despite computational separations between replicability and privacy. Additionally, we leverage our parity learner to prove that, assuming $RP \neq NP$, converting replicability to pure differential privacy requires a strict loss in sample complexity. Our main building block is a new, efficient, and replicable algorithm that, given a set of vectors, outputs a subspace of their linear span that covers most of them.

</details>


### [409] [Prioritize the Process, Not Just the Outcome: Rewarding Latent Thought Trajectories Improves Reasoning in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10520)
*Jonathan Williams,Esin Tureci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLTT框架，通过在LoopLMs的完整潜在推理轨迹上分配奖励来解决GRPO仅对最终状态赋信的问题，在数学推理基准上相比GRPO提升显著（1.4B规模+5.8%，2.6B规模+10.9%），且能泛化到非数学推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习目标（如GRPO）只对LoopLMs的最终潜在状态进行赋信，与模型内部多步潜在推理的计算过程存在根本性不匹配，限制了推理能力的进一步提升。

Method: 提出RLTT框架，在完整潜在推理轨迹上分配稠密的轨迹级奖励信号，无需外部验证器，可直接替代GRPO且计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在Ouro-1.4B/2.6B-Thinking模型上，RLTT相比GRPO在MATH-500、AIME24/26、BeyondAIME上平均准确率提升1.4B规模+5.8%，2.6B规模+10.9%；且训练于数学任务后能有效迁移至非数学推理基准。

Conclusion: 轨迹级赋信是提升LoopLMs强化学习效果的有效策略，RLTT为循环语言模型提供了一种轻量且高效的替代GRPO的RL框架。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) perform multi-step latent reasoning prior to token generation and outperform conventional LLMs on reasoning benchmarks at smaller parameter budgets. However, attempts to further improve LoopLM reasoning with reinforcement learning have failed - standard objectives such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) only assign credit to the final latent state, creating a fundamental mismatch with the model's internal computation. To resolve this, we introduce RLTT (Reward Latent Thought Trajectories), a reinforcement learning framework which distributes reward across the full latent reasoning trajectory. RLTT provides dense, trajectory-level credit assignment without relying on external verifiers and can directly replace GRPO with negligible overhead. Across extensive experiments with Ouro-1.4B/2.6B-Thinking under identical training and inference conditions, RLTT yields statistically significant improvements over GRPO on challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, improving mean accuracy over MATH-500, AIME24/26, and BeyondAIME by +5.8% on the 1.4B scale, and +10.9% on the 2.6B scale. Despite being trained exclusively on mathematics, RLTT also transfers effectively to non-mathematical reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness of trajectory-level credit assignment for reinforcement learning in LoopLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/jonwill8/RLTT.git.

</details>


### [410] [Collaborative Threshold Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10765)
*Tameem Bakr,Anish Ambreth,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种(t,K)-阈值水印方法，允许K个联邦学习客户端协作嵌入共享水印，只有至少t个客户端组成的联盟才能重建水印密钥并验证模型，解决了现有方案随客户端数量增多水印稀释以及单个客户端可篡改水印的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中多个客户端共同训练模型，需要机制来证明联合训练模型的来源。现有方案要么随客户端数量K增长导致水印稀释，要么给予单个客户端验证和移除水印的能力，缺乏安全且可扩展的协作水印方案。

Method: 采用(t,K)-门限水印机制：将水印密钥τ进行秘密共享，使得少于t个客户端的联盟无法重构密钥；训练过程中客户端协作嵌入共享水印；验证可在不暴露τ明文的情况下完成；在白盒设置下实现，并支持IID和非IID图像分类任务以及语言模型微调。

Result: 在K=128的规模下水印仍可检测，准确率损失极小；在自适应微调攻击（使用高达20%训练数据）下仍保持在检测阈值（z≥4）以上。

Conclusion: 门限水印机制有效解决了联邦学习中多客户端模型来源证明问题，兼顾可扩展性、安全性和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: In federated learning (FL), $K$ clients jointly train a model without sharing raw data. Because each participant invests data and compute, clients need mechanisms to later prove the provenance of a jointly trained model. Model watermarking embeds a hidden signal in the weights, but naive approaches either do not scale with many clients as per-client watermarks dilute as $K$ grows, or give any individual client the ability to verify and potentially remove the watermark. We introduce $(t,K)$-threshold watermarking: clients collaboratively embed a shared watermark during training, while only coalitions of at least $t$ clients can reconstruct the watermark key and verify a suspect model. We secret-share the watermark key $τ$ so that coalitions of fewer than $t$ clients cannot reconstruct it, and verification can be performed without revealing $τ$ in the clear. We instantiate our protocol in the white-box setting and evaluate it on image classification tasks on both IID and non-IID partitions, as well as language models fine-tuning setting. Our watermark remains detectable at scale ($K=128$) with minimal accuracy loss and stays above the detection threshold ($z\ge 4$) under attacks including adaptive fine-tuning using up to 20% of the training data. Code is available at https://github.com/tameemalaa/collaborative-threshold-watermark.

</details>


### [411] [GICDM: Mitigating Hubness for Reliable Distance-Based Generative Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16449)
*Nicolas Salvy,Hugues Talbot,Bertrand Thirion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生成模型评估中常用的高维嵌入空间存在枢纽点现象（hubness），扭曲了近邻关系并导致基于距离的指标产生偏差。作者基于经典ICDM方法提出GICDM，通过修正真实数据和生成数据的邻域估计来缓解此问题，并引入多尺度扩展以提升经验表现。实验表明GICDM能有效解决枢纽点引起的失效问题，恢复可靠的度量行为，并改善与人类评估的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型评估依赖高维嵌入空间中的距离度量，但这些空间存在枢纽点现象（hubness），导致近邻关系失真、距离指标产生偏差，影响评估可靠性。

Method: 基于经典迭代上下文相异度量（ICDM）提出生成式ICDM（GICDM），通过修正真实数据和生成数据的邻域估计来消除枢纽点偏差；同时引入多尺度扩展以改善实际性能。

Result: 在合成数据和真实基准上的大量实验表明，GICDM解决了枢纽点引起的评估失效问题，恢复了可靠的度量行为，并提升了与人类评估的对齐程度。

Conclusion: GICDM能有效缓解生成模型评估中因高维空间枢纽点现象导致的度量偏差，是提升评估可靠性和与人类判断一致性的有效方法。

Abstract: Generative model evaluation commonly relies on high-dimensional embedding spaces to compute distances between samples. We show that dataset representations in these spaces are affected by the hubness phenomenon, which distorts nearest-neighbor relationships and biases distance-based metrics. Building on the classical Iterative Contextual Dissimilarity Measure (ICDM), we introduce Generative ICDM (GICDM), a method to correct neighborhood estimation for both real and generated data. We introduce a multi-scale extension to improve empirical behavior. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real benchmarks demonstrate that GICDM resolves hubness-induced failures, restores reliable metric behavior, and improves alignment with human assessment.

</details>


### [412] [RAT+: Train Dense, Infer Sparse -- Recurrence Augmented Attention for Dilated Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18196)
*Xiuying Wei,Caglar Gulcehre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAT+是一种密集预训练架构，通过增强全序列循环和主动循环学习，使得单一模型可以在推理时灵活切换为不同扩张率的稀疏注意力模式，无需为每种配置重新训练。在1.5B参数/100B token训练下，D=16时几乎不损失精度，D=64时仅下降2-3个点；更大规模模型（2.6B和7.6B）表现更优，可在64倍注意力FLOPs和KV缓存缩减下仅损失1个点精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构化扩张注意力虽然可以通过扩张因子D在推理时灵活调整计算效率和KV缓存大小，但直接对预训练密集模型进行稀疏化会导致严重的精度下降。先前的工作需要为每种配置从头训练模型，缺乏灵活的重用能力。RAT+旨在解决单一模型在不同推理场景下灵活切换注意力稀疏模式的问题。

Method: RAT+在密集预训练阶段引入全序列循环（full-sequence recurrence）和主动循环学习（active recurrence learning）来增强标准注意力。训练完成后，模型可在推理时灵活切换为扩张注意力（可选加局部窗口）或混合层/头组合，仅需短暂的1B token分辨率适应微调，无需重新训练单独的稀疏模型。

Result: 在1.5B参数/100B token训练下，RAT+在D=16时接近密集模型精度；D=64时在常识推理和LongBench任务上仅下降约2-3个点。扩展到2.6B和7.6B参数时表现更优，平均精度仅损失1个点，同时实现64倍的注意力FLOPs和KV缓存缩减。

Conclusion: RAT+成功实现了单一密集预训练模型在推理时灵活切换不同稀疏度的注意力模式，显著降低了计算成本和KV缓存需求，同时保持较高的精度表现，且模型越大收益越明显。

Abstract: Structured dilated attention has an appealing inference-time efficiency knob: it reduces the FLOPs of attention and the KV cache size by a factor of the dilation size D, while preserving long-range connectivity. While prior work studies it by training each configuration from scratch, directly sparsifying a pretrained attention model into a dilated pattern leads to severe accuracy degradation, preventing flexible reuse across inference scenarios. We introduce RAT+, a dense-pretraining architecture that augments attention with full-sequence recurrence and active recurrence learning. A single RAT+ model is pretrained densely once and can then be flexibly switched at inference time to dilated attention (optionally with local windows) or hybrid layer/head compositions, requiring only a short 1B-token resolution adaptation rather than retraining separate sparse models. At 1.5B parameters trained on 100B tokens, RAT+ closely matches dense accuracy at D = 16, and drops by about 2-3 points at D = 64 on commonsense reasoning and LongBench tasks. We further scale to 2.6B and 7.6B parameters and observe even more promising performance (e.g., a 1-point average accuracy loss with a 64x reduction in attention FLOPs and KV cache size). Code is available at https://github.com/wimh966/rat-plus.

</details>


### [413] [Is Your Diffusion Sampler Actually Correct? A Sampler-Centric Evaluation of Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19619)
*Luhan Tang,Longxuan Yu,Shaorong Zhang,Greg Ver Steeg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 离散扩散语言模型的现有评估指标无法区分去噪器近似误差和采样器引入的误差。作者提出了一种基于oracle的框架，发现即使使用理想去噪器，少步离散扩散采样器在分布上也不正确，且NLL、GenPPL、MAUVE等指标的改进并不意味着采样正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散语言模型的评估指标将去噪器近似误差与采样动力学引入的采样器误差混为一谈，而自回归模型不存在此问题。需要一种方法隔离并量化采样器本身的误差。

Method: 提出一个以采样器为中心的oracle框架，用精确的隐马尔可夫模型后验（基于真实马尔可夫链推导）替代学习得到的去噪器，在受控设置下隔离采样器引入的误差。

Result: 即使使用oracle去噪器，少步离散扩散采样器在分布上也不正确，存在转移层面的不匹配，只有当步数接近序列长度时这种不匹配才会消失。此外，NLL、GenPPL、MAUVE等指标的改进并不意味着采样正确。

Conclusion: 现有评估指标不能反映离散扩散采样器的分布正确性，需要更好的评估方法。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) provide a fast and flexible alternative to autoregressive models (ARMs) via iterative denoising with parallel updates. However, their evaluation is challenging: existing metrics conflate denoiser approximation error with sampler-induced error from the sampling dynamics, a problem that does not arise for ARMs whose autoregressive sampling exactly reflects the learned probability model. We introduce a sampler-centric oracle framework that replaces learned denoisers with an exact Hidden Markov Model posterior derived from a ground-truth Markov chain, isolating sampler-induced error in a controlled setting. We show that few-step discrete diffusion samplers are not distributionally correct even under an oracle denoiser, with transition-level mismatch that vanishes only as the number of steps approaches the sequence length. Moreover, improvements in negative log-likelihood (NLL), generative perplexity (GenPPL), or MAUVE do not imply correct sampling. Code is available at https://luhantang.github.io/dllm_sampler

</details>


### [414] [Routing by Reaching: Composition of Pre-trained GFlowNets for Multi-Objective Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21565)
*Seokwon Yoon,Youngbin Choi,Seunghyuk Cho,Seungbeom Lee,MoonJeong Park,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在推理时组合预训练GFlowNets的框架，无需重新训练即可适应多目标奖励函数的各种组合，包括线性和非线性算子，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标GFlowNets方法需要为每个目标组合进行联合训练，这意味着目标集合的任何变化都需要从头开始重新训练，缺乏灵活性和计算效率。

Method: 提出一个在推理时组合预训练GFlowNets的框架，无需微调或重新训练即可快速适应。该框架能够处理从线性标量化到复杂非线性算子等多样化的奖励组合，并针对线性标量化精确恢复目标分布，对非线性算子通过失真因子量化近似质量。

Result: 在合成2D网格和真实分子生成任务上的实验表明，该方法达到了与基线方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为多目标GFlowNets提供了一种灵活高效的组合方案，避免了重复训练的开销，在科学发现等需要探索多个候选方案的应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) learn to sample diverse candidates in proportion to a reward function, making them well-suited for scientific discovery, where exploring multiple promising solutions is crucial. Further extending GFlowNets to multi-objective settings has attracted growing interest as real-world applications often involve multiple, conflicting objectives. However, existing approaches require joint training for each combination of objectives, meaning that any change in the objective set necessitates retraining from scratch. We propose a framework that composes pre-trained GFlowNets at inference time, enabling rapid adaptation without fine-tuning or retraining. Importantly, our framework is flexible, capable of handling diverse reward combinations ranging from linear scalarization to complex nonlinear operators, which are often handled separately in previous literature. We prove that our method exactly recovers the target distribution for linear scalarization, and quantify the approximation quality for nonlinear operators through a distortion factor. Experiments on a synthetic 2D grid and real-world molecule generation tasks demonstrate that our approach achieves performance comparable to baselines.

</details>


### [415] [Improving Full Waveform Inversion in Large Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00377)
*Yinan Feng,Peng Jin,Yuzhe Guo,Yinpeng Chen,Youzuo Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文表明，通过协调缩放模型容量、数据多样性和训练策略三个轴，一个完全在简单模拟数据上训练的十亿参数模型，能够在未见过的复杂地质基准上取得显著泛化，将SSIM从0.5844提升至0.7669，大幅缩小了数据驱动全波形反演的泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动FWI方法受限于小模型和小规模数据集，在合成数据上表现良好但无法泛化到真实复杂地质结构，存在严重的过拟合问题。

Method: 提出了一个三轴协调缩放策略：（1）模型容量缩放至十亿参数；（2）数据多样性增强；（3）优化训练策略。模型完全在模拟的简单合成数据上训练。

Result: 在OpenFWI上达到最先进水平；在Marmousi、2D SEG/EAGE Salt、Overthrust、2004 BP、Sigsbee、SEAM Phase I六个挑战性基准上，SSIM从0.5844提升至0.7669，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过适当的缩放策略，在简单合成数据上训练的大模型能够实现对更复杂真实地质结构的有效泛化，为数据驱动FWI提供了可行的范式。

Abstract: Full Waveform Inversion (FWI) is a highly nonlinear and ill-posed problem that aims to recover subsurface velocity maps from surface-recorded seismic waveforms data. Existing data-driven FWI typically uses small models, as available datasets have limited volume, geological diversity, and spatial extent, leading to substantial concerns about overfitting. Although they perform well on synthetic datasets, current methods fail to generalize to more realistic geological structures. In this work, we show that a model trained entirely on simulated and relatively simple data can generalize remarkably well to challenging and unseen geological benchmarks. We provide a working recipe that tames a billion-parameter model for FWI through coordinated scaling across three axes: model capacity, data diversity, and training strategy. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on OpenFWI and significantly narrows the generalization gap in data-driven FWI. Across six challenging geophysical benchmarks, including Marmousi, 2D SEG/EAGE Salt and Overthrust, 2004 BP, Sigsbee, and SEAM Phase I, it infers complex structures absent from the training set and delivers significant performance improvements (SSIM from 0.5844 to 0.7669). Overall, our results demonstrate that with an appropriate scaling strategy, large models trained on simple synthetic data can achieve substantial generalization to more complex and realistic geological structures.

</details>


### [416] [Relational In-Context Learning via Synthetic Pre-training with Structural Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03805)
*Yanbo Wang,Jiaxuan You,Chuan Shi,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RDB-PFN，首个完全通过合成数据训练的 relational foundation model，利用关系先验生成器生成无限多样的关系数据库，在超过200万个合成任务上预训练，通过上下文学习实现对任意新数据库的即时适应，在19个真实关系预测任务上达到强少样本性能，超越现有表格基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库缺乏类似文本或视觉领域的基础模型，主要障碍在于高质量关系数据库稀缺、私有且结构异质，无法进行互联网规模的预训练。为解决数据稀缺问题，作者提出纯合成数据训练方案。

Method: 受Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs)启发，设计Relational Prior Generator从零生成无限多样的关系数据库，在超过200万个合成单表和关系任务上预训练，使模型通过真正的上下文学习适应新数据库。

Result: 在19个真实关系预测任务上，RDB-PFN在少样本场景下优于现有最先进的表格基础模型（使用相同DFS线性化输入），同时保持轻量级架构和快速推理。

Conclusion: 纯合成数据训练范式可以有效克服关系数据库数据稀缺问题，构建出能在真实任务上达到强少样本性能的关系基础模型。

Abstract: Relational Databases (RDBs) are the backbone of modern business, yet they lack foundation models comparable to those in text or vision. A key obstacle is that high-quality RDBs are private, scarce, and structurally heterogeneous, making internet-scale pre-training infeasible. To overcome this data scarcity, we introduce RDB-PFN, the first relational foundation model trained purely via synthetic data. Inspired by Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs), where synthetic data generated from Structural Causal Models (SCMs) enables reasoning on single tables, we design a Relational Prior Generator to create an infinite stream of diverse RDBs from scratch. Pre-training on over 2 million synthetic single-table and relational tasks, RDB-PFN learns to adapt to any new database instantly via genuine in-context learning. Experiments show that RDB-PFN achieves strong few-shot performance on 19 real-world relational prediction tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art tabular foundation models evaluated on the same DFS-linearized inputs, while using a lightweight architecture and fast inference. The code is available at https://github.com/MuLabPKU/RDBPFN.

</details>


### [417] [Sparse Scheduled Diffusion Guidance for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07860)
*Abduragim Shtanchaev,Albina Ilina,Yazid Janati,Arip Asadulaev,Martin Takac,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spin提出了一种稀疏调度引导扩散逆问题求解方法，通过从中间时间步后验采样作为暖启动，并仅在调度的时间步施加轻量级像素空间校正，无需通过去噪器反向传播，在FFHQ和ImageNet上实现了2x至50x的加速和更低内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散先验用于贝叶斯逆问题时，数据一致性引导需贯穿整个反向轨迹，计算成本高昂；虽有些方法避免了向量-雅可比积，但仍需密集引导或昂贵内循环求解。

Method: Spin首先从中间时间步t*的后验时间边际采样获得暖启动状态，再执行引导反向扩散；在引导阶段仅在调度的时间步施加轻量级像素空间校正（无需通过去噪器或解码器反向传播），将先验细化与数据一致性解耦。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet上的线性和非线性逆问题中，Spin实现了与现有方法竞争的重建质量，同时运行时间加快2倍（像素空间模型）至50倍（潜在扩散模型），且内存开销更低。

Conclusion: Spin通过稀疏调度引导和暖启动策略有效降低了扩散先验在逆问题中的计算成本，证明了将先验细化与数据一致性解耦的可行性。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models are effective priors for Bayesian inverse problems, but posterior sampling with these priors is often costly because data-consistency guidance is applied throughout the full reverse trajectory. Existing methods have shown that vector-Jacobian products through the denoiser can sometimes be avoided, yet they typically still rely on dense guidance through the full trajectory or expensive inner solves. We introduce Sparse Scheduled Diffusion Guidance for Inverse Problems (Spin), a solver that avoids starting posterior sampling from pure noise. Spin first samples from a posterior time-marginal at an intermediate timestep $t_*$, and then uses that state as a warm start for a guided reverse diffusion process. At guidance time, instead of enforcing the measurement constraint at every denoising step, Spin applies lightweight corrections only at scheduled timesteps where the denoiser can still clean up artifacts. The resulting procedure decouples prior refinement from data consistency: the prior supplies denoising, while lightweight pixel-space optimization enforces the measurement constraint without backpropagation through the denoiser or decoder. Across linear and nonlinear inverse problems on FFHQ and ImageNet, Spin achieves competitive reconstruction quality with a substantially better runtime--memory profile, running 2x faster on pixel-space models and up to 50x faster on latent diffusion models, with lower memory costs.

</details>


### [418] [Enhancing LLM Training via Spectral Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14315)
*Xiaowen Jiang,Andrei Semenov,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPECTRA是一个通用优化框架，通过在更新后施加谱裁剪约束、在梯度前进行可选的谱噪声抑制，有效提升LLM预训练中多种优化器（AdamW、Signum、Mars、AdEMAMix）的验证损失，并达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自适应优化器（如AdamW）未考虑权重和梯度的谱结构，导致LLM训练中两个问题：（i）优化器更新的谱范数过大，破坏训练稳定性并损害泛化；（ii）随机梯度噪声存在稀疏谱尖峰，少数奇异值远大于其余值。

Method: 提出SPECTRA框架，包含：（i）后谱裁剪（post-spectral clipping）：对更新施加谱范数约束；（ii）前谱裁剪（pre-spectral clipping）：可选地抑制梯度中的谱噪声尖峰。理论证明后裁剪是一种具有谱范数约束和权重正则化的Composite Frank-Wolfe方法。通过Newton-Schulz迭代实现高效的软谱裁剪，避免昂贵的SVD分解。

Result: 在LLM预训练实验中，SPECTRA对AdamW、Signum、Mars、AdEMAMix等多种优化器均一致地提升了验证损失，最优变体达到SOTA结果。训练出的模型权重范数更小，验证了谱裁剪与正则化之间的关联。

Conclusion: SPECTRA是一种通用的、即插即用的优化增强框架，通过谱结构约束有效提升LLM训练的稳定性和泛化性能，且计算开销可控。

Abstract: While spectral-based optimizers like Muon operate directly on the spectrum of updates, standard adaptive methods such as AdamW do not account for the spectral structure of weights and gradients, leaving them vulnerable to two empirical issues in large language model (LLM) training: (i) the optimizer updates can have large spectral norms, potentially destabilizing training and degrading generalization; (ii) stochastic gradient noise can exhibit sparse spectral spikes, with a few dominant singular values much larger than the rest. We propose SPECTRA, a general framework addressing these by (i) post-spectral clipping of updates to enforce spectral-norm constraints (ii) optional pre-spectral clipping of gradients to suppress spectral noise spikes. We prove that post-clipping constitutes a Composite Frank-Wolfe method with spectral-norm constraints and weight regularization. We further analyze how pre-clipping mitigates sparse spectral spikes. We propose efficient soft spectral clipping via Newton-Schulz iterations, avoiding expensive SVD. Experiments on LLM pretraining show SPECTRA uniformly improves validation loss for various optimizers, including AdamW, Signum, Mars, and AdEMAMix, with the best-performing variants achieving state-of-the-art results. Models trained with SPECTRA exhibit smaller weight norms, confirming the link between spectral clipping and regularization.

</details>


### [419] [Maximizing Mutual Information Between Prompt and Response Improves LLM Performance With No Additional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19294)
*Hyunji Nam,Haoran Li,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出互信息偏好优化（MIPO）方法，通过对比数据增强构建偏好对，在不依赖外部监督的情况下提升LLM在可验证和非可验证任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法严重依赖人工标注数据或外部验证器，已有数据已被充分利用，新数据收集成本高，且真正的智能远超可验证任务，需要一种对外部信号依赖更少、适用范围更广的自我改进框架。

Method: MIPO通过对比数据增强构建偏好对：正确提示下生成正样本回复，随机无关提示下生成负样本回复；然后使用直接偏好优化（DPO）学习该配对数据，从而最大化基础LLM下提示与回复之间的点互信息。

Result: 在1-7B参数的Llama和Qwen指令模型上，MIPO在个性化任务上相比提示基线取得3-16%的提升（Qwen2.5-1B-Instruct提升51%）；在数学和多项选择题回答等可验证领域，无需额外数据和外部监督即可获得1-20%的提升。

Conclusion: 通过对比数据对导出的内在信号进行自我改进是一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: While post-training has successfully improved large language models (LLMs) across a variety of domains, these gains heavily rely on human-labeled data or external verifiers. Existing data has already been exploited, and new data is expensive to collect. Moreover, true intelligence goes far beyond verifiable tasks. Therefore, we need self-improvement frameworks that are less dependent on external signals and more broadly applicable to both verifiable and non-verifiable domains. We propose **Mutual Information Preference Optimization (MIPO)**, a contrastive data augmentation method that constructs preference pairs by generating a positive response conditioning on the correct prompt, and a negative response by conditioning on a random, unrelated prompt. We show that using Direct Preference Optimization to learn from this paired data maximizes pointwise mutual information *under the base LLM* between prompts and model responses. Experiments with with 1-7B parameter Llama and Qwen instruct models show that MIPO achieves 3-16% gains (and 51% increase for Qwen2.5-1B-Instruct) on personalization compared to prompting baselines. Surprisingly, MIPO can also be useful in verifiable domains, such as math and multiple-choice question answering, yielding 1-20% gains *without any additional data or external supervision*. These results suggest a promising direction for self-improvement using intrinsic signals derived from contrastive data pairs.

</details>


### [420] [Path-Space Mirror Descent for On-Policy Reinforcement Learning under the Generalized Schrödinger Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21621)
*Yuehu Gong,Zeyuan Wang,Yulin Chen,Shutong Ding,Qingyuan Zhou,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于扩散/流模型的生成式策略在在线策略优化中无法直接计算终端动作似然的问题，提出GSB-MDPO方法，将在线策略生成式策略优化形式化为广义薛定谔桥问题，利用路径空间KL散度作为近端项来约束动作分布，无需显式终端似然评估。在14个连续控制任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典在线策略算法（如PPO）依赖可处理的动作似然进行近端更新，但采用简单高斯策略表达力有限；而扩散/流模型生成式策略虽更具表达力，但其终端动作密度难以处理，与基于似然的在线策略近端更新不匹配。

Method: 将在线策略生成式策略优化建模为广义薛定谔桥（GSB）问题，通过镜面下降策略优化（MDPO）实例化路径测度更新；利用GSB路径空间KL散度作为MDPO中的近端项，同时上界约束终端动作KL，无需显式终端动作似然评估。

Result: 在Playground和Gym-MuJoCo的14个连续控制任务上验证了GSB-MDPO的 empirical 有效性，支持路径空间正则化作为多步生成式策略的原则性近端更新。

Conclusion: 路径空间KL正则化是一种 principled 的在线策略近端更新方式，能够有效弥合生成式策略表达力与在线策略可训练性之间的差距。

Abstract: Classical on-policy algorithms such as PPO and mirror descent policy optimization provide stable proximal policy updates through tractable action likelihoods, but are typically instantiated with simple Gaussian policies whose expressiveness can be limited in complex continuous-control tasks. Generative policies based on diffusion and flow models provide more expressive action distributions, but they naturally define distributions over multi-step denoising paths whose terminal action density is often intractable, creating a mismatch with likelihood-based on-policy proximal updates. To address this mismatch, we introduce \textbf{GSB-MDPO} (\emph{Generalized Schrödinger Bridge Mirror Descent Policy Optimization}), which formulates on-policy generative policy optimization as a Generalized Schrödinger Bridge problem over state-conditioned generation paths and instantiates the resulting path-measure update through mirror descent policy optimization. The key insight is that the GSB path-space KL plays the role of the proximal term in MDPO while upper-bounding the terminal action KL, enabling direct control of the executed action distribution without explicit terminal action likelihood evaluation. Experiments on 14 continuous-control tasks across Playground and Gym-MuJoCo demonstrate the empirical effectiveness of GSB-MDPO and support path-space regularization as a principled proximal update for multi-step generative policies.

</details>


### [421] [Learning Safely Without Knowing the World:COMPASS-Hedge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22348)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPASS-Hedge算法，这是首个在全信息在线学习场景下同时实现对抗环境中的极小极大最优遗憾、随机环境中的实例最优间隙相关遗憾，以及相对于固定基线策略的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$遗憾的"三世界最优"算法，且无需任何先验参数知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习算法面临一个根本性三难困境：在对抗性和随机性两种环境下同时保证良好的遗憾界，并提供相对于固定比较器的基线安全性。现有方法只能在一或两个领域表现出色，无法在不牺牲最优速率或需要问题参数先验知识的情况下统一所有三个目标。

Method: COMPASS-Hedge的核心创新包括：(1) 自适应伪遗憾缩放（adaptive pseudo-regret scaling）；(2) 基于阶段的激进度控制（phase-based aggression）；(3) 比较器感知的混合策略（comparator-aware mixing strategy）。三者有机结合实现了参数自由的"三世界最优"保证。

Result: 在全信息设定下，COMPASS-Hedge同时实现了（至多对数因子）：(i) 对抗环境中的极小极大最优遗憾；(ii) 随机环境中依赖间隙的实例最优遗憾；(iii) 相对于指定基线策略的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$遗憾。这是首个实现此类"三世界最优"保证的全信息在线学习算法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基线安全性不必以牺牲最坏情况鲁棒性或随机效率为代价，为在线学习算法的设计提供了新的理论下限和上限统一框架。

Abstract: Online learning algorithms often face a fundamental trilemma: balancing regret guarantees between adversarial and stochastic settings and providing baseline safety against a fixed comparator. While existing methods excel in one or two of these regimes, they typically fail to unify all three without sacrificing optimal rates or requiring oracle access to problem-dependent parameters. In this work, we bridge this gap by introducing COMPASS-Hedge. To the best of our knowledge, our algorithm is the first full-information anytime method to simultaneously achieve, up to logarithmic factors: i) minimax-optimal regret in adversarial environments; ii) instance-optimal, gap-dependent regret in stochastic environments; and iii) $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$ regret relative to a designated baseline policy. Crucially, COMPASS-Hedge is parameter-free and requires no prior knowledge of the environment's nature or the magnitude of the stochastic suboptimality gaps. Our approach hinges on a novel integration of adaptive pseudo-regret scaling and phase-based aggression, coupled with a comparator-aware mixing strategy. To the best of our knowledge, this provides the first "best-of-three-world" guarantee in the full-information setting, establishing that baseline safety does not have to come at the cost of worst-case robustness or stochastic efficiency.

</details>


### [422] [Negative Ontology of True Target for Machine Learning: Towards Evaluation and Learning under Democratic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24824)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从哲学角度审视机器学习中“真实目标”存在性假设的转变，提出基于“真实目标不存在”的本体论立场，定义了“民主监督”范式，并构建了基于多重不准确真实目标（MIATTs）的评估与学习框架（EL-MIATTs），应用于教育及职业发展领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流机器学习范式默认假设真实目标（TT）客观存在，但作者认为这一假设在现实世界中不成立。本文旨在通过否定TT的客观存在性，提出新的学习范式（民主监督），以突破传统监督学习的局限。

Method: 1) 采用否定本体论视角，明确假设TT在现实世界中不存在；2) 基于此假设定义民主监督（Democratic Supervision）；3) 提出多重不准确真实目标（MIATTs）作为民主监督的实例级实现；4) 推导MIATTs的逻辑驱动生成与评估原则、逻辑评估公式，以及不可定义真实目标学习策略；5) 构建评估与学习框架（EL-MIATTs）。

Result: 建立了EL-MIATTs框架；通过真实世界应用验证了该框架在支持个人教育与职业发展方面的潜力，并与教育及职业发展领域的民主监督理论相呼应。

Conclusion: 否认TT客观存在的民主监督范式可以有效指导机器学习预测建模，EL-MIATTs框架在实践中具有可行性，尤其适用于需要个性化、多元评估的教育与职业发展场景。

Abstract: This article philosophically examines how shifts in assumptions regarding the existence and non-existence of the true target (TT) give rise to new perspectives and insights for machine learning (ML)-based predictive modeling and, correspondingly, proposes a knowledge system for evaluation and learning under Democratic Supervision. By systematically analysing the existence assumption of the TT in current mainstream ML paradigms, we explicitly adopt a negative ontology perspective, positing that the TT does not objectively exist in the real world, and, grounded in this non-existence assumption, define Democratic Supervision for ML. We further present Multiple Inaccurate True Targets (MIATTs) as an instance-level realization of Democratic Supervision. Building upon MIATTs, we derive principles, for the logic-driven generation and assessment of MIATTs, a logical assessment formulation for evaluation with MIATTs, and undefinable true target learning for learning with MIATTs. Based on these components, we establish the evaluation and learning with MIATTs (EL-MIATTs) framework for ML-based predictive modelling. A real-world application demonstrates the potential of the proposed EL-MIATTs framework in supporting education and professional development for individuals, aligning with prior discussions of Democratic Supervision in the fields of education and professional development.

</details>


### [423] [CompleteRXN: Toward Completing Open Chemical Reaction Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00222)
*Gabriel Vogel,Minouk Noordsij,Evgeny Pidko,Jana M. Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompleteRXN，一个大规模反应补全基准，并设计了带约束解码的CRB模型，在基准上达到99.20%准确率，但实际场景中性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学反应数据集（如USPTO）普遍存在副产品、共反应物和化学计量系数缺失的问题，严重限制了其在下游任务中的适用性和可靠性。

Method: 构建CompleteRXN基准：将USPTO记录与精心整理的机理反应对齐，生成对齐的不完整反应与原子平衡反应对。提出CRB（Constrained Reaction Balancer），一种带约束解码的编码器-解码器反应补全模型。

Result: CRB在随机划分上达99.20%的等价准确率，在极端分布外划分上达91.12%；SynRBL生成更多平衡且化学合理的补全但准确率较低；所有方法在不完整度增加时性能下降，在未筛选的完整USPTO上性能大幅降低。

Conclusion: CompleteRXN可作为反应补全的标准化评估基准；CRB在基准上表现优异但实际鲁棒性不足，揭示了基准性能与实用鲁棒性之间的差距，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Chemical reaction datasets such as USPTO suffer from substantial incompleteness, frequently missing byproducts, co-reactants, and stoichiometric coefficients. This limits their applicability and reliability in downstream applications. Here, we introduce CompleteRXN, a large-scale supervised benchmark for reaction completion under realistic missing-data conditions. We construct a dataset of aligned incomplete and atom-balanced reactions by mapping USPTO records to curated mechanistic reactions. We evaluate representative baselines, including a novel encoder-decoder reaction completion model with constrained decoding, the Constrained Reaction Balancer (CRB), and a recent algorithmic method, SynRBL. On our CompleteRXN benchmark, the CRB achieves high performance across splits of increasing difficulty, reaching 99.20% equivalence accuracy on the random split and 91.12% on the extreme out-of-distribution split. SynRBL produces many balanced and chemically plausible completions, but with lower accuracy on the benchmark test splits. Across all methods, performance degrades with increasing incompleteness. We observe a substantial drop when evaluating on reactions outside the benchmark (full uncurated USPTO), highlighting the gap between benchmark performance and practical robustness and motivating future work.

</details>


### [424] [Statistical Consistency and Generalization of Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02116)
*Yuanfan Li,Xiyuan Wei,Tianbao Yang,Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了对比表示学习（CRL）的统一统计学习理论。在下游任务中，证明了对比损失与最优排序统计一致，并建立了校准型不等式来量化对比风险与检索次优性之间的关系。在上游训练中，针对监督和自监督对比目标，分别推导了泛化界O(1/m + 1/√n)和O(1/√m + 1/√n)，解释了大规模负样本集的经验优势以及m与n之间的显式权衡，实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比表示学习理论存在三个关键缺陷：(i) 统计一致性尚不明确；(ii) 泛化界随负样本数增加而恶化，与经验观察相悖；(iii) CRL的检索性能缺乏理论关注。

Method: 使用AUC型群体准则评估检索质量，证明对比损失的统计一致性；建立校准型不等式；推导监督和自监督两种场景下的泛化界，其中m为负样本数，n为锚点数。

Result: 监督对比学习的泛化界为O(1/m + 1/√n)，自监督对比学习的泛化界为O(1/√m + 1/√n)，揭示了m和n之间明确的权衡关系，并通过大规模视觉-语言模型实验验证。

Conclusion: 该统一理论弥补了CRL统计学习理论的空白，解决了泛化界与经验观察的矛盾，并为大规模负样本和锚点数量的平衡提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Contrastive representation learning (CRL) underpins many modern foundation models. Despite recent theoretical progress, existing analyses suffer from several key limitations: (i) the statistical consistency of CRL remains poorly understood; (ii) available generalization bounds deteriorate as the number of negative samples increases, contradicting the empirical benefits of large negative sets; and (iii) the retrieval performance of CRL has received limited theoretical attention. In this paper, we develop a unified statistical learning theory for CRL. For downstream tasks, we evaluate retrieval quality using an AUC-type population criterion and show that the contrastive loss is \emph{statistically consistent} with optimal ranking. We further establish a \emph{calibration-style inequality} that quantitatively relates excess contrastive risk to excess retrieval suboptimality. For upstream training, we study both supervised and self-supervised contrastive objectives and derive generalization bounds of order $O(1/m + 1/\sqrt{n})$ and $O(1/\sqrt{m} + 1/\sqrt{n})$, respectively, where $m$ denotes the number of negative samples and $n$ the number of anchor points. These bounds not only explain the empirical advantages of large negative sets but also reveal an explicit trade-off between $m$ and $n$. Extensive experiments on large-scale vision--language models corroborate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [425] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新解读了顺序无关自回归模型在缺失数据场景下的应用，提出了首个可直接在不完整数据上训练的理论框架，并利用其条件密度估计能力实现主动信息获取。实验表明所提MO-ARM模型在多个真实基准上优于传统插补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但其在缺失数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，从缺失数据的视角重新审视该类模型。

Method: 1) 证明了完整数据上的标准训练过程隐式执行了完全随机缺失机制下的插补；2) 提出了首个在一般缺失机制下直接在不完整数据上训练的理论框架；3) 利用其摊销条件密度估计能力进行主动信息获取，即顺序选择最具信息量的缺失变量。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上，所提出的MO-ARM模型在插补任务上一致优于已有的基线方法。

Conclusion: 顺序无关自回归模型可以被重新解释为缺失数据模型，并且能够在不完整数据上直接训练，同时在主动信息获取场景中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [426] [Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07804)
*Zhicheng Yang,Zhijiang Guo,Yifan Song,Minrui Xu,Yongxin Wang,Yiwei Wang,Xiaodan Liang,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prune-OPD通过动态检测学生与教师模型之间的前缀漂移，在训练过程中自动截断低质量轨迹并重新分配计算资源，从而在保持甚至提升性能的前提下，将推理模型的蒸馏训练时间减少37.6%-68.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation（OPD）在长程推理任务中面临关键缺陷：当学生生成的前缀不可避免地偏离教师思路时，教师提供的密集奖励信号会失去局部可剥削性，继续在此类漂移轨迹上生成和评估token不仅降低奖励质量，还造成大量计算浪费。

Method: Prune-OPD通过持续监控学生与教师预测之间的局部兼容性（如top-k重叠度），实时检测前缀漂移事件。一旦检测到严重漂移，即单调降低后续不可靠奖励的权重，并触发动态轨迹截断，从而停止无效生成，将计算资源重新分配给可靠的教师监督。

Result: 在多种教师-学生组合上，Prune-OPD在保持并常提升AMC、AIME、HMMT等基准性能的同时，将训练时间减少37.6%-68.0%。当学生-教师兼容性高时，它通过扩展训练窗口自动保留长上下文监督。

Conclusion: Prune-OPD改进OPD的方式并非盲目缩短rollout长度，而是将计算资源重新分配给局部可剥削的教师奖励，实现了计算与监督可靠性的动态对齐。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) leverages dense teacher rewards to enhance reasoning models. However, scaling OPD to long-horizon tasks exposes a critical flaw: as the student's generated prefix inevitably diverges from the teacher's thought process, the teacher's dense reward loses local exploitability. Continuing to generate and evaluate tokens on these ``drifted'' trajectories not only degrades reward quality but also incurs massive computational waste. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Prune-OPD}, a framework that dynamically aligns training budgets with supervision quality. By continuously monitoring the local compatibility between student and teacher predictions (e.g., via top-$k$ overlap), Prune-OPD detects prefix-drift events in real time. Upon detecting severe drift, it monotonically down-weights subsequent unreliable rewards and triggers dynamic rollout truncation. This allows the training process to halt futile generation and reallocate compute strictly to reliable teacher supervision. Across diverse teacher-student combinations, Prune-OPD consistently aligns computation with supervision reliability. When prefix drift makes dense teacher rewards unreliable, it reduces training time by 37.6\%--68.0\% while preserving, and often improving, performance on challenging benchmarks (AMC, AIME, HMMT). When student-teacher compatibility remains high, it automatically preserves long-context supervision by expanding the training window. These results suggest that Prune-OPD improves OPD not by blindly shortening rollouts, but by reallocating computation toward locally exploitable teacher rewards.

</details>


### [427] [PRIM: Meta-Learned Bayesian Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08786)
*Christopher Lohse,Anish Dhir,Amadou Ba,Bradley Eck,Marco Ruffini,Jonas Wahl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIM是一种基于因果元学习的方法，将根因分析建模为贝叶斯推断任务，通过合成因果模型先验的边缘化来隐式识别分布变化，实现零样本推断（17ms处理100变量系统），在多个基准上达到与已知因果结构方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的根因分析面临三个主要挑战：多变量间的错误传播、对结构因果知识的需求以及测试时推断的计算成本高昂。现有方法要么需要已知的因果图结构，要么计算开销大，难以在实际场景中高效部署。

Method: PRIM采用仿真基础的元学习范式，核心组件是MACE（Model-Averaged Causal Estimation）Transformer神经过程。该方法在合成因果模型先验上进行训练，通过边缘化结构不确定性来隐式学习因果关系，同时联合关注观测样本、异常样本和节点间的因果结构，无需在测试时进行模型拟合或显式统计检验。

Result: 在合成基准和两个真实基准数据集（PetShop和CausRCA）上，PRIM与预先知道因果图结构的方法性能相当，同时在多个任务上超越了对图结构无知的方法。系统可在17毫秒内完成对多达100个变量的零样本推断，且通过轻量级微调可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: PRIM通过元学习范式有效解决了RCA中的结构不确定性问题和计算效率问题，实现了无需因果先验知识的零样本快速推断。该方法在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了测试时计算成本，并具有良好的领域适应能力。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in complex systems is challenging due to error propagation across multiple variables, the need for structural causal knowledge, and the computational cost of inference at test time. We introduce PRIM (Prior-fitted Root cause Identification with Meta-learning), a causal meta-learning approach that frames RCA as a Bayesian inference task over a synthetic prior of causal models. By marginalising out structural uncertainty, PRIM implicitly identifies changes in the data-generating mechanism between baseline and anomalous periods. In doing so, PRIM infers distributional differences without explicit statistical testing, and implicitly learns causal structure without model fitting at test time. Following the simulation-based meta-learning paradigm of prior-fitted networks, PRIM uses a Model-Averaged Causal Estimation (MACE) transformer neural process that jointly attends over observational and anomalous samples and the causal structure of nodes, enabling zero-shot inference in 17,ms for systems with up to 100 variables. Across synthetic benchmarks and two realistic benchmark datasets, PetShop and CausRCA, PRIM is competitive with methods that are aware of the system's causal graphical structure a priori while outperforming graph-unaware methods on several tasks. Lightweight fine-tuning to specific domains and data dynamics improves performance further.

</details>


### [428] [Inpainting physics: self-supervised learning for context-driven fluid simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08832)
*Jonas Weidner,Yeray Martin-Ruisanchez,Daniel Rueckert,Benedikt Wiestler,Julian Suk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将稳态CFD推理重构成图像修复问题，通过自监督先验和局部邻域分词器，在颅内动脉瘤血流动力学中实现边界条件变化和局部几何编辑下的高效流场重建，性能超越传统监督式神经替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经替代模型作为前向算子，紧密绑定训练时的显式条件变量（如几何、边界条件），在边界条件偏移或局部几何改变时难以复用，限制了泛化能力。

Method: 将CFD推理重构为图像修复问题：学习速度场的自监督先验，推理时通过固定已知区域（入口、出口、未变区域）施加边界约束；引入局部邻域分词器将高分辨率速度场压缩为空间潜在token，训练潜在流匹配和掩码自编码器模型。

Result: 在颅内动脉瘤血流动力学中，该方法能从稀疏边界上下文中重建完整速度场，在边界条件偏移和数据集偏移下优于监督式神经替代模型，并支持通过复用未变模拟上下文进行局部几何编辑。

Conclusion: 将CFD推理视为上下文条件化的图像修复，可将神经替代模型从任务专用预测器转变为可复用的流场先验。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models for computational fluid dynamics (CFD) are typically trained as forward operators that map explicit problem specifications, such as geometry and boundary conditions, to solution fields. This ties the model to the conditioning variables seen during training and limits reuse under boundary-condition shifts or local geometry changes. We propose to reformulate steady CFD inference as an inpainting problem: instead of training on explicit boundary conditions, we learn a self-supervised prior over velocity fields and impose boundary constraints only during inference by fixing known regions such as inlet, outlet or unchanged regions from previous simulations. To scale this idea to large 3D meshes, we introduce a local neighbourhood tokeniser that represents high-resolution velocity fields as compact spatial latent tokens and train latent flow-matching and masked-autoencoder models on these tokens. On intracranial aneurysm hemodynamics, our method reconstructs full velocity fields from sparse boundary context, outperforms supervised neural surrogates under boundary-condition and dataset shift and enables local geometry editing by reusing unchanged simulation context. These results suggest that viewing CFD inference as context-conditioned inpainting can turn neural surrogates from task-specific predictors into reusable flow priors.

</details>


### [429] [TopoGeoScore: A Self-Supervised Source-Only Geometric Framework for OOD Checkpoint Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08870)
*Farid Hazratian,Ali Zia,Hien Duy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoGeoScore，一种仅使用源域表示（无目标样本或标签）的无监督鲁棒性检查点选择方法。通过构造类条件互KNN图，提取扭转启发的拉普拉斯对数行列式、Ollivier-Ricci曲率和高阶拓扑摘要三类可解释信号，并利用自监督目标学习非负线性评分，在CIFAR、ImageNet-C、MNLI→HANS和OGBN-Arxiv等基准上验证了源域表示中包含可测量的拓扑证据，支持部署前的检查点选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标域标签不可用的情况下，诊断模型在分布外（OOD）场景下的鲁棒性十分困难。现有方法通常需要目标样本或标签，而本文希望在更严格的源域单样本（source-only）设定下，仅利用源域表示实现无标签的鲁棒检查点选择。

Method: 1) 利用源域嵌入构造类条件互K近邻图；2) 提取三类可解释几何-拓扑信号：扭转启发的简化拉普拉斯对数行列式（全局流形复杂度）、Ollivier-Ricci曲率（局部邻域规则性）、高阶拓扑摘要（碎片化连接、环路、全局-局部不一致性）；3) 通过自监督目标学习非负线性评分，该目标强制近似几何保持嵌入视图下的评分不变性，同时与破坏结构的视图分离。

Result: 在CIFAR系列（含Corruption和分布偏移基准）、ImageNet-C、MNLI→HANS迁移以及OGBN-Arxiv数据集上验证了方法的有效性，表明源域表示中存在可测量的全局-局部-拓扑鲁棒性证据。

Conclusion: TopoGeoScore能够在无目标域样本和标签的条件下进行有效的鲁棒检查点选择，评分保持可解释性，为部署前面对分布偏移时的模型选择提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness is difficult to diagnose when target-domain labels are unavailable. We consider a more restrictive source-only variant of unsupervised accuracy estimation: selecting robust checkpoints using only source-domain representations, with no target samples or target labels. We propose \textbf{TopoGeoScore}, a source-only geometric scorer for label-free OOD checkpoint selection. Given a trained checkpoint, we construct class-conditional mutual $k$-nearest-neighbour graphs from source embeddings and extract three interpretable signals: a torsion-inspired reduced Laplacian log-determinant for global class-manifold complexity, Ollivier--Ricci curvature for local neighbourhood regularity, and higher-order topological summaries for fragmented connectivity, loops, and global--local inconsistency. Instead of fixing their weights by hand, TopoGeoScore learns a non-negative linear score through a self-supervised objective that enforces invariance under approximately geometry-preserving embedding views and separation from structure-breaking views. The score remains interpretable and uses no target-domain samples or labels. Results across CIFAR-based corruption and distribution-shift benchmarks, ImageNet-C, MNLI$\to$HANS transfer, and OGBN-Arxiv suggest that source representations contain measurable global--local--topological evidence of robustness, supporting practical checkpoint selection before deployment under distribution shift.

</details>


### [430] [Nearly-Optimal Algorithm for Adversarial Kernelized Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10299)
*Shogo Iwazaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗环境下的核化强盗（高斯过程强盗）问题，提出指数权重算法实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$的对抗遗憾界，对于SE和Matérn核证明了算法无关下界以确认最优性，并基于Nyström近似给出了计算高效的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核化强盗研究主要关注随机环境，但在实际应用中（如在线广告、推荐系统）奖励函数可能被对抗性选择，缺乏对抗环境下核化强盗的理论保证。

Method: 采用指数权重（Exp3类）算法进行对抗性核化强盗决策；利用Nyström近似实现计算高效的算法变体。

Result: 证明了指数权重算法达到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$的对抗遗憾界；对SE和Matérn核给出了算法无关下界，证明算法在多项式对数因子意义下最优。

Conclusion: 指数权重算法在对抗环境下对核化强盗问题是最优的（至多多项式对数因子差距），且可通过Nyström近似实现高效计算。

Abstract: This paper studies kernelized bandits (also known as Gaussian process bandits) in an adversarial environment, where the reward functions in a known reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) may be adversarially chosen at each round. We show that the exponential-weight algorithm achieves $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$ adversarial regret, where $T$ and $γ_T$ denote the number of total rounds and the maximum information gain, respectively. For squared exponential (SE) and $ν$-Matérn kernels, we also show algorithm-independent lower bounds that guarantee the optimality of our algorithm up to polylogarithmic factors. Furthermore, we present a computationally efficient variant of our algorithm using Nyström approximation while maintaining nearly optimal regret guarantees.

</details>


### [431] [Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization for On-Policy Reasoning Distillation under Large Policy Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13230)
*Xinyu Liu,Kechen Jiao,Chunyang Xiao,Runsong Zhao,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Jiahao Liu,Qifan Wang,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Chenglong Wang,Tong Xiao,JingBo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TGPO方法，通过教师直接指导学生生成的token级别生成，结合RLVR轨迹奖励，解决了大策略差异下反向KL蒸馏失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反向KL的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法在教师-学生策略差异较大时，RL驱动的探索会产生教师分布外的轨迹，导致无效的负反馈。

Method: TGPO采用教师直接指导token级别生成（基于学生生成的上下文），并结合RLVR风格的轨迹级别奖励，引导探索朝向改进的续接方向。

Result: 在推理基准上，TGPO持续优于现有基于反向KL的OPD方法，且在不同教师模型下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: TGPO作为在线策略推理蒸馏方法，在大策略差异场景下仍然有效，比传统RKL方法更鲁棒。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a promising paradigm for reasoning-oriented post-training of large language models (LLMs), especially when combined with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR). Existing OPD methods rely on reverse KL (RKL)-based teacher supervision over trajectories sampled from the student policy. However, we identify a critical limitation: under large teacher--student policy divergence, RL-driven exploration often produces trajectories outside the teacher distribution, resulting in uninformative negative feedback. To address this, we propose Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO), an on-policy reasoning distillation method that remains effective under large policy divergence settings. Rather than relying solely on evaluative supervision, TGPO uses teacher to directly guide token level generation conditioning on student-generated contexts; together with RLVR-style trajectory level rewards, TGPO steers exploration toward improved continuations. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that TGPO consistently outperforms existing RKL-based OPD methods and remains robust across different teacher models.

</details>


### [432] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一个在表格数据预测领域扩展至百万行数据、显著提升性能与速度的基础模型，在多项基准测试中超越现有方法，并引入了测试时计算扩展等新功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在科学和工业领域中支撑着大多数高价值预测问题。TabPFN已经推动了该模态的基础模型革命，但仍需要在更大规模数据集上的性能提升以及训练和推理效率的改进。

Method: TabPFN-3完全使用先验生成的合成数据进行预训练，通过减少KV缓存和行分块技术扩展到100万行数据，引入测试时计算扩展以提升推理性能，并针对时序、关系型和表格-文本数据开发了专门检查点。

Result: 在TabArena基准上，单次前向传播显著优于所有其他模型（包括调参和集成的基线）；在多样数据集上排名第一；TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) 超过非TabPFN模型200 Elo以上，在最大数据子集上达420 Elo；比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍；在关系型数据（RelBenchV1）、表格-文本数据（TabSTAR）上达到新SOTA；时序检查点fev-bench排名第二；SHAP值计算加速120倍；整体比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3通过规模扩展、速度提升和新能力扩展，显著推动了表格数据预测的前沿，为表格基础模型设立了新标杆。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [433] [Turning Stale Gradients into Stable Gradients: Coherent Coordinate Descent with Implicit Landscape Smoothing for Lightweight Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14373)
*Chen Liang,Xiatao Sun,Qian Wang,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD)，一种确定性、样本高效且预算感知的零阶优化器。通过利用梯度相干性将历史（过时）梯度转化为计算资产，CoCD实现了每步O(1)的查询复杂度，同时保持全局下降方向。实验表明，CoCD在样本效率和收敛稳定性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化方法面临样本效率低（如标准有限差分）或方差大（如随机子空间方法）的困境，亟需一种兼具样本高效性和低方差的确定性优化方法。

Method: 提出CoCD方法，形式化梯度相干性概念，证明其等价于带"热启动"的块循环坐标下降（BCCD），将历史（过时）梯度从负担转化为计算资产；理论推导了更大有限差分步长可产生隐式平滑效应，降低有效光滑常数以提升收敛稳定性。

Result: 在MLP、CNN和ResNet（最高27万参数）上，CoCD在样本效率和收敛损失/准确率上显著优于BCCD，且比随机零阶方法具有更优越的稳定性。

Conclusion: 确定性、结构感知的更新为轻量级零阶优化提供了优于随机化的替代方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization is pivotal for scenarios where backpropagation is unavailable, such as memory-constrained on-device learning and black-box optimization. However, existing methods face a stark trade-off: they are either sample-inefficient (e.g., standard finite differences) or suffer from high variance due to randomized estimation (e.g., random subspace methods). In this work, we propose Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD), a deterministic, sample-efficient, and budget-aware ZO optimizer. Theoretically, we formalize the notion of gradient coherence and demonstrate that CoCD is equivalent to Block Cyclic Coordinate Descent (BCCD) with ``warm starts,'' effectively converting historical (stale) gradients from a liability into a computational asset. This mechanism enables $O(1)$ query complexity per step while maintaining global descent directions. Furthermore, we derive error bounds revealing a counter-intuitive insight: larger finite-difference step sizes can induce an implicit smoothing effect on the optimization landscape by reducing the effective smoothness constant, thereby improving convergence stability. Experiments on MLP, CNN, and ResNet architectures (up to 270k parameters) demonstrate that CoCD significantly outperforms BCCD in terms of sample efficiency and convergence loss/accuracy, and exhibits superior stability over randomized ZO methods. Our results suggest that deterministic, structure-aware updates offer a superior alternative to randomization for lightweight ZO optimization.

</details>


### [434] [To MRL or not to MRL: Text Embeddings are Robust to Truncation Without Matryoshka Learning, Except In Heavy Truncation Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16608)
*Sotaro Takeshita,Yurina Takeshita,Simone Paolo Ponzetto,Daniel Ruffinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了MRL训练方法对文本嵌入截断鲁棒性的影响，发现非MRL模型在截断幅度不超过80%时，其性能与MRL模型相当甚至更优，表明截断鲁棒性未必源于MRL训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRL是一种广泛用于训练文本编码器的方法，旨在提供不同大小的有用文本表示。然而，已有研究表明随机截断嵌入对性能影响不大，但尚无工作直接比较随机截断与MRL的有效性，因此需要明确两者作为嵌入压缩方法的优劣。

Method: 将MRL使用的相同截断策略分别应用于经MRL训练和未经MRL训练的模型，在多个模型和下游任务上进行对比实验。

Result: 除非进行重度截断（缩减至少80%），非MRL模型的截断嵌入与MRL模型性能相当甚至更优；MRL的优势仅在重度截断场景下显现。

Conclusion: 截断鲁棒性不一定来自MRL训练，是否采用MRL取决于是否需要支持重度截断（缩减80%以上）的场景。

Abstract: Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) is a widely adopted approach for training text encoders so they provide useful text representations at various sizes, available by simply truncating the resulting vectors at sizes pre-determined at training time. Recent works have shown that randomly truncating text embeddings has minimal impact in downstream performance unless vectors are reduced in size by at least 70%, suggesting that embeddings are already robust to truncation without the use of MRL. However, no prior work has compared random truncation to MRL, so it is unclear how the two methods compare as effective embedding reduction methods. In this paper, we study this by applying the same truncation used by MRL to models trained with and without MRL. Our results across several models and downstream tasks show that, unless heavily truncating embeddings (i.e. reducing their size by at least 80%), truncated embeddings of non-MRL models are competitive with, and often outperform models trained with MRL. This suggests that truncation robustness may not necessarily come from MRL, and that the choice of spending the additional training cost of MRL depends on whether heavy truncation is desired. We make our code available for reproduction.

</details>


### [435] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出马特罗什卡概念瓶颈模型(MCBM)，通过嵌套层次化概念组织实现自适应概念利用，将预期干预成本从线性降至对数级别O(log K)，并保证单调性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型(CBM)在测试时干预成本高昂，传统方法要么使用单一静态概念集导致专家需穷举标注所有概念，要么针对不同概念预算训练多个模型造成计算和维护开销巨大。

Method: 受马特罗什卡表示学习启发，MCBM基于最大相关性和最小冗余度将概念组织成嵌套层次结构，使得单一模型无需重新训练即可在不同概念粒度级别进行推理。

Result: 理论上证明预期干预成本从线性降至对数级别O(log K)；实验上MCBM性能与独立训练模型相当，同时实现了动态高效的专家交互。

Conclusion: MCBM通过单一统一架构解决了概念瓶颈模型中测试时干预成本高的问题，实现了自适应概念利用与高效专家交互的统一。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

</details>


### [436] [A Tutorial on Diffusion Theory: From Differential Equations to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22586)
*Jiayi Fu,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提供了一篇关于扩散模型的统一教程，从微分方程视角系统梳理了扩散概率模型、基于分数的建模、随机微分方程和数值采样方法，阐明了它们之间的数学联系，并将DDPM、DDIM、流匹配和基于分数的SDE置于统一框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的数学基础（扩散概率模型、分数匹配、SDE、数值采样）通常被分开讲解，缺乏统一视角。作者希望从微分方程出发，提供一个自洽的、统一的教程，弥合扩散过程分析基础与现代生成算法之间的鸿沟。

Method: 从条件高斯加噪过程出发，推导ODE和SDE表示；建立边际前向动力学；推导反向时间SDE和概率流ODE；通过分数匹配导出去噪目标函数（噪声预测参数化）；将DDPM、DDIM、流匹配、基于分数的SDE纳入共同框架；扩展到连续嵌入空间的扩散语言模型和离散掩码token扩散。

Result: 揭示了反向采样中的核心未知量是边际分数，证明了分数匹配在噪声预测参数化下等价于标准的去噪目标；构建了多种主流扩散方法的统一框架；展示了该方法在语言模型等离散数据上的扩展路径。

Conclusion: 本教程作为扩散过程分析基础与现代生成算法之间的桥梁，为研究者提供了一个从微分方程视角统一理解扩散模型的系统性参考。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as a dominant framework for generative modeling, but their mathematical foundations are often presented separately through diffusion probabilistic models, score-based modeling, stochastic differential equations, and numerical sampling methods. We write this tutorial to provide a unified and self-contained account of these viewpoints from the perspective of differential equations. Starting from a conditional Gaussian noising process, we derive ordinary differential equation (ODE) and stochastic differential equation (SDE) representations, pass to the corresponding marginal forward dynamics, and then obtain the reverse-time SDE and probability-flow ODE that make generation possible. We show that the central unknown quantity in reverse sampling is the marginal score, explain how score matching becomes the standard denoising objective under a noise-prediction parameterization, and discuss practical reverse-time sampling and guidance. We further place DDPM, DDIM, flow matching, and score-based SDEs in a common framework, and conclude with diffusion language models in continuous embedding space together with a brief discussion of discrete masked-token diffusion. The tutorial is intended as a bridge between the analytical foundations of diffusion processes and the modern generative algorithms built upon them.

</details>


### [437] [Building a privacy-preserving Federated Recommender system for mobile devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22924)
*Aasheesh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段联邦推荐系统流水线，将非敏感的用户偏好数据与从不离机的敏感移动上下文数据分离，第一阶段在云端进行协同过滤生成候选列表，第二阶段在设备端利用敏感信号进行重排序，仅交换模型更新/梯度，并在多个数据集上验证后以Kotlin Multiplatform库形式提供生产级实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式服务个性化内容需要收集用户敏感数据到中央服务器，这与现代隐私期望和地域法规（如GDPR）日益冲突，亟需一种在保护隐私的同时仍能提供高质量移动端推荐的方法。

Method: 两阶段联邦推荐系统：第一阶段在云端对非敏感的应用上下文数据运行协同过滤模型，生成候选项目短列表；第二阶段在设备端利用敏感移动信号（如传感器数据）对这些候选进行重排序，仅模型更新或梯度离开设备，实现数据不离开设备的隐私保护。

Result: 在MovieLens、UCI Human Activity Recognition和一个专有试点数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并交付了可部署于Android和iOS的生产级Kotlin Multiplatform库实现。

Conclusion: 通过原则性地分离非敏感与敏感数据，结合云端协同过滤与设备端重排序的两阶段联邦架构，能够在满足隐私合规要求的同时实现移动端个性化推荐，且具备跨平台生产部署可行性。

Abstract: Serving personalized content on mobile devices has traditionally required pooling sensitive user data on centralized servers, a practice increasingly at odds with modern privacy expectations and geographical regulations. We present a two-stage federated recommendation system pipeline for mobile devices, built around a principled separation between non-sensitive user preference data and sensitive mobile context data that never leaves the device. The first stage runs a collaborative filtering model on non-sensitive app-context data in the cloud to generate a shortlist of relevant items. The second stage re-ranks these candidates on-device using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients ever leaving the device. We validate the approach on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and a proprietary pilot dataset, and deliver a production-ready implementation as a Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on Android and iOS.

</details>


### [438] [Tiny Brains, Giant Impact: Uncovering the Keystone Neurons of LLM with Just a Few Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24846)
*Xiangtian Ji,Yuxin Chen,Zhengzhou Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer模型中存在一类“基石神经元”（keystone neurons），这些神经元在推理时跨任务高度一致激活，且数量极其稀疏。移除这些神经元会导致模型行为崩溃。这些神经元主要在预训练阶段形成，其参数值对模型能力至关重要。基于此，提出仅更新基石神经元的微调方法，在参数量大幅减少的情况下，性能可与全参数微调相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽展现出强大的综合能力，但其内部工作机制尚不明确，缺乏对模型行为背后关键神经元的理解。

Method: 1) 跨任务激活强度分析，识别出一组在多种能力维度下高度一致激活的稀疏神经元子集；2) 通过移除实验验证这些神经元的关键作用；3) 提出仅更新基石神经元的监督微调方法。

Result: 基石神经元是模型稳定且固有的神经元子集，主要在预训练阶段形成；其参数值在训练过程中被紧密校准，对模型能力至关重要；仅更新基石神经元的微调方法在参数量大幅减少的情况下，任务增益可媲美甚至超越全参数微调，且更好保留其他能力维度性能。

Conclusion: 识别出基石神经元这一关键结构，并证明仅微调这些神经元即可高效实现任务性能提升，为理解LLM内部机制和参数高效微调提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) display strong comprehensive abilities, yet the internal mechanisms that support these behaviors remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we show that across a wide range of open-weight Transformers, a subset of neurons remains consistently highly activated during inference across tasks of multiple capability dimensions. By probing along the cross-task activation strength, an extremely sparse subset is isolated, whose removal causes a collapse in model behavior, which we term keystone neurons. Our analysis reveals that keystone neurons are a stable and intrinsic neuron subset of the model that is largely established during pretraining. The parameters associated with these neurons are tightly calibrated during the training process, and their precise values are critical for the capabilities of the model. Building on these insights, we propose a supervised fine-tuning approach that updates only keystone neurons, achieving task gains comparable to or even better than full-parameter fine-tuning while better preserving performance in other capability dimensions, despite modifying a much smaller number of parameters.

</details>


### [439] [Theoretical Analysis of Sparse Optimization with Reparameterization, Weight Decay, and Adaptive Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25134)
*Huangyu Xu,Jingqin Yang,Qianqian Xu,Jiaye Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReWA方法，通过重参数化、权重衰减和自适应学习率解决$\ell_p$正则化在$0<p<1$时梯度无界导致的优化不稳定问题，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上相比$\ell_1$正则化显著提升稀疏性且保持测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\ell_p$正则化（$0<p<1$）在稀疏优化中表现优异，但其梯度无界导致优化不稳定，亟需一种既能保持稀疏性又能稳定优化的方法。

Method: 提出ReWA方法，结合重参数化（Reparameterization）、权重衰减（Weight decay）和自适应学习率（Adaptive learning rate），构建与$\ell_p$正则化相关但优化景观不同的目标函数，从而缓解梯度爆炸问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet数据集上使用ResNet进行实验，ReWA相比$\ell_1$正则化方法实现了显著的稀疏性提升，同时测试精度保持不变。

Conclusion: ReWA是一种有效的稀疏优化替代方案，克服了$\ell_p$正则化的优化不稳定性，在稀疏性和精度之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Sparse optimization is a fundamental challenge in various practical applications. A popular approach to sparse optimization is $\ell_p$ regularization. However, it may encounter optimization instability due to the unbounded gradients when $0<p<1$. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach to sparse optimization termed ReWA, based on Reparameterization, Weight decay, and Adaptive learning rate. ReWA is closely connected to $\ell_p$-regularization, yet it unveils a distinct optimization landscape that helps mitigate instability issues. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet with ResNets demonstrate that ReWA leads to significant sparsity improvements over the $\ell_1$-regularization approach while preserving test accuracy.

</details>


### [440] [Autoregression-Free Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25413)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Caiyan Qin,Haoyu Bian,Libin Cai,Yi Lu,Chaoning Zhang,Wei Dong,Yuanfang Guo,Yang Yang,Hen Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自回归自由神经算子(AFNO)，将PDE时间演化映射到潜在空间，利用流匹配学习连续时间向量场，避免自回归滚动，提升长期预测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经算子的时变PDE求解方法依赖自回归滚动预测，导致长时程误差累积严重，且缺乏连续时间建模能力。

Method: AFNO将PDE时间演化映射到潜在空间，在该空间中用流匹配(flow matching)学习连续时间向量场，通过显式条件化物理参数捕捉不同参数配置下的动力学，避免自回归滚动。

Result: 在6种PDE上的理论分析和大量实验表明，AFNO相比基线方法提升了长期预测稳定性，并持续减少了滚动误差。

Conclusion: AFNO通过潜在空间连续时间建模和流匹配，有效解决了神经算子中长期预测的误差累积问题，是一种更稳定的PDE求解范式。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings from function-dependent inputs to solutions, providing an effective framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). For time-dependent PDEs, existing methods typically perform long-horizon prediction through autoregressive rollout directly in high-dimensional physical field spaces, where each predicted state is recursively fed back as the input for the next step. Although effective for short-term prediction, this autoregressive rollout and the lack of continuous-time modeling lead to progressive error accumulation over long-horizon rollouts. In this work, we propose Autoregression-Free Neural Operators (AFNO), which map the time evolution of PDEs into a latent space and model continuous-time vector fields within it. AFNO uses flow matching to learn the latent vector field, thereby enabling continuous evolution over extended horizons, avoiding autoregressive rollout and capturing dynamics under varying parameter configurations through explicit conditioning on physical parameters. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments on six PDEs demonstrate that AFNO improves long-horizon prediction stability and consistently reduces rollout errors compared with the baselines.

</details>


### [441] [GoQuant: Geometric Orthogonal Residual Projection for Multiplier-Free Power-of-Two Transformer Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26092)
*Maoyang Xiang,Bo Wang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GoQuant是一种面向超低位宽(≤4-bit)量化的算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过几何正交残差投影量化方法，将对数幂(PoT)量化的非均匀指数格点替换为基于移位-加操作的高分辨率残差格点，在3-bit(W3/A16)条件下对LLaMA-2-7B实现6.10的困惑度，并将28nm工艺下关键路径延迟降至0.35ns，无需密集MAC阵列即可缓解边缘部署的时序瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上部署大语言模型(LLMs)和视觉Transformer(ViTs)面临严重的内存限制和密集乘累加(MAC)阵列带来的时序瓶颈。超低位宽量化中，对数幂(PoT)量化虽可用移位操作替代MAC，但其非均匀指数格点存在低角分辨率固有问题(尤其在sub-4-bit阈值下)，导致高维特征流形显著退化。

Method: 将量化问题形式化为双基几何投影，自适应合成更高分辨率的残差格点，仅使用移位-加操作实现；分析方法替代梯度优化，将LLaMA-2-7B的全模型校准时间缩短至约15分钟。

Result: 在3-bit(W3/A16)约束下，LLaMA-2-7B困惑度达6.10，优于AWQ等MAC密集型基线；无需非对称缩放即可在4-bit场景保持竞争力。28nm标准单元RTL综合表明，并行移位-加数据通路将组合逻辑深度平坦化，关键路径延迟降至0.35ns。

Conclusion: GoQuant通过算法-硬件协同设计有效解决了超低位宽量化中的角分辨率问题，在模型精度和硬件效率间取得了优越平衡，为边缘设备部署LLM/ViT提供了实用的量化方案。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) on edge devices is significantly constrained by memory limitations and the critical timing bottlenecks introduced by dense Multiply-Accumulate (MAC) arrays. In the ultra-low bit regime, logarithmic Power-of-Two (PoT) quantization provides a hardware-efficient alternative by replacing MAC operations with bit-shifts. However, the non-uniform exponential lattice is inherently limited by a \textbf{Low Angular Resolution Regime}, a structural flaw that becomes particularly pronounced at sub-4-bit thresholds, leading to a notable degradation of high-dimensional feature manifolds. To address this geometric limitation, we propose Geometric Orthogonal Residual Projection Quantization (GoQuant), an algorithm-hardware co-design framework. By formulating quantization as a dual-basis geometric projection, GoQuant adaptively synthesizes a higher-resolution residual lattice using strictly shift-and-add operations. Furthermore, its analytical solver offers a practical alternative to computationally intensive gradient-based optimization, reducing the full-model calibration time for LLaMA-2-7B to approximately 15 minutes. Extensive evaluations demonstrate GoQuant's applicability across modalities and its hardware efficiency. Under the 3-bit (W3/A16) constraint, it achieves a perplexity of 6.10 on LLaMA-2-7B, comparing favorably to conventional MAC-intensive baselines like AWQ without relying on asymmetric scaling, while maintaining competitive accuracy in 4-bit scenarios. At the silicon level, standard-cell RTL synthesis at a 28nm node indicates that GoQuant effectively mitigates the timing bottlenecks associated with dense multiplier trees. By flattening the combinational logic depth, our parallel shift-and-add datapath reduces the critical path delay to 0.35 ns.

</details>


### [442] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAD框架，通过统一分类（Outlier Exposure）与重构（Masked Autoencoder）两种范式，利用分类模块生成概率软掩码指导重构模块，同时重构模块缓解分类模块的泛化问题，从而有效检测微妙和复杂的时间序列异常，在多个高质量基准数据集上显著超越现有方法，且轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测方法中，基于Outlier Exposure（OE）的分类方法受限于泛化能力差，而基于Masked Autoencoder（MAE）的重构方法存在掩码对齐问题。两类方法各有优劣，需统一两者互补优势、弥补各自不足。

Method: 提出CoAD框架，核心为协作设计：分类模块（OE范式）生成概率指导的软掩码，输入重构模块（MAE范式）进行掩码重建；重构模块反向缓解分类模块的泛化问题。分类模块还针对分类粒度不当和频率信息忽略问题进行了专门设计。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集上，采用严格评估协议，CoAD显著优于现有深度学习方法和传统数据挖掘方法；同时模型轻量，速度远超现有最优方法，适用于大规模实时应用。

Conclusion: CoAD通过统一分类与重构两种范式，有效解决了OE泛化差和MAE掩码对齐问题，在时间序列异常检测中展现了深度学习方法的潜力，兼具高性能与实用性。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [443] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究脊髓损伤病理步态分析中，哪种预训练目标能使时间序列表示在分类和回归任务间均衡迁移，发现动态中心混合目标（局部重建+时间连续性+上下文条件）效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习受限于小样本、异质队列和协议漂移，而下游任务同时涉及分类（病理诊断）和回归（时序预测）。基础模型预训练虽具吸引力，但关键问题在于预训练目标应施加何种归纳偏置，才能使表示跨越任务类型和主体进行迁移。

Method: 提出PathoFM，一个以编码器为中心的Transformer，对多变量步态窗口进行预训练，采用三个互补目标：(1) 局部完成——重建连续掩码跨度以强化局部结构；(2) 时间连续性——从观测前缀预测掩码中期延续以强化平滑性和因果一致性；(3) 无监督上下文动态——通过注意力机制基于主体样本窗口的支持-查询重构。

Result: 动态中心混合目标产生最均衡的迁移表现：分组目标有利于判别边界但降低连续目标所需的幅度保真度；纯重建目标保留波形结构但在分类上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 通过对比实验发现，动态中心目标混合（局部重建+时间连续性+上下文条件）能产生最均衡的迁移表现，在分类和回归任务间取得良好平衡，且跨主体泛化能力稳健。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [444] [Function-Valued Causal Influence in Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26408)
*Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有因果发现方法使用标量边缘分数总结非线性时间序列模型中的因果关系，这会导致信息瓶颈；提出使用函数值因果影响（基于Individual Conditional Expectation框架）来捕捉状态依赖、非线性、阈值和符号变化等复杂因果效应，并通过合成实验和民主发展案例研究验证了该方法能揭示标量方法遗漏的因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性机器学习模型在时序因果发现中被广泛使用，但现有做法几乎总是用标量边缘分数来总结因果关系，这掩盖了非线性自回归模型实际学习到的状态依赖函数——其效应随机制、幅度和上下文而变化。标量因果分数构成了严重的信息瓶颈，混淆了状态间变异与状态内残差噪声。

Method: 以Neural Additive Vector Autoregression为代表性架构，基于Individual Conditional Expectation（ICE）提出了直接从训练模型中估计因果响应函数的实用框架。

Result: 通过受控合成实验证明，具有不可区分标量分数的边可以表现出截然不同的函数行为，包括单调、阈值、饱和及符号变化效应。在民主发展的应用案例研究中，函数值分析揭示了标量方法系统性忽略的特定机制和不对称因果结构。

Conclusion: 标量因果分数在非线性因果发现中存在根本性信息损失，函数值因果影响分析能够揭示更丰富的因果结构信息，是现有标量方法的重要补充。

Abstract: Causal discovery in time series is increasingly performed using nonlinear machine-learning models, yet the resulting causal relationships are almost always summarized by scalar edge scores. We argue that this practice obscures the true object learned by nonlinear autoregressive models: a state-dependent function whose effect varies across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. We formalize function-valued causal influence for additive, contribution-decomposable architectures and show that scalar causal scores constitute a severe information bottleneck, conflating between-state variation with within-state residual noise. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a representative architecture, we introduce a practical framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation for estimating causal response functions directly from trained models. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors, including monotonic, thresholded, saturating, and sign-changing effects. An applied case study on democratic development further shows that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

</details>


### [445] [Two Speeds of Learning: A Representation-Readout Decomposition of Grokking and Double Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27078)
*Chi-Ning Chou,Oscar Uzdelewicz,Neng-Chun Chiu,Yao-Yuan Yang,SueYeon Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征-读出分解框架，通过分析编码器表征学习与最终分类器读出校准两个竞争过程，解释了grokking和epoch-wise double descent等看似异常的学习动态现象，并揭示了这些现象背后的统一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对grokking和epoch-wise double descent等学习动态现象的解释往往是任务特定的，缺乏一个跨任务、跨架构的任务无关分析框架来诊断和理解这些现象。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用表征几何、神经正切核（NTK）和线性探针等工具，将训练动态分解为编码器中的表征学习和最终分类器中的读出校准两个竞争过程，通过分析两者相对速度的波动来解释异常泛化动态。

Result: 1）发现grokking过程中读出在onset前偏向训练集，表征学习是渐进的而非缺失，与lazy-to-rich假说相悖；2）框架能提供诊断信号区分虚假泛化与真正泛化；3）在MNIST grokking和epoch-wise double descent案例中，表观延迟或非单调泛化源于非标准训练导致的表征退化和读出错位。

Conclusion: 表征-读出分解是一个自顶向下的分析框架，能够统一理解学习动态并揭示底层算法，对可解释性研究具有重要价值。

Abstract: Training loss and accuracy are the standard signals used to monitor generalization during deep neural network training.
